def _tensor_basic_stats(tf: torch.Tensor) -> dict[str, float]:
    finite = tf[torch.isfinite(tf)]
    if finite.numel() == 0:
        return {"min": 0.0, "max": 0.0, "mean": 0.0, "std": 0.0, "norm": 0.0}
    return {
        "min": round(_safe_float(float(finite.min().item())), 5),
        "max": round(_safe_float(float(finite.max().item())), 5),
        "mean": round(_safe_float(float(finite.mean().item())), 5),
        "std": round(_safe_float(float(finite.std(unbiased=False).item())), 5) if finite.numel() > 1 else 0.0,
        "norm": round(_safe_float(float(finite.norm().item())), 5),
    }


def _valid_seq_slice(
    tensor: torch.Tensor,
    mask: Optional[torch.Tensor],
    sample_idx: int,
    max_steps: int,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, int, int]:
    """抽取单样本序列 (L_eff, D)，感知 Mask 截断并按需等距下采样。"""
    b_idx = max(0, min(tensor.shape[0] - 1, sample_idx))
    seq = tensor[b_idx].float()
    total_len = int(seq.shape[0])
    valid_len = total_len
    if mask is not None and mask.ndim >= 2:
        m = mask[b_idx].bool()
        cnt = int(m.sum().item())
        if 0 < cnt <= total_len:
            valid_len = cnt
            seq = seq[:valid_len]

    if seq.shape[0] > max_steps and max_steps >= 2:
        idx = torch.linspace(0, seq.shape[0] - 1, steps=max_steps, device=seq.device).long()
        seq = seq.index_select(0, idx)
    return seq, valid_len, total_len


class _GateDynamicsModule(nn.Module):
    """LSTM 多门控时序演化与饱和度分析探针。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        sample_idx: int = 0,
        max_steps: int = 64,
        saturation_thresh: float = 0.1,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.sample_idx = sample_idx
        self.max_steps = max_steps
        self.saturation_thresh = saturation_thresh
        self._last_gates: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        self._last_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None

    def forward(self, inputs: dict[str, Signal]) -> dict[str, Signal]:
        captured: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        found_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        for key in ("i", "f", "o", "c"):
            sig = inputs.get(key)
            if sig is not None and sig.data.device.type != "meta":
                captured[key] = sig.data.detach()
                if found_mask is None and sig.mask is not None:
                    found_mask = sig.mask.detach()
        if captured:
            self._last_gates = captured
            self._last_mask = found_mask
        return {}

    def capture_snapshot(self) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self._last_gates:
            return None

        with torch.no_grad():
            mask = self._last_mask.cpu() if self._last_mask is not None else None
            first_key = next(iter(self._last_gates))
            first_raw = self._last_gates[first_key].cpu()
            tf_first = first_raw.float()
            stats = _tensor_basic_stats(tf_first)

            gates_payload: dict[str, Any] = {}
            valid_len_out = int(tf_first.shape[1]) if tf_first.ndim >= 2 else 1
            total_len_out = valid_len_out
            steps_out = 0

            strip_rows: list[list[int]] = []
            strip_labels: list[str] = []

            for key in ("i", "f", "o", "c"):
                if key not in self._last_gates:
                    continue
                t = self._last_gates[key].cpu()
                seq, valid_len, total_len = _valid_seq_slice(t, mask, self.sample_idx, self.max_steps)
                valid_len_out = valid_len
                total_len_out = total_len
                steps_out = int(seq.shape[0])

                mean_c = seq.mean(dim=-1)
                std_c = seq.std(dim=-1, unbiased=False) if seq.shape[-1] > 1 else torch.zeros_like(mean_c)
                sat_low = float((seq < self.saturation_thresh).float().mean().item()) if key != "c" else 0.0
                sat_high = float((seq > (1.0 - self.saturation_thresh)).float().mean().item()) if key != "c" else 0.0

                # 压缩出最多 4 条代表通道的时序条带 (steps,) -> [0..255]
                sub_h = seq[:, : min(4, seq.shape[1])].T  # (K, L)
                q = _quantize_2d(sub_h, symmetric=(key == "c"))
                cols = q["cols"]
                for r in range(q["rows"]):
                    strip_rows.append(q["data"][r * cols : (r + 1) * cols])
                    strip_labels.append(key if r == 0 else "")

                gates_payload[key] = {
                    "mean": [round(_safe_float(float(v)), 4) for v in mean_c.tolist()],
                    "std": [round(_safe_float(float(v)), 4) for v in std_c.tolist()],
                    "sat_low": round(sat_low, 4),
                    "sat_high": round(sat_high, 4),
                    "abs_mean": round(_safe_float(float(seq.abs().mean().item())), 4),
                    "min": round(_safe_float(float(seq.min().item())), 4),
                    "max": round(_safe_float(float(seq.max().item())), 4),
                }

            return {
                "probe_kind": "gate_dynamics",
                "view": "gate_dynamics",
                "shape": list(first_raw.shape),
                "dtype": str(first_raw.dtype).replace("torch.", ""),
                "layout": "BLD",
                "stats": stats,
                "scalar": stats["mean"],
                "gate_dynamics": {
                    "gates": gates_payload,
                    "steps": steps_out,
                    "valid_len": valid_len_out,
                    "total_len": total_len_out,
                    "strip_rows": strip_rows,
                    "strip_labels": strip_labels,
                },
            }


@register_component("gate_dynamics")
class GateDynamics(BaseComponent):
    """LSTM 门控时序演化分析器：同屏对比 i/f/o/c 门控时间曲线、神经元条带热图与饱和度。"""

    category = "output"
    display_name = "GateDynamics"
    title = "门控动态分析"
    description = "LSTM 多门控时序曲线、条带热图与饱和度分析（可同时接 gate_i/f/o/cell_state）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec("sample_idx", "int", 0, min=0, max=256, step=1, description="观察的 Batch 样本索引"),
            ParamSpec("max_steps", "int", 64, min=8, max=256, step=8, description="最大显示时间步数"),
            ParamSpec("saturation_thresh", "float", 0.1, min=0.01, max=0.45, step=0.02, description="饱和判断阈值（<t 或 >1-t）"),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[
                PortSpec(name="i", title="输入门序列", description="接 LSTM 的 tap:gate_i，BLD", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD", optional=True),
                PortSpec(name="f", title="遗忘门序列", description="接 LSTM 的 tap:gate_f，BLD", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD", optional=True),
                PortSpec(name="o", title="输出门序列", description="接 LSTM 的 tap:gate_o，BLD", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD", optional=True),
                PortSpec(name="c", title="细胞状态序列", description="接 LSTM 的 tap:cell_state，BLD", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD", optional=True),
            ],
            outputs=[],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        return _GateDynamicsModule(
            sample_idx=int(params.get("sample_idx", 0)),
            max_steps=int(params.get("max_steps", 64)),
            saturation_thresh=float(params.get("saturation_thresh", 0.1)),
        )


class _HiddenTrajectoryModule(nn.Module):
    """隐状态 2D 相空间轨迹分析探针（支持 BLD 时间轨迹与 BD Batch 分布）。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        method: str = "pca",
        sample_idx: int = 0,
        max_points: int = 64,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.method = method
        self.sample_idx = sample_idx
        self.max_points = max_points
        self._last_tensor: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self._last_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self._last_layout: str = "ANY"

    def forward(self, inputs: dict[str, Signal]) -> dict[str, Signal]:
        sig = inputs["x"]
        if sig.data.device.type != "meta":
            self._last_tensor = sig.data.detach()
            self._last_mask = sig.mask.detach() if sig.mask is not None else None
            self._last_layout = sig.layout
        return {}

    def capture_snapshot(self) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        if self._last_tensor is None:
            return None

        with torch.no_grad():
            raw = self._last_tensor.cpu()
            tf = raw.float()
            stats = _tensor_basic_stats(tf)
            mask = self._last_mask.cpu() if self._last_mask is not None else None
            is_seq = tf.ndim == 3

            if is_seq:
                mat, valid_len, total_len = _valid_seq_slice(tf, mask, self.sample_idx, self.max_points)
            elif tf.ndim == 2:
                n = min(tf.shape[0], self.max_points)
                mat = tf[:n]
                valid_len = int(n)
                total_len = int(tf.shape[0])
            else:
                mat = tf.reshape(tf.shape[0], -1)[: self.max_points] if tf.ndim >= 1 else tf.reshape(1, 1)
                valid_len = int(mat.shape[0])
                total_len = valid_len

            n_pts, dim = mat.shape
            explained_var = 0.0
            if dim == 0 or n_pts == 0:
                coords = torch.zeros((1, 2), dtype=torch.float32)
            elif dim == 1:
                t_axis = torch.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n_pts).unsqueeze(-1)
                coords = torch.cat([mat, t_axis], dim=-1)
            elif self.method == "pca" and n_pts >= 2 and dim >= 2:
                centered = mat - mat.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)
                try:
                    _u, s, vh = torch.linalg.svd(centered, full_matrices=False)
                    if vh.shape[0] >= 2:
                        coords = centered @ vh[:2].T
                        total_e = float((s ** 2).sum().item())
                        if total_e > 1e-12:
                            explained_var = float(((s[:2] ** 2).sum() / total_e).item())
                    else:
                        coords = torch.cat([centered[:, :1], torch.zeros((n_pts, 1))], dim=-1)
                except Exception:
                    coords = mat[:, :2]
            else:
                coords = mat[:, :2]

            xs = coords[:, 0]
            ys = coords[:, 1]
            xmin, xmax = float(xs.min().item()), float(xs.max().item())
            ymin, ymax = float(ys.min().item()), float(ys.max().item())

            if n_pts >= 2:
                diffs = coords[1:] - coords[:-1]
                step_dists = diffs.norm(dim=-1)
                path_len = float(step_dists.sum().item())
                net_disp = float((coords[-1] - coords[0]).norm().item())
            else:
                path_len = 0.0
                net_disp = 0.0

            points = [
                [round(_safe_float(float(px)), 4), round(_safe_float(float(py)), 4)]
                for px, py in coords.tolist()
            ]

            return {
                "probe_kind": "hidden_trajectory",
                "view": "trajectory",
                "shape": list(raw.shape),
                "dtype": str(raw.dtype).replace("torch.", ""),
                "layout": self._last_layout,
                "stats": stats,
                "scalar": round(_safe_float(path_len), 5),
                "trajectory": {
                    "points": points,
                    "method": self.method,
                    "is_sequence": is_seq,
                    "valid_len": valid_len,
                    "total_len": total_len,
                    "explained_var": round(explained_var, 4),
                    "path_len": round(_safe_float(path_len), 4),
                    "net_disp": round(_safe_float(net_disp), 4),
                    "xmin": round(_safe_float(xmin), 4),
                    "xmax": round(_safe_float(xmax), 4),
                    "ymin": round(_safe_float(ymin), 4),
                    "ymax": round(_safe_float(ymax), 4),
                },
            }


@register_component("hidden_trajectory")
class HiddenTrajectory(BaseComponent):
    """隐状态 2D 相空间轨迹：将 BLD 序列（或 BD 隐向量）投影至 2D 绘制状态迁移轨迹。"""

    category = "output"
    display_name = "HiddenTrajectory"
    title = "隐状态轨迹"
    description = "隐状态 2D 相空间轨迹（PCA 投影展示状态从 t=0 到终点的演化路径）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec("method", "select", "pca", options=["pca", "first2"], description="2D 投影方式"),
            ParamSpec("sample_idx", "int", 0, min=0, max=256, step=1, description="3D 序列的样本索引"),
            ParamSpec("max_points", "int", 64, min=8, max=256, step=8, description="最大轨迹点数"),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[
                PortSpec(name="x", title="隐状态序列", description="BLD/BD 隐状态，PCA 投影到 2D 相空间", kind="tensor", rank=[2, 3], layout="ANY"),
            ],
            outputs=[],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        return _HiddenTrajectoryModule(
            method=str(params.get("method", "pca")),
            sample_idx=int(params.get("sample_idx", 0)),
            max_points=int(params.get("max_points", 64)),
        )


class _StepEnergyModule(nn.Module):
    """时间步响应能量与步间状态跃迁分析探针（BLD）。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        sample_idx: int = 0,
        max_steps: int = 64,
        norm_mode: str = "l2",
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.sample_idx = sample_idx
        self.max_steps = max_steps
        self.norm_mode = norm_mode
        self._last_tensor: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self._last_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None

    def forward(self, inputs: dict[str, Signal]) -> dict[str, Signal]:
        sig = inputs["x"]
        if sig.data.device.type != "meta":
            self._last_tensor = sig.data.detach()
            self._last_mask = sig.mask.detach() if sig.mask is not None else None
        return {}

    def capture_snapshot(self) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        if self._last_tensor is None:
            return None

        with torch.no_grad():
            raw = self._last_tensor.cpu()
            tf = raw.float()
            stats = _tensor_basic_stats(tf)
            mask = self._last_mask.cpu() if self._last_mask is not None else None
            seq, valid_len, total_len = _valid_seq_slice(tf, mask, self.sample_idx, self.max_steps)

            if self.norm_mode == "l2_mean":
                energy = seq.pow(2).mean(dim=-1).sqrt()
            elif self.norm_mode == "linf":
                energy = seq.abs().max(dim=-1).values
            else:
                energy = seq.norm(dim=-1)

            if seq.shape[0] >= 2:
                delta_tail = (seq[1:] - seq[:-1]).norm(dim=-1)
                delta = torch.cat([torch.zeros(1, dtype=seq.dtype), delta_tail], dim=0)
            else:
                delta = torch.zeros_like(energy)

            peak_idx = int(energy.argmax().item()) if energy.numel() > 0 else 0
            peak_val = float(energy[peak_idx].item()) if energy.numel() > 0 else 0.0
            first_e = float(energy[0].item()) if energy.numel() > 0 else 0.0
            last_e = float(energy[-1].item()) if energy.numel() > 0 else 0.0
            decay_ratio = last_e / max(1e-6, first_e) if first_e > 1e-6 else 1.0

            return {
                "probe_kind": "step_energy",
                "view": "step_energy",
                "shape": list(raw.shape),
                "dtype": str(raw.dtype).replace("torch.", ""),
                "layout": "BLD",
                "stats": stats,
                "scalar": round(_safe_float(peak_val), 5),
                "step_energy": {
                    "energy": [round(_safe_float(float(v)), 4) for v in energy.tolist()],
                    "delta": [round(_safe_float(float(v)), 4) for v in delta.tolist()],
                    "peak_step": peak_idx,
                    "peak_val": round(_safe_float(peak_val), 4),
                    "decay_ratio": round(_safe_float(decay_ratio), 4),
                    "valid_len": valid_len,
                    "total_len": total_len,
                    "norm_mode": self.norm_mode,
                },
            }


@register_component("step_energy")
class StepEnergy(BaseComponent):
    """时间步响应与记忆衰减分析：展示各时间步 L2 能量与步间状态跃迁 ||h_t - h_{t-1}||。"""

    category = "output"
    display_name = "StepEnergy"
    title = "时间步能量分析"
    description = "时间步响应能量与步间跃迁分析（识别关键记忆写入步与衰减）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec("sample_idx", "int", 0, min=0, max=256, step=1, description="观察的样本索引"),
            ParamSpec("max_steps", "int", 64, min=8, max=256, step=8, description="最大显示时间步数"),
            ParamSpec(
                "norm_mode",
                "select",
                "l2",
                options=["l2", "l2_mean", "linf"],
                description="每步特征能量度量方式",
            ),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[
                PortSpec(name="x", title="序列输入", description="BLD 序列，逐时间步计算响应能量", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD"),
            ],
            outputs=[],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        return _StepEnergyModule(
            sample_idx=int(params.get("sample_idx", 0)),
            max_steps=int(params.get("max_steps", 64)),
            norm_mode=str(params.get("norm_mode", "l2")),
        )

================
netpulse/components/sources.py
================
"""内置数据源元件：极速合成数据与纯 PyTorch MNIST 加载器。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import math
import struct
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset

from netpulse.core.component import BaseComponent
from netpulse.core.registry import register_component
from netpulse.core.types import ComponentSignature, ParamSpec, PortSpec, ResolvedSpec, Signal


class _SourceModule(nn.Module):
    """数据源运行时占位模块：从 batch 中透传信号，支持 Meta 推导与 DataLoader 构造。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        sample_x_shape: tuple[int, ...],
        sample_y_shape: tuple[int, ...],
        x_layout: str,
        loader_factory,
        sample_has_mask: bool = False,
        y_dtype: torch.dtype = torch.int64,
        y_layout: str = "B",
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.sample_x_shape = sample_x_shape
        self.sample_y_shape = sample_y_shape
        self.x_layout = x_layout
        self._loader_factory = loader_factory
        self.sample_has_mask = sample_has_mask
        self.y_dtype = y_dtype
        self.y_layout = y_layout

    def forward(self, inputs: dict[str, Signal]) -> dict[str, Signal]:
        if "x" in inputs and "y" in inputs:
            return inputs
        # Meta 推导或空输入时返回虚拟符号张量
        dev = "meta"
        x = torch.empty(self.sample_x_shape, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)
        y = torch.empty(self.sample_y_shape, dtype=self.y_dtype, device=dev)
        mask = (
            torch.empty(self.sample_x_shape[:2], dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
            if self.sample_has_mask and len(self.sample_x_shape) >= 2
            else None
        )
        return {
            "x": Signal(data=x, mask=mask, meta={"layout": self.x_layout}),
            "y": Signal(data=y, meta={"layout": self.y_layout}),
        }

    def build_dataloaders(self, batch_size: int, seed: int) -> tuple[DataLoader, Optional[DataLoader]]:
        return self._loader_factory(batch_size=batch_size, seed=seed)


@register_component("spiral_data")
class SpiralData(BaseComponent):
    """内置秒开 2D 螺旋分类合成数据源。"""

    category = "source"
    display_name = "Spiral Data"
    title = "螺旋数据源"
    description = "2D 螺旋多分类合成数据源（秒开，适合快速实验）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec("num_classes", "int", 3, min=2, max=20, step=1, description="分类数"),
            ParamSpec("points_per_class", "int", 200, min=20, max=5000, step=20, description="每类样本数"),
            ParamSpec("noise", "float", 0.15, min=0.0, max=1.0, step=0.01, description="角度高斯噪声"),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[],
            outputs=[
                PortSpec(name="x", title="特征坐标", description="每行一个二维点 (x1, x2)，形状 BD", kind="tensor", rank=[2], layout="BD", dtype="float32"),
                PortSpec(name="y", title="类别标签", description="整型类别索引，形状 B", kind="tensor", rank=[1], layout="B", dtype="int64"),
            ],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        num_classes = int(params.get("num_classes", 3))
        points_per_class = int(params.get("points_per_class", 200))
        noise = float(params.get("noise", 0.15))
        batch_hint = int(params.get("meta_batch_size", 32))

        def _factory(batch_size: int, seed: int):
            g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
            xs, ys = [], []
            for c in range(num_classes):
                r = torch.linspace(0.1, 1.0, points_per_class)
                t = (
                    torch.linspace(c * 4.0, (c + 1) * 4.0, points_per_class)
                    + torch.randn(points_per_class, generator=g) * noise
                )
                x_c = torch.stack([r * torch.sin(t), r * torch.cos(t)], dim=1)
                y_c = torch.full((points_per_class,), c, dtype=torch.int64)
                xs.append(x_c)
                ys.append(y_c)
            all_x = torch.cat(xs, dim=0)
            all_y = torch.cat(ys, dim=0)
            perm = torch.randperm(all_x.shape[0], generator=g)
            all_x, all_y = all_x[perm], all_y[perm]

            split = int(all_x.shape[0] * 0.8)
            train_ds = TensorDataset(all_x[:split], all_y[:split])
            val_ds = TensorDataset(all_x[split:], all_y[split:])
            return (
                DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True),
                DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False),
            )

        return _SourceModule(
            sample_x_shape=(batch_hint, 2),
            sample_y_shape=(batch_hint,),
            x_layout="BD",
            loader_factory=_factory,
        )


_MNIST_FILES = {
    "train_images": "train-images-idx3-ubyte.gz",
    "train_labels": "train-labels-idx1-ubyte.gz",
    "test_images": "t10k-images-idx3-ubyte.gz",
    "test_labels": "t10k-labels-idx1-ubyte.gz",
}

_MNIST_MIRRORS = [
    "https://ossci-datasets.s3.amazonaws.com/mnist/",
    "https://storage.googleapis.com/cvdf-datasets/mnist/",
]


def _download_file(filename: str, target_dir: Path) -> Path:
    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest = target_dir / filename
    if dest.exists() and dest.stat().st_size > 0:
        return dest

    last_err: Optional[Exception] = None
    for base in _MNIST_MIRRORS:
        url = base + filename
        try:
            tmp = dest.with_suffix(".tmp")
            urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, tmp)
            tmp.replace(dest)
            return dest
        except Exception as exc:
            last_err = exc
    raise RuntimeError(f"无法下载 MNIST 文件 {filename}: {last_err}")


def _read_idx_images(gz_path: Path) -> torch.Tensor:
    with gzip.open(gz_path, "rb") as f:
        raw = f.read()
    magic, num, rows, cols = struct.unpack(">IIII", raw[:16])
    if magic != 2051:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid MNIST image magic {magic} in {gz_path}")
    arr = torch.frombuffer(bytearray(raw[16:]), dtype=torch.uint8).clone()
    return arr.view(num, 1, rows, cols).to(torch.float32).div_(255.0)


def _read_idx_labels(gz_path: Path) -> torch.Tensor:
    with gzip.open(gz_path, "rb") as f:
        raw = f.read()
    magic, num = struct.unpack(">II", raw[:8])
    if magic != 2049:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid MNIST label magic {magic} in {gz_path}")
    arr = torch.frombuffer(bytearray(raw[8:]), dtype=torch.uint8).clone()
    return arr.view(num).to(torch.int64)


@register_component("mnist_data")
class MNISTData(BaseComponent):
    """内置纯 PyTorch 轻量 MNIST 数据源（零 torchvision 依赖，自动缓存）。"""

    category = "source"
    display_name = "MNIST Data"
    title = "MNIST 手写数字"
    description = "手写数字数据集（1×28×28 灰度图，10 分类）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec("subset_train", "int", 1024, min=32, max=60000, step=64, description="训练子集样本数（留空全量）"),
            ParamSpec("subset_val", "int", 256, min=32, max=10000, step=32, description="验证子集样本数（留空全量）"),
            ParamSpec("normalize", "bool", True, description="是否做均值方差标准化"),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[],
            outputs=[
                PortSpec(name="x", title="图像批", description="28×28 灰度图，形状 BCHW，已归一化", kind="tensor", rank=[4], layout="BCHW", dtype="float32"),
                PortSpec(name="y", title="数字标签", description="0~9 整型标签，形状 B", kind="tensor", rank=[1], layout="B", dtype="int64"),
            ],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        data_dir = Path(params.get("data_dir", ".cache/mnist"))
        subset_train = params.get("subset_train")
        subset_val = params.get("subset_val")
        normalize = bool(params.get("normalize", True))
        batch_hint = int(params.get("meta_batch_size", 64))

        def _factory(batch_size: int, seed: int):
            paths = {k: _download_file(v, data_dir) for k, v in _MNIST_FILES.items()}
            x_train = _read_idx_images(paths["train_images"])
            y_train = _read_idx_labels(paths["train_labels"])
            x_val = _read_idx_images(paths["test_images"])
            y_val = _read_idx_labels(paths["test_labels"])

            if normalize:
                x_train = (x_train - 0.1307) / 0.3081
                x_val = (x_val - 0.1307) / 0.3081

            g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
            if subset_train is not None:
                n = min(int(subset_train), x_train.shape[0])
                idx = torch.randperm(x_train.shape[0], generator=g)[:n]
                x_train, y_train = x_train[idx], y_train[idx]
            if subset_val is not None:
                n = min(int(subset_val), x_val.shape[0])
                idx = torch.randperm(x_val.shape[0], generator=g)[:n]
                x_val, y_val = x_val[idx], y_val[idx]

            return (
                DataLoader(TensorDataset(x_train, y_train), batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True),
                DataLoader(TensorDataset(x_val, y_val), batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False),
            )

        return _SourceModule(
            sample_x_shape=(batch_hint, 1, 28, 28),
            sample_y_shape=(batch_hint,),
            x_layout="BCHW",
            loader_factory=_factory,
        )


@register_component("seq_wave_data")
class SeqWaveData(BaseComponent):
    """内置秒开合成时序数据源：支持波形频率分类与长程记忆线索（含可选变长 Mask）。"""

    category = "source"
    display_name = "SeqWave Data"
    title = "序列波形数据源"
    description = "合成多维序列分类数据源（波形模式 / 记忆线索，支持变长 Mask）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec(
                "task",
                "select",
                "memory_cue",
                options=["memory_cue", "freq_pattern"],
                description="任务类型（memory_cue 突出长程记忆门控；freq_pattern 多频波形分类）",
            ),
            ParamSpec("seq_len", "int", 28, min=8, max=256, step=4, description="最大序列长度 L"),
            ParamSpec("feat_dim", "int", 4, min=1, max=64, step=1, description="每步输入特征维 D"),
            ParamSpec("num_classes", "int", 3, min=2, max=10, step=1, description="分类数"),
            ParamSpec("num_samples", "int", 600, min=64, max=5000, step=50, description="总样本数"),
            ParamSpec("variable_length", "bool", True, description="是否生成随机有效长度并附带 Mask"),
            ParamSpec("noise", "float", 0.12, min=0.0, max=1.0, step=0.02, description="观测高斯噪声"),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[],
            outputs=[
                PortSpec(name="x", title="序列输入", description="波形/记忆线索输入，形状 BLD，附带变长 mask", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD", dtype="float32"),
                PortSpec(name="y", title="类别标签", description="整型类别索引，形状 B", kind="tensor", rank=[1], layout="B", dtype="int64"),
            ],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        task = str(params.get("task", "memory_cue"))
        seq_len = int(params.get("seq_len", 28))
        feat_dim = int(params.get("feat_dim", 4))
        num_classes = int(params.get("num_classes", 3))
        num_samples = int(params.get("num_samples", 600))
        variable_length = bool(params.get("variable_length", True))
        noise = float(params.get("noise", 0.12))
        batch_hint = int(params.get("meta_batch_size", 32))

        def _factory(batch_size: int, seed: int):
            g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
            x = torch.randn((num_samples, seq_len, feat_dim), generator=g) * noise
            y = torch.randint(0, num_classes, (num_samples,), generator=g, dtype=torch.int64)
            mask = torch.ones((num_samples, seq_len), dtype=torch.bool)

            t = torch.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, seq_len)
            min_valid = max(6, seq_len // 2)

            for i in range(num_samples):
                cls_id = int(y[i].item())
                valid_len = (
                    int(torch.randint(min_valid, seq_len + 1, (1,), generator=g).item())
                    if variable_length
                    else seq_len
                )
                if task == "memory_cue":
                    # 开头 3~5 步注入类别原型脉冲，中间主要为噪声，结尾给出查询触发脉冲
                    cue_len = min(4, max(2, seq_len // 6))
                    angle = 2.0 * math.pi * cls_id / num_classes
                    x[i, :cue_len, 0] += 1.8 * math.cos(angle)
                    if feat_dim > 1:
                        x[i, :cue_len, 1] += 1.8 * math.sin(angle)
                    if feat_dim > 2:
                        # 第 2 维放门控提示脉冲：开头写提示=+1，结尾读提示=-1
                        x[i, :cue_len, 2] += 1.2
                        x[i, valid_len - 2 : valid_len, 2] -= 1.2
                else:
                    # 多频正余弦与斜坡包络波形
                    freq = 1.0 + cls_id * 1.15
                    phase = float(torch.rand(1, generator=g).item()) * 0.5
                    wave = torch.sin(freq * t + phase)
                    cos_w = torch.cos(freq * t + phase)
                    x[i, :, 0] += wave
                    if feat_dim > 1:
                        x[i, :, 1] += cos_w
                    if feat_dim > 2:
                        x[i, :, 2] += (cls_id / max(1, num_classes - 1) - 0.5) * torch.linspace(0.2, 1.0, seq_len)

                if valid_len < seq_len:
                    x[i, valid_len:] = 0.0
                    mask[i, valid_len:] = False

            split = int(num_samples * 0.8)
            if variable_length:
                train_ds = TensorDataset(x[:split], y[:split], mask[:split])
                val_ds = TensorDataset(x[split:], y[split:], mask[split:])
            else:
                train_ds = TensorDataset(x[:split], y[:split])
                val_ds = TensorDataset(x[split:], y[split:])
            return (
                DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True),
                DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False),
            )

        return _SourceModule(
            sample_x_shape=(batch_hint, seq_len, feat_dim),
            sample_y_shape=(batch_hint,),
            x_layout="BLD",
            loader_factory=_factory,
            sample_has_mask=variable_length,
        )


@register_component("seq_forecast_data")
class SeqForecastData(BaseComponent):
    """经典时间序列滑窗回归预测数据源（多谐波叠加 / 阻尼振荡 / 自回归趋势）。"""

    category = "source"
    display_name = "SeqForecast Data"
    title = "时序预测数据源"
    description = "时间序列滑窗预测数据源（x 为历史窗口 BLD，y 为未来 horizon 步真值 BD）"

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [
            ParamSpec(
                "pattern",
                "select",
                "sine_mix",
                options=["sine_mix", "damped_osc", "ar_trend"],
                description="信号生成模式（sine_mix 多谐波；damped_osc 阻尼振荡；ar_trend 自回归趋势）",
            ),
            ParamSpec("window_len", "int", 24, min=6, max=128, step=2, description="输入历史窗口长度 L"),
            ParamSpec("horizon", "int", 1, min=1, max=16, step=1, description="预测未来步数（y 维度）"),
            ParamSpec("feat_dim", "int", 3, min=1, max=16, step=1, description="每步通道数 D（第 0 维为目标序列）"),
            ParamSpec("num_samples", "int", 800, min=64, max=5000, step=50, description="样本总数"),
            ParamSpec("noise", "float", 0.05, min=0.0, max=0.5, step=0.01, description="观测高斯噪声"),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[],
            outputs=[
                PortSpec(name="x", title="历史窗口", description="过去若干步观测值，形状 BLD", kind="tensor", rank=[3], layout="BLD", dtype="float32"),
                PortSpec(name="y", title="未来真值", description="未来 horizon 步目标值，形状 BD", kind="tensor", rank=[2], layout="BD", dtype="float32"),
            ],
        )

    def build(self, params: dict[str, Any], in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec]) -> nn.Module:
        pattern = str(params.get("pattern", "sine_mix"))
        window_len = int(params.get("window_len", 24))
        horizon = int(params.get("horizon", 1))
        feat_dim = int(params.get("feat_dim", 3))
        num_samples = int(params.get("num_samples", 800))
        noise = float(params.get("noise", 0.05))
        batch_hint = int(params.get("meta_batch_size", 32))

        def _factory(batch_size: int, seed: int):
            g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
            total_steps = window_len + horizon
            x = torch.zeros((num_samples, window_len, feat_dim), dtype=torch.float32)
            y = torch.zeros((num_samples, horizon), dtype=torch.float32)

            t_full = torch.linspace(0.0, 4.0 * math.pi, total_steps)
            for i in range(num_samples):
                phase = float(torch.rand(1, generator=g).item()) * 2.0 * math.pi
                freq = 0.8 + 0.8 * float(torch.rand(1, generator=g).item())
                amp = 0.7 + 0.5 * float(torch.rand(1, generator=g).item())

                if pattern == "damped_osc":
                    decay = torch.exp(-0.18 * t_full)
                    base = amp * decay * torch.sin(freq * t_full + phase) + 0.3 * torch.cos(0.5 * t_full)
                elif pattern == "ar_trend":
                    trend = 0.08 * t_full - 0.4
                    base = trend + 0.65 * amp * torch.sin(freq * t_full + phase) + 0.25 * torch.sin(2.1 * freq * t_full)
                else:
                    # sine_mix: 主频 + 二次谐波 + 慢包络
                    base = (
                        amp * torch.sin(freq * t_full + phase)
                        + 0.4 * torch.cos(2.0 * freq * t_full + 0.5 * phase)
                        + 0.2 * torch.sin(0.35 * t_full)
                    )

                series = base + torch.randn(total_steps, generator=g) * noise
                x[i, :, 0] = series[:window_len]
                y[i, :] = base[window_len:]  # 预测干净未来值，稳定收敛

                if feat_dim > 1:
                    # 一阶差分特征（局部斜率）
                    diff = torch.zeros_like(series[:window_len])
                    diff[1:] = series[1:window_len] - series[: window_len - 1]
                    x[i, :, 1] = diff
                if feat_dim > 2:
                    # 归一化绝对位置编码 / 相位线索
                    x[i, :, 2] = torch.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, window_len)
                for d in range(3, feat_dim):
                    x[i, :, d] = torch.cos((d - 1) * freq * t_full[:window_len] + phase)

            # 随机划分训练/验证集
            perm = torch.randperm(num_samples, generator=g)
            x, y = x[perm], y[perm]
            split = int(num_samples * 0.8)
            train_ds = TensorDataset(x[:split], y[:split])
            val_ds = TensorDataset(x[split:], y[split:])
            return (
                DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True),
                DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False),
            )

        return _SourceModule(
            sample_x_shape=(batch_hint, window_len, feat_dim),
            sample_y_shape=(batch_hint, horizon),
            x_layout="BLD",
            loader_factory=_factory,
            sample_has_mask=False,
            y_dtype=torch.float32,
            y_layout="BD",
        )

================
netpulse/core/compiler.py
================
"""GraphCompiler：将校验后的 GraphIR 编译为标准 PyTorch nn.Module。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Literal, Optional

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

from netpulse.core.erc import AutoBridgePlan, ERCResult, run_erc
from netpulse.core.ir import EdgeIR, GraphIR, LossConfig, NodeIR
from netpulse.core.registry import ComponentRegistry, get_default_registry
from netpulse.core.types import GraphOutput, Signal


class _AutoBridgeModule(nn.Module):
    """由 ERC 自动插入的矩阵投影桥（Linear 或 1x1 Conv）。"""

    def __init__(self, plan: AutoBridgePlan) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        src = plan.src_spec
        dst = plan.dst_spec
        self.layout = src.layout
        if src.layout == "BCHW" and src.rank == 4:
            self.proj: nn.Module = nn.Conv2d(src.shape[1], dst.shape[1], kernel_size=1)
        else:
            self.proj = nn.Linear(src.shape[-1], dst.shape[-1])

    def forward(self, sig: Signal) -> Signal:
        return sig.clone_with(data=self.proj(sig.data))


class CompiledGraph(nn.Module):
    """编译产物：标准 PyTorch nn.Module，支持 Signal 数据流、Tap 总线与多 Loss 汇集。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        graph_ir: GraphIR,
        erc_result: ERCResult,
        nodes_mod: nn.ModuleDict,
        bridges_mod: nn.ModuleDict,
        categories: dict[str, str],
        tap_hook_targets: dict[str, dict[str, tuple[str, str]]],
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.graph_ir = graph_ir
        self.erc_result = erc_result
        self.nodes_mod = nodes_mod
        self.bridges_mod = bridges_mod
        self.categories = categories

        self._node_map: dict[str, NodeIR] = {n.id: n for n in graph_ir.nodes}
        self._incoming_edges: dict[str, list[EdgeIR]] = defaultdict(list)
        for edge in graph_ir.edges:
            self._incoming_edges[edge.dst_node].append(edge)

        self._used_taps_by_node: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        for edge in graph_ir.edges:
            if edge.edge_kind == "tap":
                self._used_taps_by_node[edge.src_node].add(edge.src_port)

        self._loss_configs: dict[str, LossConfig] = {
            lc.node_id: lc for lc in graph_ir.losses
        }

        # 持久化注册前向 Hook（用于非 forward_with_taps 模块的隐层抽头）
        self._hook_buffer: dict[str, dict[str, Signal]] = defaultdict(dict)
        self._hook_handles = []
        for nid, taps in tap_hook_targets.items():
            mod = self.nodes_mod[nid]
            for tap_name, (sub_path, layout) in taps.items():
                submod = mod.get_submodule(sub_path)

                def _make_hook(node_id: str, tname: str, tlayout: str):
                    def _hook(_m, _inp, out):
                        t = out.data if isinstance(out, Signal) else out
                        self._hook_buffer[node_id][tname] = Signal(
                            data=t, meta={"layout": tlayout}
                        )

                    return _hook

                self._hook_handles.append(
                    submod.register_forward_hook(_make_hook(nid, tap_name, layout))
                )

    def forward(
        self,
        batch: Optional[dict[str, dict[str, Signal]]] = None,
        mode: Literal["train", "eval", "infer"] = "train",
        compute_losses: Optional[bool] = None,
    ) -> GraphOutput:
        if compute_losses is None:
            compute_losses = mode == "train"

        batch = batch or {}
        outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Signal]] = {}
        tap_bus: dict[str, Signal] = {}
        loss_dict: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}

        for nid in self.erc_result.topo_order:
            cat = self.categories[nid]
            if cat == "loss" and not compute_losses:
                continue

            mod = self.nodes_mod[nid]

            if cat == "source":
                src_inputs = batch.get(nid, {})
                outputs[nid] = mod(src_inputs)
                continue

            # 组装当前节点输入
            node_inputs: dict[str, Signal] = {}
            missing_input = False
            for edge in self._incoming_edges[nid]:
                if edge.edge_kind == "tap":
                    key = f"{edge.src_node}.{edge.src_port}"
                    if key not in tap_bus:
                        missing_input = True
                        break
                    sig = tap_bus[key]
                else:
                    src_out = outputs.get(edge.src_node)
                    if src_out is None or edge.src_port not in src_out:
                        missing_input = True
                        break
                    sig = src_out[edge.src_port]

                if edge.id in self.bridges_mod:
                    sig = self.bridges_mod[edge.id](sig)

                node_inputs[edge.dst_port] = sig

            if cat == "output":
                if missing_input:
                    continue
                with torch.no_grad():
                    outputs[nid] = mod(node_inputs)
                continue

            used_taps = self._used_taps_by_node.get(nid)
            if used_taps and hasattr(mod, "forward_with_taps"):
                node_out, raw_taps = mod.forward_with_taps(node_inputs)
                for tname in used_taps:
                    if tname in raw_taps:
                        tap_bus[f"{nid}.{tname}"] = raw_taps[tname]
            else:
                if nid in self._hook_buffer:
                    self._hook_buffer[nid].clear()
                node_out = mod(node_inputs)
                if nid in self._hook_buffer:
                    for tname, tsig in self._hook_buffer[nid].items():
                        tap_bus[f"{nid}.{tname}"] = tsig

            outputs[nid] = node_out

            if cat == "loss":
                loss_sig = node_out["loss"]
                loss_dict[nid] = loss_sig.data

        total_loss: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        if compute_losses and loss_dict:
            for nid, raw_loss in loss_dict.items():
                lc = self._loss_configs.get(nid)
                if lc is not None and not lc.enabled:
                    continue
                weight = lc.weight if lc is not None else 1.0
                term = raw_loss * weight
                total_loss = term if total_loss is None else (total_loss + term)

        return GraphOutput(
            outputs=outputs,
            tap_bus=tap_bus,
            loss_dict=loss_dict,
            total_loss=total_loss,
        )


class GraphCompiler:
    """将 GraphIR 经 ERC 校验后编译为 CompiledGraph。"""

    @staticmethod
    def compile(
        graph_ir: GraphIR,
        registry: Optional[ComponentRegistry] = None,
    ) -> CompiledGraph:
        reg = registry or get_default_registry()
        erc = run_erc(graph_ir, reg)
        if not erc.passed:
            raise ValueError(f"ERC 校验未通过:\n{erc.format_report()}")

        nodes_mod = nn.ModuleDict()
        categories: dict[str, str] = {}
        node_map = {n.id: n for n in graph_ir.nodes}

        used_taps_by_node: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        for edge in graph_ir.edges:
            if edge.edge_kind == "tap":
                used_taps_by_node[edge.src_node].add(edge.src_port)

        tap_hook_targets: dict[str, dict[str, tuple[str, str]]] = {}

        for nid in erc.topo_order:
            node = node_map[nid]
            cls = reg.get(node.type)
            comp = cls()
            categories[nid] = cls.category
            mod = comp.build(node.params, erc.node_in_specs[nid])
            nodes_mod[nid] = mod

            used_taps = used_taps_by_node.get(nid, set())
            if used_taps and not hasattr(mod, "forward_with_taps"):
                tap_map = erc.signatures[nid].tap_map()
                tap_hook_targets[nid] = {
                    tname: (tap_map[tname].source, tap_map[tname].spec.layout)
                    for tname in used_taps
                }

        bridges_mod = nn.ModuleDict()
        for eid, plan in erc.auto_bridges.items():
            bridges_mod[eid] = _AutoBridgeModule(plan)

        return CompiledGraph(
            graph_ir=graph_ir,
            erc_result=erc,
            nodes_mod=nodes_mod,
            bridges_mod=bridges_mod,
            categories=categories,
            tap_hook_targets=tap_hook_targets,
        )

================
netpulse/core/component.py
================
"""元件基类定义。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any, ClassVar

import torch.nn as nn

from netpulse.core.types import CategoryKind, ComponentSignature, ParamSpec, ResolvedSpec


class BaseComponent(ABC):
    """所有可注册元件必须继承的基类。"""

    type_id: ClassVar[str]
    category: ClassVar[CategoryKind]
    display_name: ClassVar[str] = ""
    # 中文译名（如「二维卷积层」）与中文简介
    title: ClassVar[str] = ""
    description: ClassVar[str] = ""

    @classmethod
    def param_schema(cls) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        """声明元件可编辑参数的 Schema（供 WebUI /object_info 渲染表单）。"""
        return []

    @classmethod
    def default_params(cls) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """提取参数默认值字典（仅包含非 None 默认值）。"""
        defaults: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for p in cls.param_schema():
            if p.default is not None:
                defaults[p.name] = p.default
        return defaults

    @classmethod
    @abstractmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ComponentSignature:
        """仅依赖节点静态参数声明输入/输出/Tap端口。"""

    @abstractmethod
    def build(
        self,
        params: dict[str, Any],
        in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec],
    ) -> nn.Module:
        """根据解析后的上游维度构建 PyTorch nn.Module。"""

================
netpulse/core/erc.py
================
"""EDA 式电气规则检查（ERC）与 Meta Device 形状推导。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict, deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Optional

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

from netpulse.core.component import BaseComponent
from netpulse.core.ir import EdgeIR, GraphIR, NodeIR
from netpulse.core.registry import ComponentRegistry
from netpulse.core.types import (
    ComponentSignature,
    Diagnostic,
    PortSpec,
    ResolvedSpec,
    Signal,
)


@dataclass
class AutoBridgePlan:
    """记录需要由编译器自动插入的 MatrixBridge 信息。"""

    edge_id: str
    src_spec: ResolvedSpec
    dst_spec: ResolvedSpec


@dataclass
class ERCResult:
    """静态检查与 Meta 推导完整结果。"""

    passed: bool
    topo_order: list[str]
    diagnostics: list[Diagnostic] = field(default_factory=list)
    signatures: dict[str, ComponentSignature] = field(default_factory=dict)
    node_in_specs: dict[str, dict[str, ResolvedSpec]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    node_out_specs: dict[str, dict[str, ResolvedSpec]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    node_tap_specs: dict[str, dict[str, ResolvedSpec]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    auto_bridges: dict[str, AutoBridgePlan] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def errors(self) -> list[Diagnostic]:
        return [d for d in self.diagnostics if d.level == "error"]

    def format_report(self) -> str:
        lines = [f"ERC passed={self.passed}, nodes={len(self.topo_order)}"]
        for d in self.diagnostics:
            loc = f" node={d.node_id}" if d.node_id else ""
            loc += f" edge={d.edge_id}" if d.edge_id else ""
            lines.append(f"[{d.level.upper()}][{d.code}]{loc}: {d.message}")
        if self.auto_bridges:
            lines.append(f"AutoBridges ({len(self.auto_bridges)}):")
            for eid, plan in self.auto_bridges.items():
                lines.append(f"  - {eid}: {plan.src_spec.shape} -> {plan.dst_spec.shape}")
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _dtype_from_str(name: str) -> torch.dtype:
    mapping = {
        "float32": torch.float32,
        "float": torch.float32,
        "float16": torch.float16,
        "bfloat16": torch.bfloat16,
        "int64": torch.int64,
        "long": torch.int64,
        "bool": torch.bool,
    }
    return mapping.get(name, torch.float32)


def _feature_dim_index(layout: str, rank: int) -> Optional[int]:
    if layout == "BD" and rank == 2:
        return 1
    if layout == "BLD" and rank == 3:
        return 2
    if layout == "BCHW" and rank == 4:
        return 1
    if rank >= 2:
        return -1
    return None


def _can_autobridge(src: ResolvedSpec, dst_expected: ResolvedSpec) -> bool:
    if src.rank != dst_expected.rank:
        return False
    if src.layout != "ANY" and dst_expected.layout != "ANY" and src.layout != dst_expected.layout:
        return False
    feat_idx = _feature_dim_index(src.layout, src.rank)
    if feat_idx is None:
        return False
    norm_idx = feat_idx if feat_idx >= 0 else src.rank + feat_idx
    for i, (s_dim, d_dim) in enumerate(zip(src.shape, dst_expected.shape)):
        if i == norm_idx:
            continue
        if s_dim != d_dim:
            return False
    return src.shape[norm_idx] != dst_expected.shape[norm_idx]


def _check_port_compat(
    edge: EdgeIR,
    src_spec: ResolvedSpec,
    dst_port: PortSpec,
    diagnostics: list[Diagnostic],
) -> bool:
    if dst_port.rank is not None and src_spec.rank not in dst_port.rank:
        hint = ""
        if src_spec.rank == 4 and 2 in dst_port.rank:
            hint = "（提示：可插入 Flatten 或 GlobalAvgPool 转换 4D->2D）"
        diagnostics.append(
            Diagnostic(
                level="error",
                code="RANK_MISMATCH",
                edge_id=edge.id,
                node_id=edge.dst_node,
                message=(
                    f"端口 '{edge.dst_node}.{dst_port.name}' 期望 Rank {dst_port.rank}，"
                    f"但上游 '{edge.src_node}.{edge.src_port}' 输出为 {src_spec.shape} (Rank {src_spec.rank}){hint}"
                ),
            )
        )
        return False

    if dst_port.layout != "ANY" and src_spec.layout != "ANY" and src_spec.layout != dst_port.layout:
        diagnostics.append(
            Diagnostic(
                level="error",
                code="LAYOUT_MISMATCH",
                edge_id=edge.id,
                node_id=edge.dst_node,
                message=(
                    f"端口 '{edge.dst_node}.{dst_port.name}' 期望布局 '{dst_port.layout}'，"
                    f"但上游输出布局为 '{src_spec.layout}'"
                ),
            )
        )
        return False

    if dst_port.require_mask and not src_spec.has_mask:
        diagnostics.append(
            Diagnostic(
                level="error",
                code="MASK_REQUIRED",
                edge_id=edge.id,
                node_id=edge.dst_node,
                message=f"端口 '{edge.dst_node}.{dst_port.name}' 要求携带 Mask，但上游 Signal 无 Mask",
            )
        )
        return False

    return True


def _resolve_expected_dim(
    comp: BaseComponent,
    node: NodeIR,
    port_name: str,
    actual: ResolvedSpec,
    resolved_in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec],
) -> Optional[ResolvedSpec]:
    """检查元件是否对某端口有固定特征维约束（可触发 AutoBridge 或报错）。"""
    hook = getattr(comp, "expected_input_spec", None)
    if callable(hook):
        return hook(node.params, port_name, actual, resolved_in_specs)
    return None


def _run_meta_node(
    comp: BaseComponent,
    node: NodeIR,
    in_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec],
    used_taps: set[str],
    sig: ComponentSignature,
) -> tuple[dict[str, ResolvedSpec], dict[str, ResolvedSpec]]:
    """在 PyTorch meta device 上零显存执行单节点前向，提取输出与 Tap 形状。"""
    with torch.device("meta"):
        module: nn.Module = comp.build(node.params, in_specs)

    meta_inputs: dict[str, Signal] = {}
    for pname, rspec in in_specs.items():
        tensor = torch.empty(rspec.shape, dtype=rspec.dtype, device="meta")
        mask = (
            torch.empty(rspec.shape[:2], dtype=torch.bool, device="meta")
            if rspec.has_mask and len(rspec.shape) >= 2
            else None
        )
        meta_inputs[pname] = Signal(data=tensor, mask=mask, meta={"layout": rspec.layout})

    captured_taps: dict[str, Signal] = {}
    hooks = []
    tap_map = sig.tap_map()

    use_forward_with_taps = hasattr(module, "forward_with_taps") and callable(
        getattr(module, "forward_with_taps")
    )

    if used_taps and not use_forward_with_taps:
        for tap_name in used_taps:
            tspec = tap_map[tap_name]
            submod = module.get_submodule(tspec.source)

            def _make_hook(tname: str, layout: str):
                def _hook(_mod, _inp, out):
                    t = out.data if isinstance(out, Signal) else out
                    captured_taps[tname] = Signal(data=t, meta={"layout": layout})

                return _hook

            hooks.append(submod.register_forward_hook(_make_hook(tap_name, tspec.spec.layout)))

    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            if use_forward_with_taps and used_taps:
                out_signals, raw_taps = module.forward_with_taps(meta_inputs)
                for tap_name in used_taps:
                    if tap_name in raw_taps:
                        captured_taps[tap_name] = raw_taps[tap_name]
            else:
                out_signals = module(meta_inputs)
    finally:
        for h in hooks:
            h.remove()

    out_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec] = {}
    sig_out_map = sig.output_map()
    for oname, osig in out_signals.items():
        layout = osig.layout
        if layout == "ANY" and oname in sig_out_map:
            layout = sig_out_map[oname].layout
        out_specs[oname] = ResolvedSpec(
            shape=tuple(osig.data.shape),
            dtype=osig.data.dtype,
            layout=layout,
            has_mask=osig.mask is not None,
        )

    tap_specs: dict[str, ResolvedSpec] = {}
    for tname, tsig in captured_taps.items():
        layout = tsig.layout
        if layout == "ANY" and tname in tap_map:
            layout = tap_map[tname].spec.layout
        tap_specs[tname] = ResolvedSpec(
            shape=tuple(tsig.data.shape),
            dtype=tsig.data.dtype,
            layout=layout,
            has_mask=tsig.mask is not None,
        )

    return out_specs, tap_specs


def run_erc(graph: GraphIR, registry: ComponentRegistry) -> ERCResult:
    """对 GraphIR 执行完整 ERC 检查与 Meta 形状推导。"""
    diagnostics: list[Diagnostic] = []
    node_map: dict[str, NodeIR] = {}

    for n in graph.nodes:
        if n.id in node_map:
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(level="error", code="DUPLICATE_NODE_ID", node_id=n.id, message=f"重复节点 ID: {n.id}")
            )
            continue
        if not registry.has(n.type):
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(level="error", code="UNKNOWN_COMPONENT", node_id=n.id, message=f"未注册元件: {n.type}")
            )
            continue
        node_map[n.id] = n

    if any(d.level == "error" for d in diagnostics):
        return ERCResult(passed=False, topo_order=[], diagnostics=diagnostics)

    signatures: dict[str, ComponentSignature] = {}
    components: dict[str, BaseComponent] = {}
    for nid, node in node_map.items():
        cls = registry.get(node.type)
        comp = cls()
        components[nid] = comp
        try:
            signatures[nid] = cls.declare_ports(node.params)
        except Exception as exc:
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(
                    level="error",
                    code="DECLARE_PORTS_FAILED",
                    node_id=nid,
                    message=f"声明端口失败: {exc}",
                )
            )

    if any(d.level == "error" for d in diagnostics):
        return ERCResult(passed=False, topo_order=[], diagnostics=diagnostics)

    # 校验边与端口合法性
    incoming_by_port: dict[tuple[str, str], list[EdgeIR]] = defaultdict(list)
    adj: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    indegree: dict[str, int] = {nid: 0 for nid in node_map}
    used_taps_by_node: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    connected_nodes: set[str] = set()

    for edge in graph.edges:
        if edge.src_node not in node_map or edge.dst_node not in node_map:
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(
                    level="error",
                    code="DANGLING_EDGE",
                    edge_id=edge.id,
                    message=f"边引用了不存在的节点: {edge.src_node} -> {edge.dst_node}",
                )
            )
            continue

        src_sig = signatures[edge.src_node]
        dst_sig = signatures[edge.dst_node]

        if edge.edge_kind == "tap":
            if edge.src_port not in src_sig.tap_map():
                diagnostics.append(
                    Diagnostic(
                        level="error",
                        code="UNKNOWN_TAP",
                        edge_id=edge.id,
                        node_id=edge.src_node,
                        message=f"节点 '{edge.src_node}' 不存在抽头 '{edge.src_port}'",
                    )
                )
                continue
            used_taps_by_node[edge.src_node].add(edge.src_port)
        else:
            if edge.src_port not in src_sig.output_map():
                diagnostics.append(
                    Diagnostic(
                        level="error",
                        code="UNKNOWN_OUTPUT_PORT",
                        edge_id=edge.id,
                        node_id=edge.src_node,
                        message=f"节点 '{edge.src_node}' 不存在输出端口 '{edge.src_port}'",
                    )
                )
                continue

        if edge.dst_port not in dst_sig.input_map():
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(
                    level="error",
                    code="UNKNOWN_INPUT_PORT",
                    edge_id=edge.id,
                    node_id=edge.dst_node,
                    message=f"节点 '{edge.dst_node}' 不存在输入端口 '{edge.dst_port}'",
                )
            )
            continue

        incoming_by_port[(edge.dst_node, edge.dst_port)].append(edge)
        adj[edge.src_node].append(edge.dst_node)
        indegree[edge.dst_node] += 1
        connected_nodes.add(edge.src_node)
        connected_nodes.add(edge.dst_node)

    # 检查必填端口和重复连线
    for nid, sig in signatures.items():
        for p in sig.inputs:
            edges = incoming_by_port.get((nid, p.name), [])
            if not edges and not p.optional:
                diagnostics.append(
                    Diagnostic(
                        level="error",
                        code="UNCONNECTED_REQUIRED_PORT",
                        node_id=nid,
                        message=f"节点 '{nid}' 的必填输入端口 '{p.name}' 未连接",
                    )
                )
            elif len(edges) > 1:
                diagnostics.append(
                    Diagnostic(
                        level="error",
                        code="MULTIPLE_INPUT_EDGES",
                        node_id=nid,
                        message=f"节点 '{nid}' 的输入端口 '{p.name}' 存在多条连线（请使用 TensorAdd 等显式融合节点）",
                    )
                )

    for nid in node_map:
        if nid not in connected_nodes and len(node_map) > 1:
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(
                    level="warning",
                    code="ISOLATED_NODE",
                    node_id=nid,
                    message=f"节点 '{nid}' 未连接到任何其他节点",
                )
            )

    # 拓扑排序与环检测
    q = deque([nid for nid, deg in indegree.items() if deg == 0])
    topo_order: list[str] = []
    while q:
        curr = q.popleft()
        topo_order.append(curr)
        for nxt in adj[curr]:
            indegree[nxt] -= 1
            if indegree[nxt] == 0:
                q.append(nxt)

    if len(topo_order) != len(node_map):
        cyclic = [nid for nid, deg in indegree.items() if deg > 0]
        diagnostics.append(
            Diagnostic(
                level="error",
                code="CYCLE_DETECTED",
                message=f"计算图存在有向环，涉及节点: {cyclic}",
            )
        )

    if any(d.level == "error" for d in diagnostics):
        return ERCResult(passed=False, topo_order=topo_order, diagnostics=diagnostics, signatures=signatures)

    # Meta 形状推导
    node_in_specs: dict[str, dict[str, ResolvedSpec]] = {}
    node_out_specs: dict[str, dict[str, ResolvedSpec]] = {}
    node_tap_specs: dict[str, dict[str, ResolvedSpec]] = {}
    auto_bridges: dict[str, AutoBridgePlan] = {}

    for nid in topo_order:
        node = node_map[nid]
        comp = components[nid]
        sig = signatures[nid]
        in_map = sig.input_map()

        resolved_in: dict[str, ResolvedSpec] = {}
        node_failed = False

        # 按签名输入顺序解析，保证 TensorAdd 等多端口对比稳定
        for port_spec in sig.inputs:
            edges = incoming_by_port.get((nid, port_spec.name), [])
            if not edges:
                continue
            edge = edges[0]
            if edge.edge_kind == "tap":
                tap_dict = node_tap_specs.get(edge.src_node, {})
                if edge.src_port not in tap_dict:
                    node_failed = True
                    continue
                src_spec = tap_dict[edge.src_port]
            else:
                out_dict = node_out_specs.get(edge.src_node, {})
                if edge.src_port not in out_dict:
                    node_failed = True
                    continue
                src_spec = out_dict[edge.src_port]

            if not _check_port_compat(edge, src_spec, port_spec, diagnostics):
                node_failed = True
                continue

            expected = _resolve_expected_dim(comp, node, port_spec.name, src_spec, resolved_in)
            if expected is not None and expected.shape != src_spec.shape:
                if _can_autobridge(src_spec, expected):
                    if edge.bridge == "auto":
                        auto_bridges[edge.id] = AutoBridgePlan(
                            edge_id=edge.id,
                            src_spec=src_spec,
                            dst_spec=expected,
                        )
                        diagnostics.append(
                            Diagnostic(
                                level="info",
                                code="AUTO_BRIDGE_INSERTED",
                                edge_id=edge.id,
                                node_id=nid,
                                message=f"边 '{edge.id}' 自动插入 MatrixBridge: {src_spec.shape} -> {expected.shape}",
                            )
                        )
                        src_spec = expected
                    else:
                        diagnostics.append(
                            Diagnostic(
                                level="error",
                                code="DIM_MISMATCH",
                                edge_id=edge.id,
                                node_id=nid,
                                message=(
                                    f"维度不匹配: 上游 {src_spec.shape} vs 期望 {expected.shape}。"
                                    f"请设置 bridge='auto' 或手动插入 MatrixBridge"
                                ),
                            )
                        )
                        node_failed = True
                        continue
                else:
                    diagnostics.append(
                        Diagnostic(
                            level="error",
                            code="SHAPE_MISMATCH",
                            edge_id=edge.id,
                            node_id=nid,
                            message=f"形状不可桥接: 上游 {src_spec.shape} vs 期望 {expected.shape}",
                        )
                    )
                    node_failed = True
                    continue

            resolved_in[port_spec.name] = src_spec

        if node_failed:
            continue

        node_in_specs[nid] = resolved_in
        try:
            out_specs, tap_specs = _run_meta_node(
                comp=comp,
                node=node,
                in_specs=resolved_in,
                used_taps=used_taps_by_node.get(nid, set()),
                sig=sig,
            )
            node_out_specs[nid] = out_specs
            node_tap_specs[nid] = tap_specs
        except Exception as exc:
            diagnostics.append(
                Diagnostic(
                    level="error",
                    code="META_INFER_FAILED",
                    node_id=nid,
                    message=f"Meta 形状推导失败: {exc}",
                )
            )

    passed = not any(d.level == "error" for d in diagnostics)
    return ERCResult(
        passed=passed,
        topo_order=topo_order,
        diagnostics=diagnostics,
        signatures=signatures,
        node_in_specs=node_in_specs,
        node_out_specs=node_out_specs,
        node_tap_specs=node_tap_specs,
        auto_bridges=auto_bridges,
    )

================
netpulse/core/ir.py
================
"""GraphIR：计算图 JSON / YAML 唯一事实源。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Optional

import yaml
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class NodeIR(BaseModel):
    id: str
    type: str
    params: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    position: Optional[dict[str, float]] = None
    # 用户自定义显示标签（留空则用元件中文名）
    label: Optional[str] = None


class EdgeIR(BaseModel):
    id: str
    src_node: str
    src_port: str
    dst_node: str
    dst_port: str
    edge_kind: Literal["signal", "tap", "loss"] = "signal"
    bridge: Literal["auto", "none"] = "none"


class LossConfig(BaseModel):
    node_id: str
    weight: float = 1.0
    enabled: bool = True


class TrainerConfig(BaseModel):
    optimizer: Literal["adam", "adamw", "sgd"] = "adam"
    lr: float = 1e-3
    weight_decay: float = 0.0
    batch_size: int = 64
    epochs: int = 3
    max_steps: Optional[int] = None
    grad_clip: Optional[float] = 1.0
    device: str = "cpu"
    seed: int = 42
    eval_every_steps: int = 50
    infer_outputs: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class GraphIR(BaseModel):
    name: str = "untitled_graph"
    nodes: list[NodeIR]
    edges: list[EdgeIR]
    losses: list[LossConfig] = Field(default_factory=list)
    trainer: TrainerConfig = Field(default_factory=TrainerConfig)

    @classmethod
    def from_file(cls, path: str | Path) -> "GraphIR":
        p = Path(path)
        text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if p.suffix.lower() in {".yaml", ".yml"}:
            data = yaml.safe_load(text)
        else:
            data = json.loads(text)
        return cls.model_validate(data)

    def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent)

    def to_file(self, path: str | Path) -> None:
        p = Path(path)
        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if p.suffix.lower() in {".yaml", ".yml"}:
            text = yaml.safe_dump(self.model_dump(), allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False)
        else:
            text = self.to_json(indent=2) + "\n"
        p.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")

================
netpulse/core/registry.py
================
"""元件注册表与自定义插件扫描。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import importlib.util
import logging
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Type

from netpulse.core.component import BaseComponent

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ComponentRegistry:
    """管理所有已注册的元件类。"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._components: dict[str, Type[BaseComponent]] = {}
        self._loaded_plugin_paths: set[str] = set()
        self.plugin_errors: dict[str, str] = {}

    def register(self, type_id: str, cls: Type[BaseComponent]) -> None:
        if not issubclass(cls, BaseComponent):
            raise TypeError(f"{cls} must inherit from BaseComponent")
        cls.type_id = type_id
        self._components[type_id] = cls

    def get(self, type_id: str) -> Type[BaseComponent]:
        if type_id not in self._components:
            raise KeyError(f"Unknown component type: '{type_id}'")
        return self._components[type_id]

    def has(self, type_id: str) -> bool:
        return type_id in self._components

    def list_types(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted(self._components.keys())

    def describe_component(self, type_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """导出单个元件的元信息（对标 ComfyUI /object_info）。"""
        cls = self.get(type_id)
        defaults = cls.default_params()
        try:
            sig = cls.declare_ports(defaults)
        except Exception:
            sig = cls.declare_ports({})
        return {
            "type_id": type_id,
            "display_name": cls.display_name or type_id,
            "title": cls.title or cls.display_name or type_id,
            "category": cls.category,
            "description": cls.description or (cls.__doc__ or "").strip(),
            "params": [p.to_dict() for p in cls.param_schema()],
            "default_params": defaults,
            "inputs": [
                {
                    "name": p.name,
                    "kind": p.kind,
                    "layout": p.layout,
                    "optional": p.optional,
                    "title": p.title or p.name,
                    "description": p.description,
                }
                for p in sig.inputs
            ],
            "outputs": [
                {
                    "name": p.name,
                    "kind": p.kind,
                    "layout": p.layout,
                    "title": p.title or p.name,
                    "description": p.description,
                }
                for p in sig.outputs
            ],
            "taps": [
                {
                    "name": t.name,
                    "source": t.source,
                    "layout": t.spec.layout,
                    "title": t.spec.title or t.name,
                    "description": t.spec.description,
                }
                for t in sig.taps
            ],
        }

    def describe_all(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        """导出全部已注册元件的 Schema 与端口定义。"""
        return {type_id: self.describe_component(type_id) for type_id in self.list_types()}

    def load_plugins(self, plugins_dir: str | Path) -> list[str]:
        """扫描 plugins 目录下的 .py 文件与子包（__init__.py），单插件失败不中断整体加载。"""
        root = Path(plugins_dir)
        if not root.exists():
            return []

        candidates: list[Path] = []
        for path in sorted(root.glob("*.py")):
            if not path.name.startswith("_"):
                candidates.append(path)
        for sub in sorted(root.iterdir()):
            init_file = sub / "__init__.py"
            if sub.is_dir() and not sub.name.startswith("_") and init_file.exists():
                candidates.append(init_file)

        loaded: list[str] = []
        for path in candidates:
            resolved = str(path.resolve())
            if resolved in self._loaded_plugin_paths:
                loaded.append(str(path))
                continue
            stem = path.parent.name if path.name == "__init__.py" else path.stem
            mod_name = f"netpulse_plugin_{stem}"
            try:
                spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(mod_name, path)
                if spec and spec.loader:
                    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
                    sys.modules[mod_name] = module
                    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
                    self._loaded_plugin_paths.add(resolved)
                    self.plugin_errors.pop(str(path), None)
                    loaded.append(str(path))
            except Exception as exc:
                self.plugin_errors[str(path)] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                logger.warning("Failed to load plugin %s: %s", path, exc)
        return loaded


_DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ComponentRegistry()
_BUILTINS_LOADED = False


def register_component(
    type_id: str,
    registry: ComponentRegistry | None = None,
):
    """装饰器：将元件类注册到指定或全局默认注册表。"""

    def decorator(cls: Type[BaseComponent]) -> Type[BaseComponent]:
        target = registry or _DEFAULT_REGISTRY
        target.register(type_id, cls)
        return cls

    return decorator


def get_default_registry(plugins_dir: str | Path | None = "plugins") -> ComponentRegistry:
    """获取包含内置元件及可选 plugins 自定义元件的默认注册表。"""
    global _BUILTINS_LOADED
    if not _BUILTINS_LOADED:
        importlib.import_module("netpulse.components")
        _BUILTINS_LOADED = True
    if plugins_dir is not None:
        _DEFAULT_REGISTRY.load_plugins(plugins_dir)
    return _DEFAULT_REGISTRY

netpulse/core/trainer.py
