"""Trainer：基于 CompiledGraph 的 PyTorch 训练与评估闭环。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

from netpulse.core.compiler import CompiledGraph
from netpulse.core.types import Signal


@dataclass
class TrainResult:
    """训练完成后的摘要指标与历史记录。"""

    steps: int
    history: list[dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=list)
    final_train_loss: float = 0.0
    final_val_loss: Optional[float] = None
    final_val_acc: Optional[float] = None


def _build_optimizer(model: nn.Module, name: str, lr: float, weight_decay: float) -> torch.optim.Optimizer:
    params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
    if not params:
        raise ValueError("CompiledGraph 中没有可训练参数")
    if name == "adam":
        return torch.optim.Adam(params, lr=lr, weight_decay=weight_decay)
    if name == "adamw":
        return torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=lr, weight_decay=weight_decay)
    if name == "sgd":
        return torch.optim.SGD(params, lr=lr, momentum=0.9, weight_decay=weight_decay)
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported optimizer: {name}")


def _infer_x_layout(x: torch.Tensor) -> str:
    if x.ndim == 4:
        return "BCHW"
    if x.ndim == 3:
        return "BLD"
    if x.ndim == 2:
        return "BD"
    return "ANY"


def _to_signal_batch(
    source_id: str,
    raw_batch: Any,
    device: torch.device,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Signal]]:
    if isinstance(raw_batch, (tuple, list)) and len(raw_batch) in (2, 3):
        x = raw_batch[0].to(device)
        y = raw_batch[1].to(device)
        mask = raw_batch[2].to(device).bool() if len(raw_batch) == 3 and raw_batch[2] is not None else None
        x_layout = _infer_x_layout(x)
        return {
            source_id: {
                "x": Signal(data=x, mask=mask, meta={"layout": x_layout}),
                "y": Signal(data=y, meta={"layout": _infer_x_layout(y) if y.ndim >= 2 else "B"}),
            }
        }
    if isinstance(raw_batch, dict):
        mask = raw_batch.get("mask")
        mask_dev = mask.to(device).bool() if isinstance(mask, torch.Tensor) else None
        moved: dict[str, Signal] = {}
        for k, v in raw_batch.items():
            if k == "mask":
                continue
            vt = v.to(device)
            moved[k] = Signal(
                data=vt,
                mask=mask_dev if k == "x" else None,
                meta={"layout": _infer_x_layout(vt) if (k != "y" or vt.ndim >= 2) else "B"},
            )
        return {source_id: moved}
    raise TypeError(f"Unsupported batch format from source '{source_id}': {type(raw_batch)}")


def _extract_acc(
    compiled: CompiledGraph,
    out_signals: dict[str, dict[str, Signal]],
    batch: dict[str, dict[str, Signal]],
    source_id: str,
) -> Optional[tuple[int, int]]:
    if source_id not in batch or "y" not in batch[source_id]:
        return None
    y = batch[source_id]["y"].data
    if y.dtype not in (torch.int64, torch.int32):
        return None

    node_types = {n.id: n.type for n in compiled.graph_ir.nodes}
    for edge in compiled.graph_ir.edges:
        if node_types.get(edge.dst_node) == "cross_entropy_loss" and edge.dst_port == "logits":
            src_ports = out_signals.get(edge.src_node, {})
            if edge.src_port in src_ports:
                logits = src_ports[edge.src_port].data
                if logits.ndim == 2 and logits.shape[0] == y.shape[0]:
                    preds = logits.argmax(dim=-1)
                    return int((preds == y).sum().item()), int(y.shape[0])
    return None


class Trainer:
    """驱动 CompiledGraph 完成训练、验证与权重导出。"""

    def __init__(self, compiled: CompiledGraph) -> None:
        self.compiled = compiled
        self.cfg = compiled.graph_ir.trainer

    def _find_source_id(self) -> str:
        sources = [nid for nid, cat in self.compiled.categories.items() if cat == "source"]
        if len(sources) != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Phase 0 Trainer 要求恰好 1 个 source 节点，当前发现: {sources}")
        return sources[0]

    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(self, val_loader, source_id: str, device: torch.device) -> tuple[float, Optional[float]]:
        self.compiled.eval()
        total_loss = 0.0
        n_batches = 0
        correct = 0
        total_samples = 0

        for raw_batch in val_loader:
            batch = _to_signal_batch(source_id, raw_batch, device)
            out = self.compiled(batch, mode="eval", compute_losses=True)
            if out.total_loss is not None:
                total_loss += float(out.total_loss.item())
                n_batches += 1
            acc_pair = _extract_acc(self.compiled, out.outputs, batch, source_id)
            if acc_pair is not None:
                correct += acc_pair[0]
                total_samples += acc_pair[1]

        avg_loss = total_loss / max(1, n_batches)
        acc = (correct / total_samples) if total_samples > 0 else None
        return avg_loss, acc

    def fit(self, verbose: bool = True) -> TrainResult:
        torch.manual_seed(self.cfg.seed)
        device = torch.device(self.cfg.device)
        self.compiled.to(device)

        source_id = self._find_source_id()
        source_mod = self.compiled.nodes_mod[source_id]
        train_loader, val_loader = source_mod.build_dataloaders(
            batch_size=self.cfg.batch_size,
            seed=self.cfg.seed,
        )

        optimizer = _build_optimizer(
            self.compiled,
            name=self.cfg.optimizer,
            lr=self.cfg.lr,
            weight_decay=self.cfg.weight_decay,
        )

        history: list[dict[str, float]] = []
        step = 0
        last_train_loss = 0.0

        for epoch in range(self.cfg.epochs):
            self.compiled.train()
            for raw_batch in train_loader:
                batch = _to_signal_batch(source_id, raw_batch, device)
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                out = self.compiled(batch, mode="train")
                if out.total_loss is None:
                    raise RuntimeError("训练图未产生任何 Loss，请检查 LossNode 连接与配置")
                out.total_loss.backward()
                if self.cfg.grad_clip is not None:
                    nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.compiled.parameters(), self.cfg.grad_clip)
                optimizer.step()

                step += 1
                last_train_loss = float(out.total_loss.item())
                rec: dict[str, float] = {
                    "step": float(step),
                    "epoch": float(epoch + 1),
                    "total_loss": last_train_loss,
                }
                for lname, lval in out.loss_dict.items():
                    rec[f"loss/{lname}"] = float(lval.item())
                history.append(rec)

                if verbose and (step == 1 or step % self.cfg.eval_every_steps == 0):
                    parts = " ".join(f"{k}={v:.4f}" for k, v in rec.items() if k.startswith("loss/"))
                    print(f"[epoch {epoch+1} step {step}] total_loss={last_train_loss:.4f} {parts}")

                if self.cfg.max_steps is not None and step >= self.cfg.max_steps:
                    break

            if self.cfg.max_steps is not None and step >= self.cfg.max_steps:
                break

        val_loss: Optional[float] = None
        val_acc: Optional[float] = None
        if val_loader is not None:
            val_loss, val_acc = self.evaluate(val_loader, source_id, device)
            if verbose:
                acc_str = f" val_acc={val_acc:.4f}" if val_acc is not None else ""
                print(f"[eval] val_loss={val_loss:.4f}{acc_str}")

        return TrainResult(
            steps=step,
            history=history,
            final_train_loss=last_train_loss,
            final_val_loss=val_loss,
            final_val_acc=val_acc,
        )

    def save_checkpoint(self, path: str | Path) -> Path:
        p = Path(path)
        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        payload = {
            "state_dict": self.compiled.state_dict(),
            "graph_ir": self.compiled.graph_ir.model_dump(),
        }
        torch.save(payload, p)
        return p

================
netpulse/core/types.py
================
"""核心协议与运行时数据结构。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Literal, Optional
import torch

PortKind = Literal["tensor", "loss", "data_batch", "any"]
CategoryKind = Literal["source", "backbone", "op", "bridge", "loss", "output"]
ParamType = Literal["int", "float", "bool", "string", "select", "json"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParamSpec:
    """元件参数契约（用于 WebUI 动态表单渲染与 Schema 校验）。"""

    name: str
    type: ParamType = "string"
    default: Any = None
    required: bool = False
    options: Optional[list[Any]] = None
    min: Optional[float] = None
    max: Optional[float] = None
    step: Optional[float] = None
    description: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "type": self.type,
            "default": self.default,
            "required": self.required,
            "options": list(self.options) if self.options is not None else None,
            "min": self.min,
            "max": self.max,
            "step": self.step,
            "description": self.description,
        }


@dataclass
class Signal:
    """统一边载荷，封装张量本体、可选 Mask 与语义元数据。"""

    data: torch.Tensor
    mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
    meta: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def shape(self) -> tuple[int, ...]:
        return tuple(self.data.shape)

    @property
    def layout(self) -> str:
        return str(self.meta.get("layout", "ANY"))

    def clone_with(
        self,
        data: torch.Tensor,
        mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        **meta_updates: Any,
    ) -> "Signal":
        new_meta = dict(self.meta)
        new_meta.update(meta_updates)
        return Signal(
            data=data,
            mask=self.mask if mask is None else mask,
            meta=new_meta,
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PortSpec:
    """元件静态端口契约。"""

    name: str
    kind: PortKind = "tensor"
    rank: Optional[list[int]] = None
    layout: str = "ANY"
    require_mask: bool = False
    optional: bool = False
    dtype: str = "float32"
    # 中文含义（如「输入特征图」）与简单介绍，供 WebUI 展示
    title: str = ""
    description: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TapSpec:
    """隐层抽头契约。"""

    name: str
    source: str
    spec: PortSpec


@dataclass
class ComponentSignature:
    """元件静态签名（仅由 params 决定，不依赖运行时输入张量）。"""

    inputs: list[PortSpec] = field(default_factory=list)
    outputs: list[PortSpec] = field(default_factory=list)
    taps: list[TapSpec] = field(default_factory=list)

    def input_map(self) -> dict[str, PortSpec]:
        return {p.name: p for p in self.inputs}

    def output_map(self) -> dict[str, PortSpec]:
        return {p.name: p for p in self.outputs}

    def tap_map(self) -> dict[str, TapSpec]:
        return {t.name: t for t in self.taps}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ResolvedSpec:
    """经 Meta 推导后解析出的具体端口形状与语义规格。"""

    shape: tuple[int, ...]
    dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32
    layout: str = "ANY"
    has_mask: bool = False

    @property
    def rank(self) -> int:
        return len(self.shape)


@dataclass
class Diagnostic:
    """ERC 静态检查诊断条目。"""

    level: Literal["error", "warning", "info"]
    code: str
    message: str
    node_id: Optional[str] = None
    edge_id: Optional[str] = None


@dataclass
class GraphOutput:
    """CompiledGraph 单次前向输出。"""

    outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Signal]]
    tap_bus: dict[str, Signal]
    loss_dict: dict[str, torch.Tensor]
    total_loss: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
<title>NetPulse WebUI — 节点流计算图沙盒</title>

<script>
const NODE_W = 228;
const CAT_ORDER = ["source", "backbone", "op", "bridge", "loss", "output"];
const CAT_LABELS = {
  source: "数据源 Source",
  backbone: "主干 Backbone",
  op: "算子 Op",
  bridge: "桥接 Bridge",
  loss: "目标 Loss",
  output: "输出工具 Output",
};
const PALETTE = ["#38bdf8", "#34d399", "#fbbf24", "#f472b6", "#a78bfa"];
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

const state = {
  components: {},
  currentFile: "",
  draftGraph: null,       // 唯一客户端编辑状态 { name, nodes, edges, losses, trainer }
  viewData: null,         // 最近一次 /api/graph 或 /api/erc/check 返回结果
  dirty: false,
  undoStack: [],
  redoStack: [],
  selectedNodeId: null,
  selectedEdgeId: null,
  positions: {},
  pan: { x: 20, y: 20 },
  scale: 1.0,
  running: false,
  paused: false,
  history: [],
  lossSeries: {},
  ws: null,
  nodeEls: {},
  edgeMeta: [],
  lastPulseTs: 0,
  wireDrag: null,         // { srcNode, srcPort, isTap }
  ercTimer: null,
  quickPos: { x: 200, y: 200 },
  probeHistory: {},       // { nodeId: { values: [], ema: [] } }
  latestProbes: {},       // { nodeId: snapshot }
  modalProbeId: null,
  modalProbeTab: "auto",
  expandedParams: {},     // { nodeId: true } 节点卡片内联参数面板展开状态
};

function deepClone(obj) { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj)); }

function setDirty(flag) {
  state.dirty = flag;
  $("dirtyBadge").style.display = flag ? "inline-block" : "none";
}

function updateHistoryButtons() {
  $("undoBtn").disabled = state.undoStack.length === 0;
  $("redoBtn").disabled = state.redoStack.length === 0;
}

function pushUndo() {
  if (!state.draftGraph) return;
  state.undoStack.push(deepClone(state.draftGraph));
  if (state.undoStack.length > 40) state.undoStack.shift();
  state.redoStack = [];
  setDirty(true);
  updateHistoryButtons();
}

function undo() {
  if (!state.undoStack.length) return;
  state.redoStack.push(deepClone(state.draftGraph));
  state.draftGraph = state.undoStack.pop();
  updateHistoryButtons();
  setDirty(true);
  runLiveErc();
}

function redo() {
  if (!state.redoStack.length) return;
  state.undoStack.push(deepClone(state.draftGraph));
  state.draftGraph = state.redoStack.pop();
  updateHistoryButtons();
  setDirty(true);
  runLiveErc();
}

function switchTab(tab) {
  const isIns = tab === "inspector";
  $("tabTelemetryBtn").classList.toggle("active", !isIns);
  $("tabInspectorBtn").classList.toggle("active", isIns);
  $("paneTelemetry").classList.toggle("active", !isIns);
  $("paneInspector").classList.toggle("active", isIns);
}

function applyTransform() {
  $("stage").style.transform = `translate(${state.pan.x}px, ${state.pan.y}px) scale(${state.scale})`;
  renderMinimap();
}

function screenToStage(clientX, clientY) {
  const rect = $("canvasWrap").getBoundingClientRect();
  return {
    x: (clientX - rect.left - state.pan.x) / state.scale,
    y: (clientY - rect.top - state.pan.y) / state.scale,
  };
}

function scheduleLiveErc(autoLayout = false) {
  clearTimeout(state.ercTimer);
  state.ercTimer = setTimeout(() => runLiveErc(autoLayout), 80);
}

async function runLiveErc(autoLayout = false) {
  if (!state.draftGraph) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/erc/check", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        name: state.currentFile || "draft.json",
        graph: state.draftGraph,
        auto_layout: autoLayout,
      }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) {
      $("ercBadge").className = "badge fail";
      $("ercBadge").textContent = "IR 校验异常";
      return;
    }
    if (autoLayout) {
      for (const vn of data.nodes) {
        const dn = state.draftGraph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === vn.id);
        if (dn) dn.position = { ...vn.position };
      }
    }
    renderGraph(data, { fit: false, syncDraft: false });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("ERC check failed", err);
  }
}

function renderPalette(filterText = "") {
  const list = $("paletteList");
  const q = filterText.trim().toLowerCase();
  const byCat = {};
  let total = 0;
  for (const [tid, info] of Object.entries(state.components)) {
    total += 1;
    const hay = [tid, info.display_name, info.title, info.description].filter(Boolean).join(" ").toLowerCase();
    const match = !q || hay.includes(q);
    if (!match) continue;
    (byCat[info.category] = byCat[info.category] || []).push(info);
  }
  $("compCount").textContent = String(total);
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const cat of CAT_ORDER) {
    const items = byCat[cat];
    if (!items || !items.length) continue;
    const grp = document.createElement("div");
    grp.innerHTML = `<div class="palette-group-title">${CAT_LABELS[cat] || cat}</div>`;
    for (const info of items) {
      const el = document.createElement("div");
      el.className = "palette-item";
      el.dataset.cat = info.category;
      el.draggable = true;
      el.title = `${info.title || info.display_name} ${info.display_name} — ${info.description || ""}`;
      el.innerHTML = `
        <div class="palette-main">
          <span class="zh-name">${escText(info.title || info.display_name)}</span>
          <span class="en-name">${escText(info.display_name || info.type_id)}</span>
          <span class="palette-desc">${escText(info.description || "")}</span>
        </div>`;
      el.addEventListener("dragstart", (ev) => {
        ev.dataTransfer.setData("text/netpulse-type", info.type_id);
      });
      el.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const wrap = $("canvasWrap");
        const cx = (wrap.clientWidth / 2 - state.pan.x) / state.scale - NODE_W / 2;
        const cy = (wrap.clientHeight / 2 - state.pan.y) / state.scale - 60;
        addNodeAt(info.type_id, cx + (Math.random() * 40 - 20), cy + (Math.random() * 40 - 20));
      });
      grp.appendChild(el);
    }
    list.appendChild(grp);
  }
}

function nextNodeId(typeId) {
  const existing = new Set((state.draftGraph?.nodes || []).map((n) => n.id));
  if (!existing.has(typeId)) return typeId;
  let i = 1;
  while (existing.has(`${typeId}_${i}`)) i++;
  return `${typeId}_${i}`;
}

function nextEdgeId() {
  const existing = new Set((state.draftGraph?.edges || []).map((e) => e.id));
  let i = 1;
  while (existing.has(`e${i}`)) i++;
  return `e${i}`;
}

function addNodeAt(typeId, x, y) {
  if (!state.draftGraph || !state.components[typeId]) return;
  const info = state.components[typeId];
  pushUndo();
  const id = nextNodeId(typeId);
  const params = deepClone(info.default_params || {});
  state.draftGraph.nodes.push({
    id,
    type: typeId,
    params,
    position: { x: Math.round(x), y: Math.round(y) },
  });
  if (info.category === "loss") {
    state.draftGraph.losses.push({ node_id: id, weight: 1.0, enabled: true });
  }
  state.selectedNodeId = id;
  state.selectedEdgeId = null;
  switchTab("inspector");
  runLiveErc();
}

function deleteSelected() {
  if (!state.draftGraph) return;
  if (state.selectedNodeId) {
    const nid = state.selectedNodeId;
    pushUndo();
    state.draftGraph.nodes = state.draftGraph.nodes.filter((n) => n.id !== nid);
    state.draftGraph.edges = state.draftGraph.edges.filter((e) => e.src_node !== nid && e.dst_node !== nid);
    state.draftGraph.losses = state.draftGraph.losses.filter((l) => l.node_id !== nid);
    state.selectedNodeId = null;
    runLiveErc();
    return;
  }
  if (state.selectedEdgeId) {
    const eid = state.selectedEdgeId;
    pushUndo();
    state.draftGraph.edges = state.draftGraph.edges.filter((e) => e.id !== eid);
    state.selectedEdgeId = null;
    runLiveErc();
  }
}

function summarizeParams(type, params) {
  if (!params || Object.keys(params).length === 0) return "默认参数";
  const pick = [];
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (k === "data_dir" || v === null || v === undefined || v === "") continue;
    const valStr = Array.isArray(v) ? `[${v.join(",")}]` : (typeof v === "object" ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v));
    pick.push(`${k}=${valStr}`);
    if (pick.length >= 4) break;
  }
  return pick.length ? pick.join(" · ") : "默认参数";
}

const CAT_ZH = { source: "数据源", backbone: "主干", op: "算子", bridge: "桥接", loss: "目标", output: "输出" };

function escText(s) {
  return String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c]));
}

function compInfo(typeId) {
  return state.components[typeId] || null;
}

function compTitleZh(typeId) {
  const c = compInfo(typeId);
  return (c && (c.title || c.display_name)) || typeId;
}

// 端口中文含义 / 简介：优先取元件 Schema，动态抽头如 layer_3 回退到模板描述
function portInfo(typeId, side, rawName) {
  const comp = compInfo(typeId) || {};
  const name = String(rawName || "").replace(/^tap:/, "");
  const list = side === "in" ? comp.inputs : side === "out" ? comp.outputs : comp.taps;
  let hit = (list || []).find((p) => p.name === name);
  if (side === "tap") {
    // 动态抽头（layer_0 / layer_1 ...）按序号生成中文名
    const m = name.match(/^layer_(\d+)$/);
    const tpl = hit || (comp.taps || [])[0];
    if (m && tpl) hit = { title: `第 ${Number(m[1]) + 1} 层抽头`, description: tpl.description || "", layout: tpl.layout || "" };
  }
  return {
    title: (hit && hit.title) || name,
    description: (hit && hit.description) || "",
    layout: hit && hit.layout ? hit.layout : "",
    optional: Boolean(hit && hit.optional),
  };
}

function paramSpecOf(comp, name) {
  return ((comp && comp.params) || []).find((p) => p.name === name) || null;
}

function fmtParamValue(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return "自动";
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.join(", ")}]`;
  if (typeof v === "object") return JSON.stringify(v);
  return String(v);
}

function paramControlHtml(ps, node) {
  const has = node.params[ps.name] !== undefined && node.params[ps.name] !== null;
  const val = has ? node.params[ps.name] : ps.default;
  if (ps.type === "select") {
    const cur = val === null || val === undefined ? "" : String(val);
    let opts = "";
    if (ps.default === null || ps.default === undefined) opts += `<option value="" ${cur === "" ? "selected" : ""}>(默认/自动)</option>`;
    for (const opt of ps.options || []) {
      const s = String(opt);
      opts += `<option value="${escText(s)}" ${cur === s ? "selected" : ""}>${escText(s || "(默认/自动)")}</option>`;
    }
    return `<select data-param="${ps.name}" data-ptype="select">${opts}</select>`;
  }
  if (ps.type === "bool") {
    return `<label class="pf-toggle"><input type="checkbox" data-param="${ps.name}" data-ptype="bool" ${val ? "checked" : ""} /><span>启用</span></label>`;
  }
  if (ps.type === "int" || ps.type === "float") {
    const strVal = val === null || val === undefined ? "" : String(val);
    const attrs = [
      ps.min !== null && ps.min !== undefined ? `min="${ps.min}"` : "",
      ps.max !== null && ps.max !== undefined ? `max="${ps.max}"` : "",
      ps.step !== null && ps.step !== undefined ? `step="${ps.step}"` : "",
    ].join(" ");
    return `<input type="number" data-param="${ps.name}" data-ptype="${ps.type}" value="${escText(strVal)}" placeholder="自动" ${attrs} />`;
  }
  if (ps.type === "json") {
    const strVal = val === null || val === undefined ? "" : JSON.stringify(val);
    return `<textarea rows="2" data-param="${ps.name}" data-ptype="json" placeholder='例：[64, 10]'>${escText(strVal)}</textarea>`;
  }
  return `<input type="text" data-param="${ps.name}" data-ptype="string" value="${escText(val ?? "")}" />`;
}

function paramFieldHtml(ps, node, opts = {}) {
  const isDefault = node.params[ps.name] === undefined || node.params[ps.name] === null;
  const defTxt = ps.default === null || ps.default === undefined ? "自动" : fmtParamValue(ps.default);
  const range = (ps.min !== null && ps.min !== undefined) || (ps.max !== null && ps.max !== undefined)
    ? `${ps.min ?? "-∞"} ~ ${ps.max ?? "∞"}` : "";
  const hint = [ps.description || "", range ? `范围 ${range}` : "", isDefault ? "" : `默认 ${defTxt}`]
    .filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
  const cls = opts.compact ? "pf pf-compact" : "pf";
  return `
    <div class="${cls}" data-pf="${ps.name}">
      <div class="pf-head">
        <span class="pf-name">${escText(ps.name)}</span>
        <span class="pf-desc" title="${escText(hint)}">${escText(hint)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="pf-body">
        ${paramControlHtml(ps, node)}
        <button type="button" class="pf-reset" data-reset="${ps.name}" title="恢复默认值（${escText(defTxt)}）">↺</button>
      </div>
      <div class="pf-err"></div>
    </div>`;
}

function extraParamFieldsHtml(node, renderedKeys) {
  const extra = Object.entries(node.params || {}).filter(([k]) => !renderedKeys.has(k));
  if (!extra.length) return "";
  return extra.map(([k, v]) => `
    <div class="pf" data-pf="${k}">
      <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">${escText(k)}</span><span class="pf-desc">高级 JSON（不在 Schema 中）</span></div>
      <div class="pf-body">
        <textarea rows="2" data-param="${k}" data-ptype="json">${escText(JSON.stringify(v))}</textarea>
      </div>
      <div class="pf-err"></div>
    </div>`).join("");
}

function setFieldInvalid(field, message) {
  if (!field) return;
  field.classList.toggle("invalid", Boolean(message));
  const err = field.querySelector(".pf-err");
  if (err) err.textContent = message || "";
}

// 参数表单绑定：类型转换 + 范围/JSON 校验 + 恢复默认
function bindParamForm(scope, node, comp, onChange) {
  scope.querySelectorAll("[data-param]").forEach((inp) => {
    const pname = inp.dataset.param;
    const ptype = inp.dataset.ptype;
    const ps = paramSpecOf(comp, pname);
    const field = inp.closest(".pf");
    const commit = () => {
      const raw = inp.value.trim();
      let val;
      if (ptype === "bool") {
        val = inp.checked;
      } else if (ptype === "int" || ptype === "float") {
        if (raw === "") {
          val = undefined;
        } else {
          val = ptype === "int" ? parseInt(raw, 10) : parseFloat(raw);
          if (!Number.isFinite(val)) return setFieldInvalid(field, "请输入数字");
          if (ps && ps.min !== null && ps.min !== undefined && val < ps.min) return setFieldInvalid(field, `不得小于 ${ps.min}`);
          if (ps && ps.max !== null && ps.max !== undefined && val > ps.max) return setFieldInvalid(field, `不得大于 ${ps.max}`);
        }
      } else if (ptype === "json") {
        if (raw === "") {
          val = undefined;
        } else {
          try {
            val = JSON.parse(raw);
          } catch (err) {
            return setFieldInvalid(field, "JSON 格式错误");
          }
        }
      } else if (ptype === "select") {
        val = raw === "" ? undefined : raw;
      } else {
        val = inp.value;
      }
      setFieldInvalid(field, null);
      const prev = node.params[pname];
      if (val === undefined && prev === undefined) return;
      if (val !== undefined && JSON.stringify(prev) === JSON.stringify(val)) return;
      pushUndo();
      if (val === undefined) {
        delete node.params[pname];
        if (ptype === "int" || ptype === "float") inp.value = "";
      } else {
        node.params[pname] = val;
      }
      if (onChange) onChange();
    };
    inp.addEventListener("change", commit);
    if (ptype === "json") inp.addEventListener("blur", commit);
  });
  scope.querySelectorAll("[data-reset]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", (ev) => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      const pname = btn.dataset.reset;
      const ps = paramSpecOf(comp, pname);
      const field = btn.closest(".pf");
      if (!ps) return;
      pushUndo();
      if (ps.default === null || ps.default === undefined) delete node.params[pname];
      else node.params[pname] = deepClone(ps.default);
      setFieldInvalid(field, null);
      const inp = field.querySelector("[data-param]");
      if (inp) {
        if (ps.type === "bool") inp.checked = Boolean(ps.default);
        else if (ps.type === "json") inp.value = ps.default === null || ps.default === undefined ? "" : JSON.stringify(ps.default);
        else if (ps.type === "select") inp.value = ps.default === null || ps.default === undefined ? "" : String(ps.default);
        else inp.value = ps.default === null || ps.default === undefined ? "" : String(ps.default);
      }
      if (onChange) onChange();
    });
  });
}

function startNodeRename(card, viewNode, draftNode) {
  const titleEl = card.querySelector(".node-title");
  if (!titleEl) return;
  const input = document.createElement("input");
  input.type = "text";
  input.className = "node-label-input";
  input.value = viewNode.label || "";
  input.placeholder = viewNode.title || viewNode.type;
  titleEl.replaceWith(input);
  input.focus();
  input.select();
  let done = false;
  const commit = (save) => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    const next = save ? input.value.trim() : (viewNode.label || "");
    const label = next ? next : null;
    if (label !== (viewNode.label || null)) {
      pushUndo();
      if (draftNode) draftNode.label = label;
      viewNode.label = label;
      scheduleLiveErc();
    } else {
      input.replaceWith(titleEl);
    }
  };
  input.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => ev.stopPropagation());
  input.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    if (ev.key === "Enter") commit(true);
    else if (ev.key === "Escape") commit(false);
  });
  input.addEventListener("blur", () => commit(true));
}

function getPortPos(nodeId, portName, side, isTap = false) {
  const pos = state.positions[nodeId] || { x: 0, y: 0 };
  const el = state.nodeEls[nodeId];
  if (!el) return { x: pos.x + (side === "left" ? 0 : NODE_W), y: pos.y + 60 };
  const selector = side === "left"
    ? `.ports-col.left .port-row[data-port="${portName}"]`
    : `.ports-col.right .port-row[data-port="${portName}"][data-tap="${isTap ? "1" : "0"}"]`;
  const row = el.querySelector(selector);
  const yOffset = row ? (row.offsetTop + row.offsetHeight / 2) : 60;
  const cardW = el.offsetWidth || NODE_W;
  return {
    x: pos.x + (side === "left" ? 0 : cardW),
    y: pos.y + yOffset,
  };
}

function renderEdges() {
  const group = $("edgesGroup");
  group.innerHTML = "";
  state.edgeMeta = [];
  if (!state.viewData) return;

  const errorEdges = new Set((state.viewData.erc?.diagnostics || []).filter((d) => d.level === "error" && d.edge_id).map((d) => d.edge_id));

  for (const edge of state.viewData.edges) {
    const isTap = edge.edge_kind === "tap";
    const p1 = getPortPos(edge.src_node, edge.src_port, "right", isTap);
    const p2 = getPortPos(edge.dst_node, edge.dst_port, "left", false);
    const dx = Math.max(48, Math.abs(p2.x - p1.x) * 0.45);
    const d = `M ${p1.x} ${p1.y} C ${p1.x + dx} ${p1.y}, ${p2.x - dx} ${p2.y}, ${p2.x} ${p2.y}`;

    const isSelected = state.selectedEdgeId === edge.id;
    const isErr = errorEdges.has(edge.id);
    const color = isErr ? "#fb7185" : (isTap ? "#fbbf24" : (edge.autobridge ? "#a855f7" : "#38bdf8"));

    const path = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "path");
    path.setAttribute("d", d);
    path.setAttribute("fill", "none");
    path.setAttribute("stroke", color);
    path.setAttribute("stroke-width", isTap ? "2" : "2.2");
    path.setAttribute("stroke-opacity", isSelected ? "1" : "0.75");
    path.setAttribute("class", `edge-visible${isSelected ? " selected" : ""}${isErr ? " error" : ""}`);
    if (isTap) path.setAttribute("stroke-dasharray", "6 4");
    group.appendChild(path);

    const hit = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "path");
    hit.setAttribute("d", d);
    hit.setAttribute("class", "edge-hit");
    hit.addEventListener("click", (ev) => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      state.selectedEdgeId = edge.id;
      state.selectedNodeId = null;
      renderEdges();
      renderNodeSelection();
      renderInspector();
      switchTab("inspector");
    });
    group.appendChild(hit);

    const midX = (p1.x + p2.x) / 2;
    const midY = (p1.y + p2.y) / 2;
    let labelText = edge.shape ? `(${edge.shape.join(",")})` : "";
    if (edge.autobridge) {
      labelText = `AutoBridge ${edge.autobridge.from.join("×")}→${edge.autobridge.to.join("×")}`;
    } else if (edge.bridge === "auto" && !isErr) {
      labelText = labelText ? `${labelText} [auto]` : "[auto]";
    }
    if (labelText) {
      const w = Math.max(44, labelText.length * 6.3 + 10);
      const rect = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "rect");
      rect.setAttribute("x", midX - w / 2);
      rect.setAttribute("y", midY - 9);
      rect.setAttribute("width", w);
      rect.setAttribute("height", 18);
      rect.setAttribute("class", "edge-label-bg");
      group.appendChild(rect);

      const txt = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "text");
      txt.setAttribute("x", midX);
      txt.setAttribute("y", midY);
      txt.setAttribute("class", "edge-label-txt");
      txt.textContent = labelText;
      group.appendChild(txt);
    }
    state.edgeMeta.push({ edge, path, color });
  }
}

function renderNodeSelection() {
  for (const [nid, card] of Object.entries(state.nodeEls)) {
    card.classList.toggle("selected", state.selectedNodeId === nid);
  }
}

function renderGraph(data, opts = { fit: true, syncDraft: true }) {
  state.viewData = data;
  if (opts.syncDraft) {
    state.draftGraph = {
      name: data.graph_name || "untitled_graph",
      nodes: data.nodes.map((n) => ({ id: n.id, type: n.type, params: deepClone(n.params || {}), position: { ...n.position }, label: n.label || null })),
      edges: data.edges.map((e) => ({
        id: e.id, src_node: e.src_node, src_port: e.src_port,
        dst_node: e.dst_node, dst_port: e.dst_port,
        edge_kind: e.edge_kind || "signal", bridge: e.bridge || "none",
      })),
      losses: deepClone(data.losses || []),
      trainer: deepClone(data.trainer || {}),
    };
    state.undoStack = [];
    state.redoStack = [];
    setDirty(false);
    updateHistoryButtons();
  }

  state.positions = {};
  state.nodeEls = {};
  const layer = $("nodesLayer");
  layer.innerHTML = "";

  const errorNodes = new Set((data.erc?.diagnostics || []).filter((d) => d.level === "error" && d.node_id).map((d) => d.node_id));

  // ERC 状态与日志
  const ercBadge = $("ercBadge");
  if (data.erc.passed) {
    ercBadge.className = "badge pass";
    ercBadge.textContent = `ERC PASS · ${data.nodes.length} 节点`;
  } else {
    const errCount = (data.erc.diagnostics || []).filter((d) => d.level === "error").length;
    ercBadge.className = "badge fail";
    ercBadge.textContent = `ERC FAIL (${errCount})`;
  }
  const logsEl = $("ercLogs");
  logsEl.innerHTML = "";
  const topoDiv = document.createElement("div");
  topoDiv.className = "log-item info";
  topoDiv.textContent = `拓扑序: ${data.erc.topo_order.join(" → ")}`;
  logsEl.appendChild(topoDiv);
  for (const d of data.erc.diagnostics) {
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = `log-item ${d.level}`;
    div.textContent = `[${d.code}] ${d.message}`;
    logsEl.appendChild(div);
  }

  $("epochsInput").placeholder = String(data.trainer.epochs);
  $("lrInput").placeholder = String(data.trainer.lr);
  renderLossItems(null);

  for (const node of data.nodes) {
    state.positions[node.id] = { ...node.position };
    const card = document.createElement("div");
    card.className = "node-card";
    if (state.selectedNodeId === node.id) card.classList.add("selected");
    if (errorNodes.has(node.id)) card.classList.add("erc-error");
    card.dataset.cat = node.category;
    card.dataset.id = node.id;
    card.style.left = `${node.position.x}px`;
    card.style.top = `${node.position.y}px`;

    const comp = compInfo(node.type) || { params: [], inputs: [], outputs: [], taps: [] };
    const quickOpen = Boolean(state.expandedParams[node.id]);

    const inHtml = node.inputs.map((p) => {
      const pi = portInfo(node.type, "in", p);
      const tip = `${p} · ${pi.title}${pi.description ? " — " + pi.description : ""}`;
      return `<div class="port-row" data-port="${p}" data-node="${node.id}" data-side="in"><span class="port-dot" title="拖入连线到此输入端口（${tip}）"></span><span class="port-label" title="${escText(tip)}"><b>${escText(pi.title)}</b></span></div>`;
    }).join("");
    const outHtml = node.outputs.map((p) => {
      const pi = portInfo(node.type, "out", p);
      const tip = `${p} · ${pi.title}${pi.description ? " — " + pi.description : ""}`;
      return `<div class="port-row" data-port="${p}" data-tap="0" data-node="${node.id}" data-side="out"><span class="port-label" title="${escText(tip)}"><b>${escText(pi.title)}</b></span><span class="port-dot" title="按住拖出连线（${tip}）"></span></div>`;
    }).join("");
    const tapHtml = node.taps.map((t) => {
      const pi = portInfo(node.type, "tap", t);
      const tip = `tap:${t} · ${pi.title}${pi.description ? " — " + pi.description : ""}`;
      return `<div class="port-row tap" data-port="${t}" data-tap="1" data-node="${node.id}" data-side="out"><span class="port-label" title="${escText(tip)}"><b>${escText(pi.title)}</b></span><span class="port-dot tap" title="按住拖出隐层抽头连线（${tip}）"></span></div>`;
    }).join("");

    const shapeEntry = Object.entries(node.out_specs || {})[0];
    const inShapeEntry = Object.entries(node.in_specs || {})[0];
    const staticShape = shapeEntry
      ? `shape=(${shapeEntry[1].shape.join(",")})`
      : (inShapeEntry ? `in=(${inShapeEntry[1].shape.join(",")})` : (errorNodes.has(node.id) ? "ERC Error" : "ready"));

    const footerHtml = node.category === "output"
      ? `
        <div class="node-probe" data-probe-node="${node.id}">
          <div class="probe-bar">
            <span class="probe-tag">${node.params?.mode || node.params?.view || "probe"}</span>
            <span class="probe-shape-txt">${staticShape}</span>
            <button type="button" class="probe-expand-btn" title="放大查看 / 切换视图">⤢</button>
          </div>
          <canvas class="probe-canvas" width="256" height="146"></canvas>
          <div class="probe-raw-wrap"></div>
          <div class="probe-hover">等待训练输出...</div>
          <div class="probe-meta"><span>min -</span><span>max -</span><span>μ -</span></div>
        </div>
      `
      : `
        <div class="node-live">
          <div class="live-text">${staticShape}</div>
          <div class="live-bar-bg"><div class="live-bar-fill"></div></div>
        </div>
      `;

    const renderedKeys = new Set(((comp && comp.params) || []).map((p) => p.name));
    const quickFields = ((comp && comp.params) || []).map((ps) => paramFieldHtml(ps, node, { compact: true })).join("");
    const quickExtra = extraParamFieldsHtml(node, renderedKeys);
    const quickHtml = quickOpen
      ? `<div class="node-quick">
           <div class="quick-tip">快速参数编辑 · 改值后自动重算 ERC</div>
           ${quickFields}${quickExtra}
           ${quickFields || quickExtra ? "" : `<div class="quick-tip">该元件无可编辑参数</div>`}
         </div>`
      : "";

    const titleZh = node.title || compTitleZh(node.type);
    const headLabel = node.label || titleZh;
    const subParts = [];
    if (node.label) subParts.push(escText(titleZh));
    if (node.description || (comp && comp.description)) subParts.push(escText(node.description || comp.description));

    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="node-head">
        <span class="node-title" title="${escText(node.id)}">${escText(headLabel)}</span>
        <span class="node-type" title="元件类型 ${escText(node.type)}">${escText(node.display_name || node.type)}</span>
        <span class="node-tools">
          <button type="button" class="node-tool-btn" data-act="rename" title="重命名显示标签">✎</button>
          <button type="button" class="node-tool-btn ${quickOpen ? "on" : ""}" data-act="params" title="展开/收起快速参数编辑">⚙</button>
        </span>
      </div>
      ${subParts.length ? `<div class="node-sub"><span class="node-sub-desc">${subParts.join(" · ")}</span></div>` : ""}
      <div class="node-params">${summarizeParams(node.type, node.params)}</div>
      <div class="node-ports">
        <div class="ports-col left">${inHtml}</div>
        <div class="ports-col right">${outHtml}${tapHtml}</div>
      </div>
      ${quickHtml}
      ${footerHtml}
    `;

    const draftNode = state.draftGraph ? state.draftGraph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === node.id) : null;
    card.querySelectorAll(".node-tool-btn").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => { ev.stopPropagation(); ev.preventDefault(); });
      btn.addEventListener("click", (ev) => {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        if (btn.dataset.act === "rename") {
          startNodeRename(card, node, draftNode);
          return;
        }
        state.selectedNodeId = node.id;
        state.selectedEdgeId = null;
        state.expandedParams[node.id] = !state.expandedParams[node.id];
        renderNodeSelection();
        renderEdges();
        renderInspector();
        btn.classList.toggle("on", Boolean(state.expandedParams[node.id]));
        // 延后重绘，避免在节点构建循环中重入 renderGraph
        setTimeout(() => renderGraph(data, { fit: false, syncDraft: false }), 0);
      });
    });

    if (quickOpen) {
      bindParamForm(card.querySelector(".node-quick"), draftNode || node, comp, () => {
        scheduleLiveErc();
      });
      card.querySelectorAll(".node-quick input, .node-quick select, .node-quick textarea, .node-quick button")
        .forEach((el) => el.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => ev.stopPropagation()));
    }

    if (node.category === "output") {
      const expandBtn = card.querySelector(".probe-expand-btn");
      expandBtn.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => ev.stopPropagation());
      expandBtn.addEventListener("click", (ev) => {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        openProbeModal(node.id);
      });
      const pCanvas = card.querySelector(".probe-canvas");
      pCanvas.addEventListener("mousemove", (ev) => handleProbeHover(ev, node.id, pCanvas, card.querySelector(".probe-hover")));
      pCanvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
        const hv = card.querySelector(".probe-hover");
        if (hv) hv.textContent = state.latestProbes[node.id] ? `shape=(${state.latestProbes[node.id].shape.join(",")})` : "等待训练输出...";
      });
      pCanvas.addEventListener("dblclick", (ev) => {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        openProbeModal(node.id);
      });
      if (state.latestProbes[node.id]) {
        renderProbeIntoCard(card, node.id, state.latestProbes[node.id]);
      }
    }

    card.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.target.closest(".port-dot")) return;
      if (ev.target.closest(".node-quick") || ev.target.closest(".node-tool-btn") || ev.target.closest(".node-label-input")) return;
      ev.stopPropagation();
      state.selectedNodeId = node.id;
      state.selectedEdgeId = null;
      renderNodeSelection();
      renderEdges();
      renderInspector();
    });

    card.addEventListener("dblclick", (ev) => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      state.selectedNodeId = node.id;
      state.selectedEdgeId = null;
      renderInspector();
      switchTab("inspector");
    });

    // 节点头拖拽移动
    const head = card.querySelector(".node-head");
    head.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      state.selectedNodeId = node.id;
      state.selectedEdgeId = null;
      renderNodeSelection();
      renderEdges();
      renderInspector();

      const startX = ev.clientX;
      const startY = ev.clientY;
      const origX = state.positions[node.id].x;
      const origY = state.positions[node.id].y;
      let moved = false;

      const onMove = (mv) => {
        const dx = (mv.clientX - startX) / state.scale;
        const dy = (mv.clientY - startY) / state.scale;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 2 || Math.abs(dy) > 2) moved = true;
        const nx = Math.round(origX + dx);
        const ny = Math.round(origY + dy);
        state.positions[node.id].x = nx;
        state.positions[node.id].y = ny;
        card.style.left = `${nx}px`;
        card.style.top = `${ny}px`;
        renderEdges();
        renderMinimap();
      };
      const onUp = () => {
        window.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
        window.removeEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
        if (moved && state.draftGraph) {
          pushUndo();
          const dn = state.draftGraph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === node.id);
          if (dn) dn.position = { ...state.positions[node.id] };
        }
      };
      window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
      window.addEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
    });

    // 端口拖拽连线
    card.querySelectorAll('.ports-col.right .port-dot').forEach((dot) => {
      dot.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        const row = dot.closest(".port-row");
        const isTap = row.dataset.tap === "1";
        state.wireDrag = { srcNode: node.id, srcPort: row.dataset.port, isTap };
        $("canvasWrap").classList.add("connecting");
        const wire = $("dragWire");
        wire.setAttribute("stroke", isTap ? "#fbbf24" : "#38bdf8");
        wire.style.display = "block";

        const p1 = getPortPos(node.id, row.dataset.port, "right", isTap);
        const updateWire = (clientX, clientY) => {
          const p2 = screenToStage(clientX, clientY);
          const dx = Math.max(40, Math.abs(p2.x - p1.x) * 0.45);
          wire.setAttribute("d", `M ${p1.x} ${p1.y} C ${p1.x + dx} ${p1.y}, ${p2.x - dx} ${p2.y}, ${p2.x} ${p2.y}`);
        };
        updateWire(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);

        const onMove = (mv) => updateWire(mv.clientX, mv.clientY);
        const onUp = (up) => {
          window.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
          window.removeEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
          $("canvasWrap").classList.remove("connecting");
          wire.style.display = "none";

          const targetEl = document.elementFromPoint(up.clientX, up.clientY);
          const inRow = targetEl?.closest?.('.ports-col.left .port-row');
          if (inRow && state.wireDrag && inRow.dataset.node !== state.wireDrag.srcNode) {
            const dstNode = inRow.dataset.node;
            const dstPort = inRow.dataset.port;
            pushUndo();
            // 替换目标单输入口的已有连线
            state.draftGraph.edges = state.draftGraph.edges.filter(
              (e) => !(e.dst_node === dstNode && e.dst_port === dstPort)
            );
            state.draftGraph.edges.push({
              id: nextEdgeId(),
              src_node: state.wireDrag.srcNode,
              src_port: state.wireDrag.srcPort,
              dst_node: dstNode,
              dst_port: dstPort,
              edge_kind: state.wireDrag.isTap ? "tap" : "signal",
              bridge: "auto",
            });
            runLiveErc();
          }
          state.wireDrag = null;
        };
        window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
        window.addEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
      });
    });

    layer.appendChild(card);
    state.nodeEls[node.id] = card;
  }

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    renderEdges();
    if (opts.fit) fitView();
    else renderMinimap();
    renderInspector();
  });
}

function renderInspector() {
  const box = $("inspectorBody");
  if (!state.draftGraph) {
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="card" style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">请先加载或创建计算图</div>`;
    return;
  }

  if (state.selectedEdgeId) {
    const edge = state.draftGraph.edges.find((e) => e.id === state.selectedEdgeId);
    const vEdge = state.viewData?.edges?.find((e) => e.id === state.selectedEdgeId);
    const srcNodeType = state.viewData?.nodes?.find((n) => n.id === edge?.src_node)?.type || "";
    const dstNodeType = state.viewData?.nodes?.find((n) => n.id === edge?.dst_node)?.type || "";
    if (edge) {
      box.innerHTML = `
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-title"><span>连线属性</span><span class="card-title-sub">${escText(edge.id)}</span></div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <label>数据流向</label>
            <div style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.7">
              <div><b>${escText(edge.src_node)}</b> · ${escText(portInfo(srcNodeType, edge.edge_kind === "tap" ? "tap" : "out", edge.src_port).title)} <span class="en-name">${escText(edge.src_port)}</span></div>
              <div style="color:var(--accent)">↓</div>
              <div><b>${escText(edge.dst_node)}</b> · ${escText(portInfo(dstNodeType, "in", edge.dst_port).title)} <span class="en-name">${escText(edge.dst_port)}</span></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="form-row"><label>类型</label><div style="font-size:12px">${escText(edge.edge_kind)} ${vEdge?.shape ? `· shape=(${vEdge.shape.join(",")})` : ""}</div></div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <label>AutoBridge 自动矩阵桥</label>
            <select id="edgeBridgeSel">
              <option value="auto" ${edge.bridge === "auto" ? "selected" : ""}>auto（维度不等时自动插桥）</option>
              <option value="none" ${edge.bridge === "none" ? "selected" : ""}>none（严格报错）</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <button id="delSelectedBtn" class="danger" style="width:100%;margin-top:4px">删除此连线 (Del)</button>
        </div>
      `;
      $("edgeBridgeSel").addEventListener("change", (ev) => {
        pushUndo();
        edge.bridge = ev.target.value;
        runLiveErc();
      });
      $("delSelectedBtn").addEventListener("click", deleteSelected);
      return;
    }
  }

  if (state.selectedNodeId) {
    const node = state.draftGraph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === state.selectedNodeId);
    if (node) {
      const comp = state.components[node.type] || { display_name: node.type, title: node.type, description: "", params: [], inputs: [], outputs: [], taps: [] };
      const lossCfg = state.draftGraph.losses.find((l) => l.node_id === node.id);
      const viewNode = (state.viewData?.nodes || []).find((n) => n.id === node.id) || {};
      const titleZh = comp.title || comp.display_name || node.type;

      const portEntries = [];
      for (const name of viewNode.inputs || []) {
        const pi = portInfo(node.type, "in", name);
        const spec = (viewNode.in_specs || {})[name];
        const meta = [
          `端口 ${name}`,
          pi.layout ? `布局 ${pi.layout}` : "",
          spec ? `shape=(${spec.shape.join(",")})` : "",
          pi.optional ? "可选" : "",
        ].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
        portEntries.push(`
          <div class="port-entry in">
            <div class="port-entry-head"><span class="port-entry-title">${escText(pi.title)}</span><span class="port-entry-key">输入 · ${escText(name)}</span></div>
            <div class="port-entry-desc">${escText(pi.description || "未填写说明")}</div>
            <div class="port-entry-meta">${escText(meta)}</div>
          </div>`);
      }
      for (const name of viewNode.outputs || []) {
        const pi = portInfo(node.type, "out", name);
        const spec = (viewNode.out_specs || {})[name];
        const meta = [`端口 ${name}`, pi.layout ? `布局 ${pi.layout}` : "", spec ? `shape=(${spec.shape.join(",")})` : ""]
          .filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
        portEntries.push(`
          <div class="port-entry out">
            <div class="port-entry-head"><span class="port-entry-title">${escText(pi.title)}</span><span class="port-entry-key">输出 · ${escText(name)}</span></div>
            <div class="port-entry-desc">${escText(pi.description || "未填写说明")}</div>
            <div class="port-entry-meta">${escText(meta)}</div>
          </div>`);
      }
      for (const name of viewNode.taps || []) {
        const pi = portInfo(node.type, "tap", name);
        const spec = (viewNode.tap_specs || {})[name];
        const meta = [`tap:${name}`, pi.layout ? `布局 ${pi.layout}` : "", spec ? `shape=(${spec.shape.join(",")})` : ""]
          .filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
        portEntries.push(`
          <div class="port-entry tap">
            <div class="port-entry-head"><span class="port-entry-title">${escText(pi.title)}</span><span class="port-entry-key">抽头 · tap:${escText(name)}</span></div>
            <div class="port-entry-desc">${escText(pi.description || "未填写说明")}</div>
            <div class="port-entry-meta">${escText(meta)}</div>
          </div>`);
      }
      const portCount = { in: (viewNode.inputs || []).length, out: (viewNode.outputs || []).length, tap: (viewNode.taps || []).length };

      const renderedKeys = new Set();
      let paramHtml = "";
      for (const ps of (comp.params || [])) {
        renderedKeys.add(ps.name);
        paramHtml += paramFieldHtml(ps, node);
      }
      paramHtml += extraParamFieldsHtml(node, renderedKeys);

      let html = `
        <div class="card comp-card">
          <div class="comp-head">
            <div>
              <div class="comp-title">${escText(titleZh)}</div>
              <div class="comp-en">${escText(comp.display_name || node.type)} · ${escText(node.type)}</div>
            </div>
            <span class="comp-cat">${escText(CAT_ZH[comp.category] || comp.category || "-")}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="comp-desc">${escText(comp.description || "（该元件暂无简介）")}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-title"><span>节点标识</span><span class="card-title-sub">改名不影响连线与 ERC</span></div>
          <div class="comp-node-id" style="margin-bottom:8px">节点 id <b>${escText(node.id)}</b></div>
          <div class="pf" style="margin-bottom:0">
            <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">显示标签</span><span class="pf-desc">留空则显示中文名「${escText(titleZh)}」</span></div>
            <div class="pf-body"><input id="nodeLabelInput" type="text" value="${escText(node.label || "")}" placeholder="${escText(titleZh)}" /></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-title"><span>端口说明</span><span class="card-title-sub">输入 ${portCount.in} · 输出 ${portCount.out} · 抽头 ${portCount.tap}</span></div>
          <div class="port-table">
            ${portEntries.length ? portEntries.join("") : `<div class="hint-text">该元件没有端口（终端探针类型）</div>`}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-title"><span>元件参数</span><span class="card-title-sub">${(comp.params || []).length} 项 · 留空表示自动</span></div>
          ${paramHtml || `<div class="hint-text">该元件无可配置参数</div>`}
        </div>
      `;

      if (lossCfg) {
        html += `
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-title"><span>训练目标汇集</span><span class="card-title-sub">total_loss</span></div>
            <div class="form-inline">
              <span>纳入 total_loss 训练目标</span>
              <input id="lossEnabledChk" type="checkbox" ${lossCfg.enabled !== false ? "checked" : ""} />
            </div>
            <div class="pf" style="margin-bottom:0">
              <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">weight</span><span class="pf-desc">该损失项在 total_loss 中的权重</span></div>
              <div class="pf-body"><input id="lossWeightInp" type="number" step="0.05" min="0" value="${lossCfg.weight ?? 1.0}" /></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      html += `<button id="delSelectedBtn" class="danger" style="width:100%">删除此节点 (Del)</button>`;
      box.innerHTML = html;

      bindParamForm(box, node, comp, () => scheduleLiveErc());

      const labelInp = $("nodeLabelInput");
      if (labelInp) {
        labelInp.addEventListener("change", () => {
          const next = labelInp.value.trim();
          if (next === (node.label || "")) return;
          pushUndo();
          node.label = next ? next : null;
          scheduleLiveErc();
        });
      }

      if (lossCfg) {
        $("lossEnabledChk").addEventListener("change", (ev) => {
          pushUndo();
          lossCfg.enabled = ev.target.checked;
          scheduleLiveErc();
        });
        $("lossWeightInp").addEventListener("change", (ev) => {
          pushUndo();
          lossCfg.weight = parseFloat(ev.target.value || "1.0");
          scheduleLiveErc();
        });
      }
      $("delSelectedBtn").addEventListener("click", deleteSelected);
      return;
    }
  }

  // 默认显示全局 Trainer 配置
  const tr = state.draftGraph.trainer;
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title"><span>未选中元件</span><span class="card-title-sub">点击节点可编辑其参数与端口说明</span></div>
      <div class="hint-text">下方为整图训练超参数；节点上的 ✎ 改显示名，⚙ 展开快速参数面板。</div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title"><span>全局训练超参数</span><span class="card-title-sub">Trainer</span></div>
      <div class="pf">
        <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">图名称</span><span class="pf-desc">工作流标题，保存时写入 GraphIR.name</span></div>
        <div class="pf-body"><input id="trGraphName" type="text" value="${escText(state.draftGraph.name)}" /></div>
      </div>
      <div class="pf">
        <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">优化器</span><span class="pf-desc">adam 通吃 / adamw 带解耦权重衰减 / sgd 经典</span></div>
        <div class="pf-body">
          <select id="trOpt">
            <option value="adam" ${tr.optimizer === "adam" ? "selected" : ""}>adam</option>
            <option value="adamw" ${tr.optimizer === "adamw" ? "selected" : ""}>adamw</option>
            <option value="sgd" ${tr.optimizer === "sgd" ? "selected" : ""}>sgd</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="pf">
        <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">学习率 lr</span><span class="pf-desc">步长，分类任务常用 1e-3 起调</span></div>
        <div class="pf-body"><input id="trLr" type="number" step="0.0005" value="${tr.lr}" /></div>
      </div>
      <div class="pf">
        <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">批大小</span><span class="pf-desc">每个 step 送入的样本数</span></div>
        <div class="pf-body"><input id="trBs" type="number" step="16" value="${tr.batch_size}" /></div>
      </div>
      <div class="pf" style="margin-bottom:0">
        <div class="pf-head"><span class="pf-name">训练轮数</span><span class="pf-desc">完整遍历数据集的次数</span></div>
        <div class="pf-body"><input id="trEp" type="number" step="1" value="${tr.epochs}" /></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  $("trGraphName").addEventListener("change", (e) => { pushUndo(); state.draftGraph.name = e.target.value; });
  $("trOpt").addEventListener("change", (e) => { pushUndo(); tr.optimizer = e.target.value; });
  $("trLr").addEventListener("change", (e) => { pushUndo(); tr.lr = parseFloat(e.target.value); });
  $("trBs").addEventListener("change", (e) => { pushUndo(); tr.batch_size = parseInt(e.target.value, 10); });
  $("trEp").addEventListener("change", (e) => { pushUndo(); tr.epochs = parseInt(e.target.value, 10); });
}

function renderMinimap() {
  const mm = $("minimap");
  const keys = Object.keys(state.positions);
  if (!keys.length) { mm.innerHTML = ""; return; }
  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const id of keys) {
    const p = state.positions[id];
    minX = Math.min(minX, p.x); minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
    maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x + NODE_W); maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y + 130);
  }
  const pad = 80;
  minX -= pad; minY -= pad; maxX += pad; maxY += pad;
  const W = 156, H = 104;
  const s = Math.min(W / Math.max(1, maxX - minX), H / Math.max(1, maxY - minY));
  let html = "";
  for (const n of (state.viewData?.nodes || [])) {
    const p = state.positions[n.id];
    if (!p) continue;
    const rx = (p.x - minX) * s;
    const ry = (p.y - minY) * s;
    const rw = Math.max(6, NODE_W * s);
    const rh = Math.max(4, 100 * s);
    const col = n.category === "source" ? "#3b82f6" : n.category === "loss" ? "#f43f5e" : n.category === "backbone" ? "#10b981" : n.category === "output" ? "#06b6d4" : "#a855f7";
    html += `<rect x="${rx.toFixed(1)}" y="${ry.toFixed(1)}" width="${rw.toFixed(1)}" height="${rh.toFixed(1)}" fill="${col}" fill-opacity="0.7" rx="1.5" />`;
  }
  const wrap = $("canvasWrap");
  const vx = (-state.pan.x / state.scale - minX) * s;
  const vy = (-state.pan.y / state.scale - minY) * s;
  const vw = (wrap.clientWidth / state.scale) * s;
  const vh = (wrap.clientHeight / state.scale) * s;
  html += `<rect x="${vx.toFixed(1)}" y="${vy.toFixed(1)}" width="${vw.toFixed(1)}" height="${vh.toFixed(1)}" fill="none" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1" />`;
  mm.innerHTML = html;
}

function fitView() {
  const keys = Object.keys(state.positions);
  if (!keys.length) return;
  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const id of keys) {
    const p = state.positions[id];
    const h = state.nodeEls[id]?.offsetHeight || 130;
    minX = Math.min(minX, p.x); minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
    maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x + NODE_W); maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y + h);
  }
  const wrap = $("canvasWrap");
  const pad = 48;
  const availW = wrap.clientWidth - pad * 2;
  const availH = wrap.clientHeight - pad * 2;
  const contentW = Math.max(200, maxX - minX);
  const contentH = Math.max(200, maxY - minY);
  const s = Math.min(1.15, Math.max(0.32, Math.min(availW / contentW, availH / contentH)));
  state.scale = s;
  state.pan.x = pad - minX * s + (availW - contentW * s) / 2;
  state.pan.y = pad - minY * s + (availH - contentH * s) / 2;
  applyTransform();
}

function emitEdgePulses(nodeStats, tapStats) {
  const now = performance.now();
  if (now - state.lastPulseTs < 55) return;
  state.lastPulseTs = now;
  const pulsesGroup = $("pulsesGroup");
  const delay = Number($("speedSlider").value);
  const duration = Math.max(180, Math.min(650, delay > 0 ? delay * 0.85 : 220));

  for (const item of state.edgeMeta) {
    const { edge, path, color } = item;
    const isTap = edge.edge_kind === "tap";
    const active = isTap ? Boolean(tapStats && tapStats[`${edge.src_node}.${edge.src_port}`]) : Boolean(nodeStats && nodeStats[edge.src_node]);
    if (!active) continue;
    const len = path.getTotalLength();
    if (!len || len < 5) continue;

    const dot = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "circle");
    dot.setAttribute("r", isTap ? "4.5" : "5");
    dot.setAttribute("fill", color);
    dot.setAttribute("filter", "url(#glow)");
    pulsesGroup.appendChild(dot);

    const t0 = performance.now();
    const tick = (t) => {
      const p = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / duration);
      const pt = path.getPointAtLength(len * p);
      dot.setAttribute("cx", pt.x);
      dot.setAttribute("cy", pt.y);
      dot.setAttribute("opacity", String(1 - p * 0.35));
      if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      else dot.remove();
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
}

function updateNodeLiveStats(nodeStats, tapStats) {
  for (const [nid, card] of Object.entries(state.nodeEls)) {
    const ports = nodeStats?.[nid];
    if (!ports) continue;
    const firstKey = Object.keys(ports)[0];
    const st = firstKey ? ports[firstKey] : null;
    if (!st) continue;
    card.classList.add("active");
    setTimeout(() => card.classList.remove("active"), 180);
    const liveText = card.querySelector(".live-text");
    const liveBar = card.querySelector(".live-bar-fill");
    if (liveText) liveText.textContent = `norm=${st.norm.toFixed(2)} μ=${st.mean.toFixed(2)} σ=${st.std.toFixed(2)}`;
    if (liveBar) {
      const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(8, Math.log1p(st.norm) * 24));
      liveBar.style.width = `${pct}%`;
    }
  }
  emitEdgePulses(nodeStats, tapStats);
}

// 调色板映射 (0..255 -> [r, g, b])
const COLORMAPS = {
  viridis: [[68,1,84],[72,40,120],[62,73,137],[49,104,142],[38,130,142],[31,158,137],[53,183,121],[110,206,88],[181,222,43],[253,231,37]],
  coolwarm: [[59,76,192],[98,130,234],[141,176,254],[184,208,249],[221,221,221],[245,196,173],[244,154,123],[222,96,77],[180,4,38]],
  magma: [[0,0,4],[28,16,68],[79,18,123],[129,37,129],[181,54,122],[229,80,100],[251,135,97],[254,194,135],[252,253,191]],
  grayscale: [[0,0,0],[255,255,255]],
};

function sampleColormap(name, v255) {
  const stops = COLORMAPS[name] || COLORMAPS.viridis;
  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v255 / 255));
  const scaled = t * (stops.length - 1);
  const idx = Math.min(stops.length - 2, Math.floor(scaled));
  const frac = scaled - idx;
  const c0 = stops[idx], c1 = stops[idx + 1];
  return [
    Math.round(c0[0] + (c1[0] - c0[0]) * frac),
    Math.round(c0[1] + (c1[1] - c0[1]) * frac),
    Math.round(c0[2] + (c1[2] - c0[2]) * frac),
  ];
}

function drawHeatmapCanvas(canvas, heatmap) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  if (!heatmap || !heatmap.rows || !heatmap.cols) return;

  const { rows, cols, data, colormap, vmin, vmax } = heatmap;
  const off = document.createElement("canvas");
  off.width = cols;
  off.height = rows;
  const octx = off.getContext("2d");
  const img = octx.createImageData(cols, rows);
  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    const [r, g, b] = sampleColormap(colormap, data[i]);
    const p = i * 4;
    img.data[p] = r; img.data[p + 1] = g; img.data[p + 2] = b; img.data[p + 3] = 255;
  }
  octx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);

  const barH = 16;
  const plotH = H - barH - 4;
  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  ctx.drawImage(off, 0, 0, W, plotH);

  // 底部色标条
  const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(6, 0, W - 6, 0);
  const stops = COLORMAPS[colormap] || COLORMAPS.viridis;
  stops.forEach((rgb, idx) => grad.addColorStop(idx / (stops.length - 1), `rgb(${rgb[0]},${rgb[1]},${rgb[2]})`));
  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.fillRect(6, plotH + 3, W - 12, 5);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
  ctx.font = "9px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(vmin.toFixed(2), 6, plotH + 9);
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillText(vmax.toFixed(2), W - 6, plotH + 9);
  canvas._heatMeta = { rows, cols, data, vmin, vmax, plotH };
}

function drawScalarCanvas(canvas, probe, histObj) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  canvas._heatMeta = null;

  const vals = histObj?.values || [];
  const emas = histObj?.ema || [];
  if (!vals.length) return;

  let minY = Math.min(...vals), maxY = Math.max(...vals);
  if (minY === maxY) { minY -= 0.5; maxY += 0.5; }
  const pad = (maxY - minY) * 0.1;
  minY -= pad; maxY += pad;

  const mL = 8, mR = 8, mT = 26, mB = 14;
  const pw = W - mL - mR, ph = H - mT - mB;
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#1e293b";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let g = 0; g <= 2; g++) {
    const gy = mT + (ph * g) / 2;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(mL, gy); ctx.lineTo(W - mR, gy); ctx.stroke();
  }

  const mapX = (i) => vals.length === 1 ? mL + pw / 2 : mL + (i / (vals.length - 1)) * pw;
  const mapY = (v) => mT + ph - ((v - minY) / Math.max(1e-8, maxY - minY)) * ph;

  ctx.strokeStyle = "#22d3ee";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
  ctx.beginPath();
  vals.forEach((v, i) => {
    const x = mapX(i), y = mapY(v);
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
  });
  ctx.stroke();

  if (emas.length >= 2 && (probe.ema_alpha || 0) > 0) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.setLineDash([4, 3]);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#fbbf24";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    emas.forEach((v, i) => {
      const x = mapX(i), y = mapY(v);
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    });
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = "#e2e8f0";
  ctx.font = "bold 14px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  ctx.fillText(Number(probe.scalar).toFixed(4), 8, 5);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
  ctx.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillText(`${probe.reduce || "val"} · n=${vals.length}`, W - 8, 7);
}

function drawImagesCanvas(canvas, images) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  canvas._heatMeta = null;
  if (!images || !images.samples || !images.samples.length) return;

  const { count, channels, height, width, samples } = images;
  const cols = count === 1 ? 1 : 2;
  const rows = Math.ceil(count / cols);
  const gap = 4;
  const cellW = Math.floor((W - gap * (cols + 1)) / cols);
  const cellH = Math.floor((H - gap * (rows + 1)) / rows);
  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;

  samples.forEach((flat, idx) => {
    const off = document.createElement("canvas");
    off.width = width;
    off.height = height;
    const octx = off.getContext("2d");
    const img = octx.createImageData(width, height);
    for (let i = 0; i < width * height; i++) {
      const p = i * 4;
      if (channels === 3) {
        img.data[p] = flat[i * 3]; img.data[p + 1] = flat[i * 3 + 1]; img.data[p + 2] = flat[i * 3 + 2];
      } else {
        const v = flat[i];
        img.data[p] = v; img.data[p + 1] = v; img.data[p + 2] = v;
      }
      img.data[p + 3] = 255;
    }
    octx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
    const gx = idx % cols, gy = Math.floor(idx / cols);
    const scale = Math.min(cellW / width, cellH / height);
    const dw = Math.max(1, Math.floor(width * scale));
    const dh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(height * scale));
    const dx = gap + gx * (cellW + gap) + Math.floor((cellW - dw) / 2);
    const dy = gap + gy * (cellH + gap) + Math.floor((cellH - dh) / 2);
    ctx.drawImage(off, dx, dy, dw, dh);
  });
}

function drawHistogramCanvas(canvas, hist) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  canvas._heatMeta = null;
  if (!hist || !hist.counts || !hist.counts.length) return;
  const counts = hist.counts;
  const maxC = Math.max(1, ...counts);
  const mL = 8, mR = 8, mT = 10, mB = 18;
  const bw = (W - mL - mR) / counts.length;
  const ph = H - mT - mB;
  counts.forEach((c, idx) => {
    const bh = (c / maxC) * ph;
    ctx.fillStyle = "#06b6d4";
    ctx.fillRect(mL + idx * bw + 1, mT + ph - bh, Math.max(2, bw - 2), bh);
  });
  ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
  ctx.font = "9px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(hist.vmin.toFixed(3), 6, H - 2);
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillText(hist.vmax.toFixed(3), W - 6, H - 2);
}

function renderRawTableHtml(raw) {
  if (!raw || !raw.values || !raw.values.length) return `<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">无数据</div>`;
  const rowsHtml = raw.values.map((row) =>
    `<tr>${row.map((v) => `<td>${v}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`
  ).join("");
  return `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:3px;font-family:ui-monospace,monospace">显示 ${raw.rows}×${raw.cols} / 原始 ${raw.total_rows}×${raw.total_cols}</div><table class="probe-raw-table">${rowsHtml}</table>`;
}

const GATE_COLORS = {
  i: "#38bdf8",
  f: "#fbbf24",
  o: "#f472b6",
  c: "#34d399",
};

function drawGateDynamicsCanvas(canvas, probe) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  canvas._heatMeta = null;
  canvas._trajMeta = null;
  canvas._energyMeta = null;

  const gd = probe.gate_dynamics;
  if (!gd || !gd.gates || !Object.keys(gd.gates).length) {
    canvas._gateMeta = null;
    return;
  }

  const gates = gd.gates;
  const keys = ["i", "f", "o", "c"].filter((k) => gates[k]);
  const steps = Math.max(1, gd.steps || 1);

  const mL = 16, mR = 8, mT = 18, mB = 14;
  const stripRows = gd.strip_rows || [];
  const hasStrip = stripRows.length > 0;
  const stripH = hasStrip ? Math.min(Math.floor(H * 0.34), Math.max(26, stripRows.length * 4 + 8)) : 0;
  const curveH = Math.max(30, H - mT - mB - stripH - (hasStrip ? 8 : 0));
  const pw = Math.max(20, W - mL - mR);

  // 背景参考线 0 / 0.5 / 1
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#1e293b";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  [0, 0.5, 1].forEach((lv) => {
    const gy = mT + curveH * (1 - lv);
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (lv === 0.5) ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
    else ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.moveTo(mL, gy);
    ctx.lineTo(W - mR, gy);
    ctx.stroke();
  });
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  const mapX = (t) => (steps === 1 ? mL + pw / 2 : mL + (t / (steps - 1)) * pw);

  // c 单独归一化到 [0, 1] 以便叠加观察趋势，i/f/o 直接按 [0, 1] 物理值绘制
  let cMin = -1, cMax = 1;
  if (gates.c) {
    const vals = gates.c.mean;
    cMin = Math.min(...vals);
    cMax = Math.max(...vals);
    if (Math.abs(cMax - cMin) < 1e-5) { cMin -= 0.5; cMax += 0.5; }
  }

  keys.forEach((k) => {
    const arr = gates[k].mean || [];
    if (!arr.length) return;
    ctx.strokeStyle = GATE_COLORS[k] || "#94a3b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = k === "f" ? 2.0 : 1.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    arr.forEach((v, idx) => {
      const normY = k === "c" ? (v - cMin) / Math.max(1e-6, cMax - cMin) : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
      const x = mapX(idx);
      const y = mT + curveH * (1 - normY);
      if (idx === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    });
    ctx.stroke();
  });

  // 顶部图例与饱和度提示
  let lx = mL;
  ctx.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  keys.forEach((k) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = GATE_COLORS[k] || "#e2e8f0";
    const g = gates[k];
    const satPct = k === "c"
      ? `|c|=${Number(g.abs_mean).toFixed(2)}`
      : `${k}:${Math.round((g.sat_low + g.sat_high) * 100)}%sat`;
    ctx.fillText(satPct, lx, 3);
    lx += ctx.measureText(satPct).width + 8;
  });
  ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillText(`L=${gd.valid_len}/${gd.total_len}`, W - mR, 3);

  // 下方代表神经元门控时序条带热图
  if (hasStrip) {
    const sy = mT + curveH + 6;
    const rows = stripRows.length;
    const rowH = Math.max(1, stripH / rows);
    const colW = pw / steps;
    const labels = gd.strip_labels || [];
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const rowData = stripRows[r];
      const ry = sy + r * rowH;
      if (labels[r]) {
        ctx.fillStyle = GATE_COLORS[labels[r]] || "#94a3b8";
        ctx.font = "9px ui-monospace, monospace";
        ctx.textAlign = "left";
        ctx.textBaseline = "top";
        ctx.fillText(labels[r], 3, ry);
      }
      for (let c = 0; c < rowData.length; c++) {
        const [cr, cg, cb] = sampleColormap("viridis", rowData[c]);
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${cr},${cg},${cb})`;
        ctx.fillRect(mL + c * colW, ry, Math.ceil(colW), Math.ceil(rowH));
      }
    }
  }

  canvas._gateMeta = { mL, pw, steps, gates, keys };
}

function drawTrajectoryCanvas(canvas, probe) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  canvas._heatMeta = null;
  canvas._gateMeta = null;
  canvas._energyMeta = null;

  const traj = probe.trajectory;
  if (!traj || !traj.points || !traj.points.length) {
    canvas._trajMeta = null;
    return;
  }

  const pts = traj.points;
  const mL = 12, mR = 12, mT = 20, mB = 14;
  const pw = W - mL - mR, ph = H - mT - mB;

  let xmin = traj.xmin, xmax = traj.xmax, ymin = traj.ymin, ymax = traj.ymax;
  if (Math.abs(xmax - xmin) < 1e-5) { xmin -= 0.5; xmax += 0.5; }
  if (Math.abs(ymax - ymin) < 1e-5) { ymin -= 0.5; ymax += 0.5; }
  const padX = (xmax - xmin) * 0.14;
  const padY = (ymax - ymin) * 0.14;
  xmin -= padX; xmax += padX; ymin -= padY; ymax += padY;

  const mapX = (x) => mL + ((x - xmin) / Math.max(1e-6, xmax - xmin)) * pw;
  const mapY = (y) => mT + ph - ((y - ymin) / Math.max(1e-6, ymax - ymin)) * ph;

  // 网格与零轴
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#172033";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.strokeRect(mL, mT, pw, ph);
  if (xmin < 0 && xmax > 0) {
    const zx = mapX(0);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]); ctx.moveTo(zx, mT); ctx.lineTo(zx, mT + ph); ctx.stroke();
  }
  if (ymin < 0 && ymax > 0) {
    const zy = mapY(0);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]); ctx.moveTo(mL, zy); ctx.lineTo(mL + pw, zy); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  const mapped = pts.map(([px, py]) => [mapX(px), mapY(py)]);

  // 渐变轨迹线 (cyan -> amber -> pink)
  for (let i = 0; i < mapped.length - 1; i++) {
    const frac = i / Math.max(1, mapped.length - 2);
    const r = Math.round(56 + (244 - 56) * frac);
    const g = Math.round(189 + (114 - 189) * frac);
    const b = Math.round(248 + (182 - 248) * frac);
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.0;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(mapped[i][0], mapped[i][1]);
    ctx.lineTo(mapped[i + 1][0], mapped[i + 1][1]);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // 节点与起点/终点标记
  mapped.forEach(([cx, cy], idx) => {
    const isStart = idx === 0;
    const isEnd = idx === mapped.length - 1;
    ctx.fillStyle = isStart ? "#34d399" : (isEnd ? "#f472b6" : "rgba(226,232,240,0.65)");
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, isStart || isEnd ? 4.2 : 2.0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  });

  if (mapped.length >= 1) {
    ctx.font = "bold 9px ui-monospace, monospace";
    ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#34d399";
    ctx.fillText("S", mapped[0][0] + 5, mapped[0][1] - 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#f472b6";
    const last = mapped[mapped.length - 1];
    ctx.fillText("E", last[0] + 5, last[1] - 2);
  }

  // 顶部指标
  ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
  ctx.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  const evrTxt = traj.method === "pca" ? `PCA ${(traj.explained_var * 100).toFixed(0)}%` : "first2";
  ctx.fillText(`${evrTxt} · len=${Number(traj.path_len).toFixed(2)}`, mL, 4);
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillText(`n=${pts.length}`, W - mR, 4);

  canvas._trajMeta = { mapped, pts };
}

function drawStepEnergyCanvas(canvas, probe) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  canvas._heatMeta = null;
  canvas._gateMeta = null;
  canvas._trajMeta = null;

  const se = probe.step_energy;
  if (!se || !se.energy || !se.energy.length) {
    canvas._energyMeta = null;
    return;
  }

  const energy = se.energy;
  const delta = se.delta || [];
  const n = energy.length;
  const maxVal = Math.max(1e-5, ...energy, ...delta);

  const mL = 8, mR = 8, mT = 20, mB = 14;
  const pw = W - mL - mR, ph = H - mT - mB;
  const bw = pw / n;

  // 背景基线
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#1e293b";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(mL, mT + ph); ctx.lineTo(W - mR, mT + ph); ctx.stroke();

  // 能量柱状图
  energy.forEach((val, idx) => {
    const bh = (val / maxVal) * ph;
    const isPeak = idx === se.peak_step;
    ctx.fillStyle = isPeak ? "#fbbf24" : "rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.55)";
    ctx.fillRect(mL + idx * bw + 1, mT + ph - bh, Math.max(2, bw - 2), bh);
  });

  // 步间跃迁折线 ||h_t - h_{t-1}||
  if (delta.length >= 2) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#f472b6";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
    ctx.beginPath();
    delta.forEach((dv, idx) => {
      const x = mL + (idx + 0.5) * bw;
      const y = mT + ph - (dv / maxVal) * ph;
      if (idx === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    });
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  ctx.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillStyle = "#fbbf24";
  ctx.fillText(`t*=${se.peak_step} (${Number(se.peak_val).toFixed(2)})`, mL, 4);
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
  ctx.fillText(`decay=${Number(se.decay_ratio).toFixed(2)} · L=${se.valid_len}/${se.total_len}`, W - mR, 4);

  canvas._energyMeta = { mL, bw, n, energy, delta };
}

function renderProbeIntoTarget(canvas, rawWrap, probe, histObj, forcedView) {
  let view = forcedView && forcedView !== "auto" ? forcedView : probe.view;
  if (view === "image" && !probe.images) view = "heatmap";
  if (view === "gate_dynamics" && !probe.gate_dynamics) view = probe.view;
  if (view === "trajectory" && !probe.trajectory) view = probe.view;
  if (view === "step_energy" && !probe.step_energy) view = probe.view;

  if (view === "raw") {
    canvas.style.display = "none";
    rawWrap.style.display = "block";
    rawWrap.innerHTML = renderRawTableHtml(probe.raw);
  } else {
    canvas.style.display = "block";
    rawWrap.style.display = "none";
    if (view === "gate_dynamics") drawGateDynamicsCanvas(canvas, probe);
    else if (view === "trajectory") drawTrajectoryCanvas(canvas, probe);
    else if (view === "step_energy") drawStepEnergyCanvas(canvas, probe);
