    else if (view === "scalar") drawScalarCanvas(canvas, probe, histObj);
    else if (view === "image") drawImagesCanvas(canvas, probe.images);
    else if (view === "hist") drawHistogramCanvas(canvas, probe.hist);
    else drawHeatmapCanvas(canvas, probe.heatmap);
  }
}

function renderProbeIntoCard(card, nid, probe) {
  const probeBox = card.querySelector(".node-probe");
  if (!probeBox || !probe) return;
  const tag = probeBox.querySelector(".probe-tag");
  const shapeTxt = probeBox.querySelector(".probe-shape-txt");
  const meta = probeBox.querySelector(".probe-meta");
  const canvas = probeBox.querySelector(".probe-canvas");
  const rawWrap = probeBox.querySelector(".probe-raw-wrap");
  if (tag) tag.textContent = probe.view;
  if (shapeTxt) shapeTxt.textContent = `(${probe.shape.join(",")}) ${probe.dtype}`;
  if (meta && probe.stats) {
    meta.innerHTML = `<span>min ${probe.stats.min.toFixed(2)}</span><span>max ${probe.stats.max.toFixed(2)}</span><span>μ ${probe.stats.mean.toFixed(2)}</span>`;
  }
  renderProbeIntoTarget(canvas, rawWrap, probe, state.probeHistory[nid], "auto");
}

function handleProbeHover(ev, nid, canvas, hoverEl) {
  if (!hoverEl) return;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = (ev.clientX - rect.left) * (canvas.width / Math.max(1, rect.width));
  const y = (ev.clientY - rect.top) * (canvas.height / Math.max(1, rect.height));

  if (canvas._gateMeta) {
    const gm = canvas._gateMeta;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(gm.steps - 1, Math.round(((x - gm.mL) / gm.pw) * Math.max(1, gm.steps - 1))));
    const parts = gm.keys.map((k) => `${k}=${Number(gm.gates[k].mean[t] ?? 0).toFixed(3)}`);
    hoverEl.textContent = `t=${t}: ${parts.join("  ")}`;
    return;
  }
  if (canvas._trajMeta) {
    const tm = canvas._trajMeta;
    let bestIdx = 0, bestD = Infinity;
    tm.mapped.forEach(([mx, my], idx) => {
      const d = (mx - x) ** 2 + (my - y) ** 2;
      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; bestIdx = idx; }
    });
    const pt = tm.pts[bestIdx];
    hoverEl.textContent = `t=${bestIdx}: (${pt[0].toFixed(3)}, ${pt[1].toFixed(3)})`;
    return;
  }
  if (canvas._energyMeta) {
    const em = canvas._energyMeta;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(em.n - 1, Math.floor((x - em.mL) / Math.max(1e-5, em.bw))));
    hoverEl.textContent = `t=${t}: E=${Number(em.energy[t] ?? 0).toFixed(3)} · Δ=${Number(em.delta[t] ?? 0).toFixed(3)}`;
    return;
  }

  const hm = canvas._heatMeta;
  if (!hm) return;
  if (y < 0 || y >= hm.plotH) return;
  const c = Math.max(0, Math.min(hm.cols - 1, Math.floor((x / canvas.width) * hm.cols)));
  const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(hm.rows - 1, Math.floor((y / hm.plotH) * hm.rows)));
  const q = hm.data[r * hm.cols + c];
  const val = hm.vmin + (q / 255) * (hm.vmax - hm.vmin);
  hoverEl.textContent = `[${r}, ${c}] ≈ ${val.toFixed(4)}`;
}

function openProbeModal(nid) {
  state.modalProbeId = nid;
  state.modalProbeTab = "auto";
  $("probeModalTabs").querySelectorAll(".probe-modal-tab").forEach((b) => {
    b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.view === "auto");
  });
  $("probeModal").classList.add("open");
  refreshProbeModal();
}

function closeProbeModal() {
  state.modalProbeId = null;
  $("probeModal").classList.remove("open");
}

function refreshProbeModal() {
  if (!state.modalProbeId) return;
  const nid = state.modalProbeId;
  const probe = state.latestProbes[nid];
  $("probeModalTitle").textContent = `输出监视器 · ${nid}`;
  if (!probe) {
    $("probeModalShape").textContent = "等待训练数据...";
    $("probeModalStats").textContent = "-";
    return;
  }
  $("probeModalTabs").querySelectorAll(".probe-modal-tab").forEach((b) => {
    const v = b.dataset.view;
    if (v === "gate_dynamics") b.style.display = probe.gate_dynamics ? "" : "none";
    else if (v === "trajectory") b.style.display = probe.trajectory ? "" : "none";
    else if (v === "step_energy") b.style.display = probe.step_energy ? "" : "none";
    else if (v === "heatmap" || v === "scalar" || v === "image" || v === "raw" || v === "hist") {
      b.style.display = (probe.heatmap || probe.raw || probe.hist) ? "" : "none";
    }
  });
  $("probeModalShape").textContent = `shape=(${probe.shape.join(",")}) · ${probe.dtype} · layout=${probe.layout}`;
  const st = probe.stats;
  $("probeModalStats").textContent = `min=${st.min}  max=${st.max}  mean=${st.mean}  std=${st.std}  norm=${st.norm}`;
  renderProbeIntoTarget(
    $("probeModalCanvas"),
    $("probeModalRaw"),
    probe,
    state.probeHistory[nid],
    state.modalProbeTab,
  );
}

function updateProbes(probes) {
  if (!probes) return;
  for (const [nid, snap] of Object.entries(probes)) {
    state.latestProbes[nid] = snap;
    let ph = state.probeHistory[nid];
    if (!ph) {
      ph = { values: [], ema: [] };
      state.probeHistory[nid] = ph;
    }
    const val = Number(snap.scalar ?? 0);
    ph.values.push(val);
    const alpha = Number(snap.ema_alpha ?? 0.1);
    const prevEma = ph.ema.length ? ph.ema[ph.ema.length - 1] : val;
    ph.ema.push(alpha > 0 ? prevEma * (1 - alpha) + val * alpha : val);
    const maxLen = Math.max(20, Number(snap.history_len ?? 120));
    if (ph.values.length > maxLen) { ph.values.shift(); ph.ema.shift(); }

    const card = state.nodeEls[nid];
    if (card) {
      card.classList.add("active");
      setTimeout(() => card.classList.remove("active"), 180);
      renderProbeIntoCard(card, nid, snap);
    }
  }
  if (state.modalProbeId && probes[state.modalProbeId]) {
    refreshProbeModal();
  }
}

function renderLossItems(lossDict) {
  const container = $("lossItems");
  if (!state.draftGraph) return;
  const weights = {};
  for (const lc of state.draftGraph.losses || []) weights[lc.node_id] = lc.weight;
  if (!lossDict) {
    const entries = Object.entries(weights);
    container.innerHTML = entries.length
      ? entries.map(([k, w]) => `<div class="loss-row"><span>${k} (w=${w})</span><span style="color:var(--muted)">-</span></div>`).join("")
      : `<div style="color:var(--muted)">无 Loss 节点</div>`;
    return;
  }
  container.innerHTML = Object.entries(lossDict).map(([k, v]) => {
    const w = weights[k] !== undefined ? weights[k] : 1.0;
    return `<div class="loss-row"><span>${k} <small style="color:var(--muted)">(w=${w})</small></span><span>${Number(v).toFixed(4)}</span></div>`;
  }).join("");
}

function renderLossChart() {
  const svg = $("lossChart");
  const legend = $("chartLegend");
  const pts = state.history;
  if (!pts.length) { svg.innerHTML = ""; legend.innerHTML = ""; $("chartRange").textContent = "-"; return; }
  const keys = ["total_loss", ...Object.keys(state.lossSeries)];
  let minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const p of pts) {
    minY = Math.min(minY, p.total_loss);
    maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.total_loss);
    for (const k of Object.keys(state.lossSeries)) {
      const v = p.loss_dict?.[k];
      if (v !== undefined) { minY = Math.min(minY, v); maxY = Math.max(maxY, v); }
    }
  }
  if (!isFinite(minY) || minY === maxY) { minY = 0; maxY = Math.max(1, maxY + 0.1); }
  const padY = (maxY - minY) * 0.08;
  minY = Math.max(0, minY - padY); maxY = maxY + padY;
  $("chartRange").textContent = `${minY.toFixed(3)} ~ ${maxY.toFixed(3)}`;

  const W = 320, H = 175, M = 8;
  const mapX = (i) => pts.length === 1 ? W / 2 : M + (i / (pts.length - 1)) * (W - M * 2);
  const mapY = (v) => H - M - ((v - minY) / Math.max(1e-6, maxY - minY)) * (H - M * 2);
  let html = "";
  for (let g = 1; g <= 3; g++) {
    const gy = M + ((H - M * 2) * g) / 4;
    html += `<line x1="0" y1="${gy}" x2="${W}" y2="${gy}" stroke="#1f2937" stroke-width="1" />`;
  }
  legend.innerHTML = "";
  keys.forEach((k, idx) => {
    const color = PALETTE[idx % PALETTE.length];
    const coords = [];
    pts.forEach((p, i) => {
      const v = k === "total_loss" ? p.total_loss : p.loss_dict?.[k];
      if (v !== undefined) coords.push(`${mapX(i).toFixed(1)},${mapY(v).toFixed(1)}`);
    });
    if (coords.length >= 2) {
      html += `<polyline fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="${k === 'total_loss' ? 2.2 : 1.5}" points="${coords.join(" ")}" />`;
    }
    legend.innerHTML += `<span><i class="legend-dot" style="background:${color}"></i>${k}</span>`;
  });
  svg.innerHTML = html;
}

function setTrainButtons(running, paused) {
  state.running = running; state.paused = paused;
  $("startBtn").disabled = running;
  $("pauseBtn").disabled = !running;
  $("stopBtn").disabled = !running;
  $("pauseBtn").textContent = paused ? "继续" : "暂停";
}

function confirmDiscardIfDirty() {
  if (!state.dirty) return true;
  return confirm("当前画布有未保存更改，确定切换并放弃当前修改吗？");
}

function updateDeleteButton() {
  $("deleteBtn").disabled = !state.currentFile;
}

async function saveGraph(targetName) {
  if (!state.draftGraph || !targetName) return;
  const res = await fetch("/api/graph/save", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name: targetName, graph: state.draftGraph }),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) { alert(data.error || "保存失败"); return; }
  state.currentFile = data.name;
  setDirty(false);
  await refreshGraphSelect(data.name);
  updateDeleteButton();
}

async function refreshGraphSelect(selectName) {
  const res = await fetch("/api/graphs");
  const data = await res.json();
  const sel = $("graphSelect");
  const opts = data.graphs.map((g) => `<option value="${g}">${g}</option>`);
  if (!selectName || !data.graphs.includes(selectName)) {
    sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- 草稿/模板 (未保存) --</option>` + opts.join("");
    sel.value = "";
  } else {
    sel.innerHTML = opts.join("");
    sel.value = selectName;
  }
  updateDeleteButton();
  return data;
}

async function newWorkflow() {
  if (!confirmDiscardIfDirty()) return;
  state.currentFile = "";
  state.selectedNodeId = null;
  state.selectedEdgeId = null;
  state.history = []; state.lossSeries = {};
  state.probeHistory = {}; state.latestProbes = {};
  state.draftGraph = {
    name: "untitled_workflow",
    nodes: [],
    edges: [],
    losses: [],
    trainer: { optimizer: "adam", lr: 0.001, weight_decay: 0.0, batch_size: 64, epochs: 5, grad_clip: 1.0, device: "cpu", seed: 42, eval_every_steps: 50 },
  };
  state.undoStack = [];
  state.redoStack = [];
  updateHistoryButtons();
  await refreshGraphSelect(null);
  await runLiveErc(false);
  setDirty(false);
}

async function deleteCurrentGraph() {
  if (!state.currentFile) return;
  if (!confirm(`确定要从服务端删除工作流文件 "${state.currentFile}" 吗？`)) return;
  const res = await fetch("/api/graph/delete", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name: state.currentFile }),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) { alert(data.error || "删除失败"); return; }
  state.currentFile = "";
  setDirty(false);
  const graphsData = await refreshGraphSelect(null);
  if (graphsData.graphs.length > 0) {
    await loadGraph(graphsData.graphs[0]);
  } else {
    await newWorkflow();
  }
}

async function openTemplateModal() {
  const res = await fetch("/api/templates");
  const data = await res.json();
  const list = $("templateList");
  const templates = data.templates || [];
  if (!templates.length) {
    list.innerHTML = `<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">未找到模板文件（templates/ 目录为空）。</div>`;
  } else {
    list.innerHTML = "";
    for (const tpl of templates) {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "template-item";
      const chips = (tpl.node_types || []).slice(0, 6).map((t) => `<span class="template-chip">${t}</span>`).join("");
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div class="template-item-head">
          <span>${tpl.name}</span>
          <small style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:400;font-family:ui-monospace,monospace">${tpl.file}</small>
        </div>
        <div class="template-item-meta">
          <span>${tpl.node_count} 节点 · ${tpl.edge_count} 连线</span>
          ${chips}
        </div>
      `;
      item.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        await loadTemplate(tpl.file);
        closeTemplateModal();
      });
      list.appendChild(item);
    }
  }
  $("templateModal").classList.add("open");
}

function closeTemplateModal() { $("templateModal").classList.remove("open"); }

async function loadTemplate(fileName) {
  if (!confirmDiscardIfDirty()) return;
  const res = await fetch(`/api/template?name=${encodeURIComponent(fileName)}`);
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) { alert(data.error || "加载模板失败"); return; }
  state.currentFile = "";
  state.selectedNodeId = null;
  state.selectedEdgeId = null;
  renderGraph(data, { fit: true, syncDraft: true });
  setDirty(true);
  await refreshGraphSelect(null);
}

async function importLocalFile(file) {
  if (!file) return;
  if (!confirmDiscardIfDirty()) return;
  const text = await file.text();
  const res = await fetch("/api/graph/import", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name: file.name, content: text, save: false }),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) { alert(data.error || "导入失败"); return; }
  state.currentFile = "";
  state.selectedNodeId = null;
  state.selectedEdgeId = null;
  renderGraph(data.graph, { fit: true, syncDraft: true });
  setDirty(true);
  await refreshGraphSelect(null);
}

function openExportModal() {
  if (!state.draftGraph) return;
  const base = (state.currentFile ? state.currentFile.replace(/\.(json|ya?ml)$/i, "") : (state.draftGraph.name || "workflow"));
  $("exportFilenameInput").value = base;
  $("exportModal").classList.add("open");
}

function closeExportModal() { $("exportModal").classList.remove("open"); }

async function exportWorkflow(fmt) {
  if (!state.draftGraph) return;
  const rawName = $("exportFilenameInput").value.trim() || state.draftGraph.name || "workflow";
  const res = await fetch("/api/graph/export", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name: rawName, format: fmt, graph: state.draftGraph }),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) { alert(data.error || "导出失败"); return; }
  const blob = new Blob([data.content], { type: data.mime || "application/json" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = data.filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  closeExportModal();
}

async function initApp() {
  const [compRes, graphsData] = await Promise.all([
    fetch("/api/components").then((r) => r.json()),
    refreshGraphSelect(null),
  ]);
  state.components = compRes.components || {};
  renderPalette();

  const sel = $("graphSelect");
  if (graphsData.status.graph_name && graphsData.graphs.includes(graphsData.status.graph_name)) {
    sel.value = graphsData.status.graph_name;
  } else if (graphsData.graphs.includes("mnist_atomic.json")) {
    sel.value = "mnist_atomic.json";
  } else if (graphsData.graphs.length > 0) {
    sel.value = graphsData.graphs[0];
  }
  setTrainButtons(graphsData.status.running, graphsData.status.paused);
  $("speedSlider").value = String(graphsData.status.step_delay_ms);
  $("speedVal").textContent = `${graphsData.status.step_delay_ms} ms`;
  if (sel.value) await loadGraph(sel.value);
}

async function loadGraph(name) {
  if (!name) return;
  state.currentFile = name;
  state.selectedNodeId = null;
  state.selectedEdgeId = null;
  const res = await fetch(`/api/graph?name=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
  if (!res.ok) return;
  const data = await res.json();
  renderGraph(data, { fit: true, syncDraft: true });
  await refreshGraphSelect(name);
}

function connectWS() {
  const proto = location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
  const ws = new WebSocket(`${proto}//${location.host}/ws`);
  state.ws = ws;
  ws.onopen = () => { $("wsBadge").textContent = "WS 已连接"; $("wsBadge").style.color = "#34d399"; };
  ws.onclose = () => {
    $("wsBadge").textContent = "WS 重连中..."; $("wsBadge").style.color = "#fbbf24";
    setTimeout(connectWS, 1500);
  };
  ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
    const msg = JSON.parse(ev.data);
    if (msg.type === "train_started") {
      state.history = []; state.lossSeries = {};
      state.probeHistory = {}; state.latestProbes = {};
      $("kpiVal").textContent = "-";
      setTrainButtons(true, false);
    } else if (msg.type === "step") {
      $("kpiStep").textContent = `E${msg.epoch}/${msg.epochs} · #${msg.step}`;
      $("kpiLoss").textContent = msg.total_loss.toFixed(4);
      $("kpiBatchAcc").textContent = msg.batch_acc !== null ? `${(msg.batch_acc * 100).toFixed(1)}%` : "-";
      renderLossItems(msg.loss_dict);
      state.history.push(msg);
      if (state.history.length > 200) state.history.shift();
      for (const k of Object.keys(msg.loss_dict || {})) state.lossSeries[k] = true;
      renderLossChart();
      updateNodeLiveStats(msg.node_stats, msg.tap_stats);
      updateProbes(msg.probes);
    } else if (msg.type === "eval") {
      const accStr = msg.val_acc !== null ? `${(msg.val_acc * 100).toFixed(1)}%` : "-";
      $("kpiVal").textContent = `${accStr} / ${msg.val_loss.toFixed(3)}`;
    } else if (msg.type === "train_finished") {
      setTrainButtons(false, false);
    } else if (msg.type === "train_error") {
      setTrainButtons(false, false);
      const div = document.createElement("div");
      div.className = "log-item error";
      div.textContent = `[TRAIN_ERROR] ${msg.message}`;
      $("ercLogs").prepend(div);
    }
  };
}

// 快速搜索弹窗逻辑
function openQuickSearch(clientX, clientY) {
  const wrapRect = $("canvasWrap").getBoundingClientRect();
  state.quickPos = screenToStage(clientX, clientY);
  const qs = $("quickSearch");
  qs.style.left = `${Math.min(wrapRect.width - 270, Math.max(12, clientX - wrapRect.left))}px`;
  qs.style.top = `${Math.min(wrapRect.height - 260, Math.max(12, clientY - wrapRect.top))}px`;
  qs.classList.add("open");
  $("quickSearchInput").value = "";
  renderQuickSearchList("");
  $("quickSearchInput").focus();
}
function closeQuickSearch() { $("quickSearch").classList.remove("open"); }
function renderQuickSearchList(q) {
  const list = $("quickSearchList");
  const lower = q.trim().toLowerCase();
  const matches = Object.values(state.components).filter((c) => {
    if (!lower) return true;
    return [c.type_id, c.display_name, c.title, c.description]
      .filter(Boolean).some((s) => String(s).toLowerCase().includes(lower));
  });
  list.innerHTML = "";
  matches.slice(0, 12).forEach((c, idx) => {
    const item = document.createElement("div");
    item.className = `quick-search-item${idx === 0 ? " focused" : ""}`;
    item.dataset.type = c.type_id;
    item.title = c.description || "";
    item.innerHTML = `<span class="zh-name">${escText(c.title || c.display_name)}</span><small>${escText(c.display_name || c.type_id)}</small>`;
    item.addEventListener("click", () => {
      addNodeAt(c.type_id, state.quickPos.x, state.quickPos.y);
      closeQuickSearch();
    });
    list.appendChild(item);
  });
}

// 画布交互
const wrap = $("canvasWrap");
wrap.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.target.closest(".node-card") || ev.target.closest("#quickSearch") || ev.target.closest("#minimap")) return;
  closeQuickSearch();
  if (state.selectedNodeId || state.selectedEdgeId) {
    state.selectedNodeId = null;
    state.selectedEdgeId = null;
    renderNodeSelection();
    renderEdges();
    renderInspector();
  }
  wrap.classList.add("grabbing");
  const sx = ev.clientX - state.pan.x;
  const sy = ev.clientY - state.pan.y;
  const onMove = (mv) => {
    state.pan.x = mv.clientX - sx;
    state.pan.y = mv.clientY - sy;
    applyTransform();
  };
  const onUp = () => {
    wrap.classList.remove("grabbing");
    window.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
    window.removeEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
  };
  window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
  window.addEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
});

wrap.addEventListener("dblclick", (ev) => {
  if (ev.target.closest(".node-card") || ev.target.closest("#quickSearch")) return;
  openQuickSearch(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
});

wrap.addEventListener("dragover", (ev) => ev.preventDefault());
wrap.addEventListener("drop", async (ev) => {
  ev.preventDefault();
  if (ev.dataTransfer.files && ev.dataTransfer.files.length > 0) {
    const file = ev.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (/\.(json|ya?ml)$/i.test(file.name)) {
      await importLocalFile(file);
    }
    return;
  }
  const typeId = ev.dataTransfer.getData("text/netpulse-type");
  if (!typeId) return;
  const pt = screenToStage(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
  addNodeAt(typeId, pt.x - NODE_W / 2, pt.y - 40);
});

wrap.addEventListener("wheel", (ev) => {
  ev.preventDefault();
  const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
  const mx = ev.clientX - rect.left;
  const my = ev.clientY - rect.top;
  const factor = ev.deltaY < 0 ? 1.08 : 0.92;
  const nextScale = Math.min(2.0, Math.max(0.28, state.scale * factor));
  state.pan.x = mx - ((mx - state.pan.x) / state.scale) * nextScale;
  state.pan.y = my - ((my - state.pan.y) / state.scale) * nextScale;
  state.scale = nextScale;
  applyTransform();
}, { passive: false });

// 快捷键：Delete/Backspace 删节点或边，Ctrl+Z/Y 撤销重做，Ctrl+S 保存
window.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  const tag = document.activeElement?.tagName;
  const editing = tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA" || tag === "SELECT";
  if (ev.key === "Escape") { closeQuickSearch(); closeProbeModal(); closeTemplateModal(); closeExportModal(); return; }
  if ((ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) && ev.key.toLowerCase() === "s") {
    ev.preventDefault();
    const target = state.currentFile || prompt("请输入保存文件名（如 my_net.json）：", `${state.draftGraph?.name || "custom_graph"}.json`);
    if (target) saveGraph(target);
    return;
  }
  if (!editing && (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) && ev.key.toLowerCase() === "z") {
    ev.preventDefault();
    if (ev.shiftKey) redo(); else undo();
    return;
  }
  if (!editing && (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) && ev.key.toLowerCase() === "y") {
    ev.preventDefault();
    redo();
    return;
  }

});

netpulse/webui/runner.py

"""可控速度的后台训练会话，用于向 WebUI 实时推送 Loss 与节点张量流状态。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

from netpulse.core.compiler import CompiledGraph, GraphCompiler
from netpulse.core.ir import GraphIR
from netpulse.core.registry import ComponentRegistry
from netpulse.core.trainer import _build_optimizer, _extract_acc, _to_signal_batch


def _tensor_summary(t: torch.Tensor) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with torch.no_grad():
        tf = t.detach().float()
        if tf.numel() == 0:
            return {"shape": list(t.shape), "mean": 0.0, "std": 0.0, "norm": 0.0}
        return {
            "shape": list(t.shape),
            "mean": round(float(tf.mean().item()), 4),
            "std": round(float(tf.std(unbiased=False).item()), 4) if tf.numel() > 1 else 0.0,
            "norm": round(float(tf.norm().item()), 4),
        }


@dataclass
class LiveSessionConfig:
    step_delay_ms: int = 150
    epochs: Optional[int] = None
    lr: Optional[float] = None


class LiveTrainRunner:
    """单会话后台训练控制器：支持实时调速、暂停/继续、停止与逐节点张量流遥测。"""

    def __init__(self, emit_cb: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None]) -> None:
        self._emit_cb = emit_cb
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
        self._stop_flag = threading.Event()
        self._pause_flag = threading.Event()  # set = paused
        self.step_delay_ms: int = 150
        self.running: bool = False
        self.paused: bool = False
        self.graph_name: Optional[str] = None

    def set_speed(self, step_delay_ms: int, paused: Optional[bool] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.step_delay_ms = max(0, min(3000, int(step_delay_ms)))
        if paused is not None:
            self.paused = bool(paused)
            if self.paused:
                self._pause_flag.set()
            else:
                self._pause_flag.clear()
        return {
            "running": self.running,
            "paused": self.paused,
            "step_delay_ms": self.step_delay_ms,
        }

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop_flag.set()
        self._pause_flag.clear()
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._thread.join(timeout=2.0)
        self.running = False
        self.paused = False

    def start(
        self,
        graph_name: str,
        graph_ir: GraphIR,
        registry: ComponentRegistry,
        cfg: LiveSessionConfig,
    ) -> None:
        self.stop()
        with self._lock:
            self._stop_flag.clear()
            self._pause_flag.clear()
            self.paused = False
            self.step_delay_ms = max(0, min(3000, int(cfg.step_delay_ms)))
            self.graph_name = graph_name
            self.running = True

            # 允许 UI 临时覆盖 epochs / lr
            run_ir = graph_ir.model_copy(deep=True)
            if cfg.epochs is not None and cfg.epochs > 0:
                run_ir.trainer.epochs = int(cfg.epochs)
            if cfg.lr is not None and cfg.lr > 0:
                run_ir.trainer.lr = float(cfg.lr)

            self._thread = threading.Thread(
                target=self._run_loop,
                args=(graph_name, run_ir, registry),
                daemon=True,
            )
            self._thread.start()

    def _run_loop(
        self,
        graph_name: str,
        graph_ir: GraphIR,
        registry: ComponentRegistry,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            compiled: CompiledGraph = GraphCompiler.compile(graph_ir, registry)
            tcfg = graph_ir.trainer
            torch.manual_seed(tcfg.seed)
            device = torch.device(tcfg.device)
            compiled.to(device)

            sources = [nid for nid, cat in compiled.categories.items() if cat == "source"]
            if len(sources) != 1:
                raise ValueError(f"当前仅支持 1 个 source 节点，发现: {sources}")
            source_id = sources[0]
            train_loader, val_loader = compiled.nodes_mod[source_id].build_dataloaders(
                batch_size=tcfg.batch_size,
                seed=tcfg.seed,
            )
            optimizer = _build_optimizer(compiled, tcfg.optimizer, tcfg.lr, tcfg.weight_decay)

            self._emit_cb({
                "type": "train_started",
                "graph_name": graph_name,
                "epochs": tcfg.epochs,
                "step_delay_ms": self.step_delay_ms,
            })

            step = 0
            last_emit_ts = 0.0
            total_steps_est = tcfg.epochs * max(1, len(train_loader))

            for epoch in range(tcfg.epochs):
                if self._stop_flag.is_set():
                    break
                compiled.train()
                for raw_batch in train_loader:
                    while self._pause_flag.is_set() and not self._stop_flag.is_set():
                        time.sleep(0.05)
                    if self._stop_flag.is_set():
                        break

                    batch = _to_signal_batch(source_id, raw_batch, device)
                    optimizer.zero_grad()
                    out = compiled(batch, mode="train")
                    if out.total_loss is None:
                        raise RuntimeError("图未产生任何 Loss")
                    out.total_loss.backward()
                    if tcfg.grad_clip is not None:
                        nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(compiled.parameters(), tcfg.grad_clip)
                    optimizer.step()
                    step += 1

                    now = time.monotonic()
                    # 当全速模式(0ms)时节流至 ~20fps 以免淹没浏览器 WS；有延迟时每步都推送
                    should_emit = (
                        self.step_delay_ms > 0
                        or step == 1
                        or (now - last_emit_ts) >= 0.05
                    )
                    if should_emit:
                        last_emit_ts = now
                        node_stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
                        for nid, ports in out.outputs.items():
                            port_stats = {}
                            for pname, sig in ports.items():
                                port_stats[pname] = _tensor_summary(sig.data)
                            node_stats[nid] = port_stats

                        tap_stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
                        for tkey, tsig in out.tap_bus.items():
                            tap_stats[tkey] = _tensor_summary(tsig.data)

                        probes: dict[str, Any] = {}
                        for nid, cat in compiled.categories.items():
                            if cat == "output":
                                mod = compiled.nodes_mod[nid]
                                cap = getattr(mod, "capture_snapshot", None)
                                if callable(cap):
                                    snap = cap()
                                    if snap is not None:
                                        probes[nid] = snap

                        loss_dict = {k: round(float(v.item()), 5) for k, v in out.loss_dict.items()}
                        acc_pair = _extract_acc(compiled, out.outputs, batch, source_id)
                        batch_acc = round(acc_pair[0] / acc_pair[1], 4) if acc_pair else None

                        self._emit_cb({
                            "type": "step",
                            "step": step,
                            "total_steps_est": total_steps_est,
                            "epoch": epoch + 1,
                            "epochs": tcfg.epochs,
                            "total_loss": round(float(out.total_loss.item()), 5),
                            "loss_dict": loss_dict,
                            "batch_acc": batch_acc,
                            "node_stats": node_stats,
                            "tap_stats": tap_stats,
                            "probes": probes,
                            "step_delay_ms": self.step_delay_ms,
                        })

                    if self.step_delay_ms > 0:
                        # 分段休眠以便快速响应停/调速
                        rem = self.step_delay_ms / 1000.0
                        while rem > 0 and not self._stop_flag.is_set():
                            sl = min(0.05, rem)
                            time.sleep(sl)
                            rem -= sl
                            # 动态读取最新 step_delay_ms
                            if self.step_delay_ms == 0:
                                break

                if self._stop_flag.is_set():
                    break

                # 每个 epoch 结束做一次快速验证集评估
                if val_loader is not None:
                    val_loss, val_acc = self._eval_val(compiled, val_loader, source_id, device)
                    self._emit_cb({
                        "type": "eval",
                        "step": step,
                        "epoch": epoch + 1,
                        "val_loss": round(val_loss, 5),
                        "val_acc": round(val_acc, 4) if val_acc is not None else None,
                    })

            self.running = False
            self._emit_cb({
                "type": "train_finished",
                "stopped": self._stop_flag.is_set(),
                "steps": step,
            })
        except Exception as exc:
            self.running = False
            self._emit_cb({"type": "train_error", "message": str(exc)})

    @torch.no_grad()
    def _eval_val(
        self,
        compiled: CompiledGraph,
        val_loader,
        source_id: str,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> tuple[float, Optional[float]]:
        compiled.eval()
        total_loss = 0.0
        n_batches = 0
        correct = 0
        total_samples = 0
        for raw_batch in val_loader:
            if self._stop_flag.is_set():
                break
            batch = _to_signal_batch(source_id, raw_batch, device)
            out = compiled(batch, mode="eval", compute_losses=True)
            if out.total_loss is not None:
                total_loss += float(out.total_loss.item())
                n_batches += 1
            acc_pair = _extract_acc(compiled, out.outputs, batch, source_id)
            if acc_pair is not None:
                correct += acc_pair[0]
                total_samples += acc_pair[1]
        compiled.train()
        avg_loss = total_loss / max(1, n_batches)
        acc = (correct / total_samples) if total_samples > 0 else None
        return avg_loss, acc

netpulse/webui/server.py
================
"""外挂 WebUI 服务端（基于 aiohttp + 原生 WebSocket）。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import ipaddress
import json
import logging
import urllib.parse
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

import yaml
from aiohttp import web
from pydantic import ValidationError

from netpulse.core.erc import run_erc
from netpulse.core.ir import GraphIR
from netpulse.core.registry import ComponentRegistry, get_default_registry
from netpulse.webui.runner import LiveSessionConfig, LiveTrainRunner

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_RUNNER_KEY = web.AppKey("runner", LiveTrainRunner)
_BROADCASTER_KEY = web.AppKey("broadcaster_task", asyncio.Task)


def _is_loopback(host: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    if not host:
        return False
    if host == "localhost":
        return True
    try:
        return ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback
    except ValueError:
        return False


def _create_origin_only_middleware():
    """借鉴 ComfyUI：本地环回服务防跨站 Origin 伪造请求。"""

    @web.middleware
    async def origin_only_middleware(request: web.Request, handler):
        if request.headers.get("Sec-Fetch-Site") == "cross-site":
            return web.json_response({"error": "Cross-site request blocked"}, status=403)

        if "Host" in request.headers and "Origin" in request.headers:
            host_raw = request.headers["Host"].lower()
            origin_parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(request.headers["Origin"])
            host_parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit("//" + host_raw)
            host_name = host_parsed.hostname
            origin_host = (origin_parsed.hostname or "").lower()

            if _is_loopback(host_name):
                host_cmp = host_raw if origin_parsed.port is not None else host_name
                origin_cmp = (origin_parsed.netloc or "").lower() if host_parsed.port is not None else origin_host
                if host_cmp and origin_cmp and host_cmp != origin_cmp:
                    logger.warning("Blocked mismatched Host/Origin: %s != %s", host_cmp, origin_cmp)
                    return web.json_response({"error": "Origin mismatch"}, status=403)

        if request.method == "OPTIONS":
            return web.Response()
        return await handler(request)

    return origin_only_middleware


def _compute_auto_layout(graph: GraphIR, topo_order: list[str], force_auto: bool = False) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    """根据拓扑层级计算清晰的左->右 DAG 节点坐标。"""
    node_map = {n.id: n for n in graph.nodes}
    order = topo_order if len(topo_order) == len(graph.nodes) else [n.id for n in graph.nodes]

    parents: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for e in graph.edges:
        parents[e.dst_node].append(e.src_node)

    layer_of: dict[str, int] = {}
    for nid in order:
        ps = parents.get(nid, [])
        layer_of[nid] = (max(layer_of.get(p, 0) for p in ps) + 1) if ps else 0

    by_layer: dict[int, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for nid in order:
        by_layer[layer_of[nid]].append(nid)

    positions: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    col_gap = 270.0
    row_gap = 155.0
    for layer_idx, nids in sorted(by_layer.items()):
        total_h = (len(nids) - 1) * row_gap
        start_y = 80.0 + max(0.0, (420.0 - total_h) / 2.0)
        for row_idx, nid in enumerate(nids):
            node = node_map[nid]
            if not force_auto and node.position and "x" in node.position and "y" in node.position:
                positions[nid] = {"x": float(node.position["x"]), "y": float(node.position["y"])}
            else:
                positions[nid] = {
                    "x": 60.0 + layer_idx * col_gap,
                    "y": start_y + row_idx * row_gap,
                }
    return positions


def serialize_graph_ir(
    graph: GraphIR,
    reg: ComponentRegistry,
    file_name: str = "unsaved.json",
    force_auto_layout: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """对任意 GraphIR 运行 ERC 并序列化为 WebUI 载荷。"""
    erc = run_erc(graph, reg)
    positions = _compute_auto_layout(graph, erc.topo_order, force_auto=force_auto_layout)

    nodes_payload = []
    for node in graph.nodes:
        cls = reg.get(node.type) if reg.has(node.type) else None
        sig = erc.signatures.get(node.id)
        if sig is None and cls is not None:
            try:
                sig = cls.declare_ports(node.params)
            except Exception:
                sig = None

        in_specs = {
            k: {"shape": list(v.shape), "layout": v.layout}
            for k, v in erc.node_in_specs.get(node.id, {}).items()
        }
        out_specs = {
            k: {"shape": list(v.shape), "layout": v.layout}
            for k, v in erc.node_out_specs.get(node.id, {}).items()
        }
        tap_specs = {
            k: {"shape": list(v.shape), "layout": v.layout}
            for k, v in erc.node_tap_specs.get(node.id, {}).items()
        }
        nodes_payload.append({
            "id": node.id,
            "type": node.type,
            "display_name": (cls.display_name or node.type) if cls else node.type,
            "title": ((cls.title or cls.display_name) if cls else node.type),
            "label": node.label,
            "category": cls.category if cls else "unknown",
            "params": node.params,
            "position": positions[node.id],
            "inputs": [p.name for p in sig.inputs] if sig else [],
            "outputs": [p.name for p in sig.outputs] if sig else [],
            "taps": [t.name for t in sig.taps] if sig else [],
            "in_specs": in_specs,
            "out_specs": out_specs,
            "tap_specs": tap_specs,
        })

    edges_payload = []
    for edge in graph.edges:
        plan = erc.auto_bridges.get(edge.id)
        shape_info: Optional[list[int]] = None
        if edge.edge_kind == "tap":
            spec = erc.node_tap_specs.get(edge.src_node, {}).get(edge.src_port)
            if spec:
                shape_info = list(spec.shape)
        else:
            spec = erc.node_out_specs.get(edge.src_node, {}).get(edge.src_port)
            if spec:
                shape_info = list(spec.shape)
        edges_payload.append({
            "id": edge.id,
            "src_node": edge.src_node,
            "src_port": edge.src_port,
            "dst_node": edge.dst_node,
            "dst_port": edge.dst_port,
            "edge_kind": edge.edge_kind,
            "bridge": edge.bridge,
            "shape": shape_info,
            "autobridge": (
                {
                    "from": list(plan.src_spec.shape),
                    "to": list(plan.dst_spec.shape),
                }
                if plan
                else None
            ),
        })

    return {
        "name": file_name,
        "graph_name": graph.name,
        "nodes": nodes_payload,
        "edges": edges_payload,
        "losses": [lc.model_dump() for lc in graph.losses],
        "trainer": graph.trainer.model_dump(),
        "erc": {
            "passed": erc.passed,
            "topo_order": erc.topo_order,
            "diagnostics": [
                {
                    "level": d.level,
                    "code": d.code,
                    "message": d.message,
                    "node_id": d.node_id,
                    "edge_id": d.edge_id,
                }
                for d in erc.diagnostics
            ],
        },
    }


def _serialize_graph_payload(graph_path: Path, plugins_dir: Optional[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    reg = get_default_registry(plugins_dir)
    graph = GraphIR.from_file(graph_path)
    return serialize_graph_ir(graph, reg, file_name=graph_path.name)


def create_app(
    graphs_dir: str | Path = "examples",
    plugins_dir: Optional[str] = "plugins",
    templates_dir: str | Path = "templates",
) -> web.Application:
    graphs_root = Path(graphs_dir)
    templates_root = Path(templates_dir)
    html_path = Path(__file__).parent / "index.html"
    clients: set[web.WebSocketResponse] = set()
    event_loop_holder: dict[str, Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop]] = {"loop": None}
    msg_queue: asyncio.Queue[dict[str, Any]] = asyncio.Queue()

    def _emit_from_worker(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        loop = event_loop_holder["loop"]
        if loop is not None and not loop.is_closed():
            loop.call_soon_threadsafe(msg_queue.put_nowait, payload)

    runner = LiveTrainRunner(_emit_from_worker)

    async def _broadcaster(_app: web.Application) -> None:
        try:
            while True:
                payload = await msg_queue.get()
                text = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
                dead: list[web.WebSocketResponse] = []
                for ws in list(clients):
                    try:
                        await ws.send_str(text)
                    except Exception:
                        dead.append(ws)
                for ws in dead:
                    clients.discard(ws)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            return

    async def on_startup(app: web.Application) -> None:
        event_loop_holder["loop"] = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        app[_BROADCASTER_KEY] = asyncio.create_task(_broadcaster(app))

    async def on_cleanup(app: web.Application) -> None:
        runner.stop()
        task = app.get(_BROADCASTER_KEY)
        if task:
            task.cancel()
            try:
                await task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        for ws in list(clients):
            await ws.close()
        clients.clear()

    def _list_dir_graphs(root: Path) -> list[Path]:
        if not root.exists():
            return []
        files = list(root.glob("*.json")) + list(root.glob("*.yaml")) + list(root.glob("*.yml"))
        return sorted(files, key=lambda p: p.name)

    def _list_graph_files() -> list[Path]:
        return _list_dir_graphs(graphs_root)

    def _list_template_files() -> list[Path]:
        return _list_dir_graphs(templates_root)

    def _resolve_safe_save_path(raw_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        name = Path(raw_name).name
        if not name or name != raw_name or ".." in raw_name or "/" in raw_name or "\\" in raw_name:
            return None
        if Path(name).suffix.lower() not in {".json", ".yaml", ".yml"}:
            name = f"{name}.json"
        graphs_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        root_resolved = graphs_root.resolve()
        candidate = (graphs_root / name).resolve()
        if candidate.parent != root_resolved:
            return None
        return candidate

    def _resolve_safe_existing_path(root: Path, raw_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        name = Path(raw_name).name
        if not name or name != raw_name or ".." in raw_name or "/" in raw_name or "\\" in raw_name:
            return None
        if not root.exists():
            return None
        root_resolved = root.resolve()
        candidate = (root / name).resolve()
        if candidate.parent != root_resolved or not candidate.is_file():
            return None
        return candidate

    async def index(_request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        return web.Response(text=html_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), content_type="text/html")

    async def api_components(_request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        reg = get_default_registry(plugins_dir)
        return web.json_response({
            "components": reg.describe_all(),
            "plugin_errors": reg.plugin_errors,
        })

    async def api_graphs(_request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        return web.json_response({
            "graphs": [p.name for p in _list_graph_files()],
            "status": {
                "running": runner.running,
                "paused": runner.paused,
                "step_delay_ms": runner.step_delay_ms,
                "graph_name": runner.graph_name,
            },
        })

    async def api_graph(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        name = request.query.get("name")
        files = {p.name: p for p in _list_graph_files()}
        if not files:
            return web.json_response({"error": "未找到任何计算图文件"}, status=404)
        target = files.get(name) if name else next(iter(files.values()))
        if target is None:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"找不到图文件: {name}"}, status=404)
        return web.json_response(_serialize_graph_payload(target, plugins_dir))

    async def api_erc_check(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        try:
            body = await request.json()
        except Exception:
            return web.json_response({"error": "无效的 JSON 请求体"}, status=400)

        raw_graph = body.get("graph", body) if isinstance(body, dict) else body
        file_name = str(body.get("name", "draft.json")) if isinstance(body, dict) else "draft.json"
        force_auto = bool(body.get("auto_layout", False)) if isinstance(body, dict) else False
        try:
            graph = GraphIR.model_validate(raw_graph)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"GraphIR 校验失败: {exc}"}, status=400)

        reg = get_default_registry(plugins_dir)
        return web.json_response(serialize_graph_ir(graph, reg, file_name=file_name, force_auto_layout=force_auto))

    async def api_graph_save(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        try:
            body = await request.json()
        except Exception:
            return web.json_response({"error": "无效的 JSON 请求体"}, status=400)

        raw_name = str(body.get("name", "")).strip()
        target = _resolve_safe_save_path(raw_name)
        if target is None:
            return web.json_response({"error": "非法文件名（仅支持当前目录 .json/.yaml/.yml）"}, status=400)

        try:
            graph = GraphIR.model_validate(body.get("graph"))
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"GraphIR 校验失败: {exc}"}, status=400)

        graph.to_file(target)
        reg = get_default_registry(plugins_dir)
        payload = serialize_graph_ir(graph, reg, file_name=target.name)
        return web.json_response({"ok": True, "name": target.name, "graph": payload})

    async def api_graph_delete(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        try:
            body = await request.json()
        except Exception:
            return web.json_response({"error": "无效的 JSON 请求体"}, status=400)

        raw_name = str(body.get("name", "")).strip()
        if not raw_name or raw_name != Path(raw_name).name or ".." in raw_name or "/" in raw_name or "\\" in raw_name:
            return web.json_response({"error": "非法文件名"}, status=400)
        target = _resolve_safe_existing_path(graphs_root, raw_name)
        if target is None:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"找不到图文件: {raw_name}"}, status=404)
        target.unlink()
        return web.json_response({"ok": True, "deleted": raw_name})

    async def api_graph_import(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        try:
            body = await request.json()
        except Exception:
            return web.json_response({"error": "无效的 JSON 请求体"}, status=400)

        raw_name = str(body.get("name", "imported_graph.json")).strip() or "imported_graph.json"
        safe_name = Path(raw_name).name
        if Path(safe_name).suffix.lower() not in {".json", ".yaml", ".yml"}:
            safe_name = f"{safe_name}.json"

        content = body.get("content")
        raw_graph: Any = None
        if isinstance(content, dict):
            raw_graph = content
        elif isinstance(content, str):
            try:
                if safe_name.lower().endswith((".yaml", ".yml")):
                    raw_graph = yaml.safe_load(content)
                else:
                    try:
                        raw_graph = json.loads(content)
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        raw_graph = yaml.safe_load(content)
            except Exception as exc:
                return web.json_response({"error": f"解析工作流文件失败: {exc}"}, status=400)
        else:
            return web.json_response({"error": "缺少工作流文件内容"}, status=400)

        try:
            graph = GraphIR.model_validate(raw_graph)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"GraphIR 校验失败: {exc}"}, status=400)

        saved_name: Optional[str] = None
        if bool(body.get("save", False)):
            save_target = _resolve_safe_save_path(safe_name)
            if save_target is None:
                return web.json_response({"error": "非法文件名"}, status=400)
            graph.to_file(save_target)
            saved_name = save_target.name

        reg = get_default_registry(plugins_dir)
        payload = serialize_graph_ir(graph, reg, file_name=saved_name or safe_name)
        return web.json_response({"ok": True, "name": saved_name or safe_name, "saved": saved_name is not None, "graph": payload})

    async def api_graph_export(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        try:
            body = await request.json()
        except Exception:
            return web.json_response({"error": "无效的 JSON 请求体"}, status=400)

        fmt = str(body.get("format", "json")).lower()
        if fmt not in {"json", "yaml", "yml"}:
            return web.json_response({"error": "导出格式仅支持 json 或 yaml"}, status=400)

        try:
            graph = GraphIR.model_validate(body.get("graph"))
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"GraphIR 校验失败: {exc}"}, status=400)

        base_name = Path(str(body.get("name") or graph.name or "workflow")).stem or "workflow"
        ext = "yaml" if fmt in {"yaml", "yml"} else "json"
        if ext == "yaml":
            text = yaml.safe_dump(graph.model_dump(), allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False)
            mime = "application/x-yaml"
        else:
            text = graph.to_json(indent=2) + "\n"
            mime = "application/json"
        return web.json_response({
            "ok": True,
            "filename": f"{base_name}.{ext}",
            "content": text,
            "mime": mime,
        })

    async def api_templates(_request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        items = []
        for p in _list_template_files():
            try:
                g = GraphIR.from_file(p)
                items.append({
                    "file": p.name,
                    "name": g.name,
                    "node_count": len(g.nodes),
                    "edge_count": len(g.edges),
                    "node_types": sorted({n.type for n in g.nodes}),
                })
            except Exception:
                continue
        return web.json_response({"templates": items})

    async def api_template(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        raw_name = str(request.query.get("name", "")).strip()
        target = _resolve_safe_existing_path(templates_root, raw_name)
        if target is None:
            return web.json_response({"error": f"找不到模板文件: {raw_name}"}, status=404)
        payload = _serialize_graph_payload(target, plugins_dir)
        payload["is_template"] = True
        return web.json_response(payload)

    async def api_train_start(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        body = await request.json()
        reg = get_default_registry(plugins_dir)

        if "graph" in body and body["graph"] is not None:
            try:
                graph_ir = GraphIR.model_validate(body["graph"])
            except ValidationError as exc:
                return web.json_response({"error": f"GraphIR 校验失败: {exc}"}, status=400)
            session_name = str(body.get("graph_name") or graph_ir.name or "custom_graph.json")
        else:
            name = body.get("graph_name")
            files = {p.name: p for p in _list_graph_files()}
            target = files.get(name) if name else (next(iter(files.values())) if files else None)
            if target is None:
                return web.json_response({"error": f"找不到图文件: {name}"}, status=404)
            graph_ir = GraphIR.from_file(target)
            session_name = target.name

        cfg = LiveSessionConfig(
            step_delay_ms=int(body.get("step_delay_ms", 150)),
            epochs=int(body["epochs"]) if body.get("epochs") else None,
            lr=float(body["lr"]) if body.get("lr") else None,
        )
        runner.start(session_name, graph_ir, reg, cfg)
        return web.json_response({"ok": True, "graph_name": session_name, "step_delay_ms": cfg.step_delay_ms})

    async def api_train_speed(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        body = await request.json()
        delay = int(body.get("step_delay_ms", runner.step_delay_ms))
        paused = body.get("paused")
        state = runner.set_speed(delay, paused)
        return web.json_response(state)

    async def api_train_stop(_request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        runner.stop()
        return web.json_response({"ok": True, "running": False})

    async def ws_endpoint(request: web.Request) -> web.WebSocketResponse:
        ws = web.WebSocketResponse()
        await ws.prepare(request)
        clients.add(ws)
        try:
            await ws.send_str(json.dumps({
                "type": "hello",
                "running": runner.running,
                "paused": runner.paused,
                "step_delay_ms": runner.step_delay_ms,
                "graph_name": runner.graph_name,
            }))
            async for _msg in ws:
                pass
        finally:
            clients.discard(ws)
        return ws

    app = web.Application(middlewares=[_create_origin_only_middleware()])
    app.add_routes([
        web.get("/", index),
        web.get("/api/components", api_components),
        web.get("/api/graphs", api_graphs),
        web.get("/api/graph", api_graph),
        web.post("/api/erc/check", api_erc_check),
        web.post("/api/graph/save", api_graph_save),
        web.post("/api/graph/delete", api_graph_delete),
        web.post("/api/graph/import", api_graph_import),
        web.post("/api/graph/export", api_graph_export),
        web.get("/api/templates", api_templates),
        web.get("/api/template", api_template),
        web.post("/api/train/start", api_train_start),
        web.post("/api/train/speed", api_train_speed),
        web.post("/api/train/stop", api_train_stop),
        web.get("/ws", ws_endpoint),
    ])
    app.on_startup.append(on_startup)
    app.on_cleanup.append(on_cleanup)
    app[_RUNNER_KEY] = runner
    return app


def run_server(
    host: str = "127.0.0.1",
    port: int = 8765,
    graphs_dir: str | Path = "examples",
    plugins_dir: Optional[str] = "plugins",
    templates_dir: str | Path = "templates",
) -> None:
    app = create_app(graphs_dir=graphs_dir, plugins_dir=plugins_dir, templates_dir=templates_dir)
    web.run_app(app, host=host, port=port)

================
README.md
================
# NetPulse

**NetPulse** 是一个面向深度学习架构实验、数学运算组合与可精确计算正则化目标的**节点流计算图沙盒**（ComfyUI 的拓扑工作流 + EDA/嘉立创式的元件封装与电气规则检查）。

将组件库中的模型模块、原子神经网络层、数学算子与损失/正则化目标以 DAG 连接为 `GraphIR`（JSON/YAML），经静态检查（ERC）与 `meta` 设备维度推导后，编译为原生 `torch.nn.Module` 运行训练与推理。

## 核心特性

- **原生 PyTorch 编译**：`GraphCompiler.compile()` 直接生成标准 `CompiledGraph(nn.Module)`，无缝复用 PyTorch `autograd`、优化器与 `state_dict` 导出。
- **EDA 式电气规则检查（ERC）与 AutoBridge**：
  - 自动执行环检测、端口契约校验与 `torch.device("meta")` 零显存形状推导。
  - 维度不匹配时报错提示插入转换元件（如 `Flatten`），或在 `bridge="auto"` 时自动插入可学习的 `MatrixBridge`（`Linear` / `1x1 Conv2d`）。
- **隐层抽头（Tap）与多目标正则化**：
  - 通过约定返回（`forward_with_taps`）或 PyTorch `register_forward_hook` 无侵入抽取任意隐藏层输出至 `tap_bus`。
  - Loss 与正则项节点（如 `cross_entropy_loss`、`activation_reg_loss` 的 `l1`/`l2`/`orthogonal` 模式）与网络层平级连线，统一加权反传。
- **双粒度元件库（30 个内置元件）与统一初始化**：
  - **数据源 (`source`，4)**：`spiral_data`、`mnist_data`（纯 PyTorch 加载与缓存，无需 `torchvision`）、`seq_wave_data`（波形/记忆线索序列，变长 `mask`）、`seq_forecast_data`（滑窗时序回归，`sine_mix` / `damped_osc` / `ar_trend`）。
  - **主干与原子层 (`backbone`，5 / `op`，10)**：`conv2d`、`pool2d`、`linear`、`activation`、`batch_norm2d`、`layer_norm`、`dropout`（支持 `kaiming` / `xavier` / `orthogonal` / `normal` / `constant` 等权重与偏置初始化）、`cnn_block`、`mlp_block`、`lstm`（步进 `LSTMCell`，Mask 感知）。
  - **算子与桥接 (`op` / `bridge`)**：`flatten`、`global_avg_pool`、`image_to_seq`、`seq_pool`、`tensor_add`、`matrix_bridge`。
  - **目标 (`loss`，3)**：`cross_entropy_loss`、`activation_reg_loss`（`l1` / `l2` / `orthogonal`）、`mse_loss`。
  - **可视化探针 (`output`，7)**：`monitor`、`scalar_watch`、`heatmap_preview`、`tensor_peek`、`gate_dynamics`、`hidden_trajectory`、`step_energy`（零梯度旁路，训练中实时渲染）。
  - 支持在 `plugins/*.py` 或 `plugins/*/__init__.py` 中通过 `@register_component` 热加载自定义元件（支持单插件错误隔离）。
- **元件自带中文元数据（Schema 内声明，UI 零映射表）**：
  - `BaseComponent.title` 声明中文译名、`description` 声明中文简介；`PortSpec.title` / `PortSpec.description` 声明每个输入/输出/Tap 端口的**中文含义**与**简单介绍**。
  - `registry.describe_component()` 将上述元数据随 `/api/components` 一起下发（插件元件同样适用），WebUI 直接消费，无需前端硬编码名称表。
  - 节点可通过 `NodeIR.label` 自定义显示名，随 `GraphIR` 保存与往返。
- **外挂交互式 WebUI**：基于 `aiohttp` + WebSocket 的零构建单页节点编辑 IDE，支持元件库拖放/双击画布快速建点、端口拖拽连线与实时 ERC/AutoBridge 校验、动态参数表单编辑、撤销重做、保存/另存为、工程文件导入导出、模板库、Minimap 小地图、动态调节步进延迟观察张量流动脉冲与实时分项 Loss 曲线。
- **中文优先的元件说明与可视排版**：
  - 元件库按「中文名 + 英文名 + 两句简介」呈现，支持中文关键词搜索。
  - 画布节点卡显示中文标题（或自定义标签）、元件简介与**中文端口标签**，悬停提示英文端口名、布局与端口说明。
  - 属性面板含元件信息卡（中文名/英文名/类别/简介）与端口说明表（中文含义 + 英文端口名 + 布局 + 实时 `shape`）。

## 目录结构

```text
netpulse/
├── core/           # Signal / PortSpec(title, description) / ParamSpec / GraphIR / Registry / ERC / GraphCompiler / Trainer
├── components/     # 内置 30 个元件（含参数 Schema、端口中文元数据）与统一初始化工具
├── webui/          # 外挂可视化与交互编辑服务端 (aiohttp + WS) 与单文件前端 index.html
└── cli.py          # 命令行入口 (list / check / train / serve)
templates/          # 内置经典工作流模板 (01_spiral_mlp … 05_lstm_time_series_forecast)
examples/           # 示例计算图 (spiral_quickstart / mnist_atomic / mnist_tap_reg / lstm_forecast_classic …)
tests/              # 单元测试与端到端训练 / WebUI 测试
docs/               # 架构设计文档 (design.md) 与系统交接文档 (ARCHITECTURE.md)
```

## 部署与运行

### 环境要求

- `Python >= 3.10`
- 核心依赖：`torch`、`pydantic`、`pyyaml`、`aiohttp`
- 测试依赖（可选）：`pytest`

### 1. 安装依赖

建议在独立虚拟环境中安装依赖（请根据本机硬件选择对应的 PyTorch CPU 或 CUDA 版本）：

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Eganchiyu/NetPulse
cd netpulse

python -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate   # Windows: .venv\Scripts\activate

pip install torch pydantic pyyaml aiohttp
# 如需运行单元测试，可额外安装 pytest：
pip install pytest
```

### 2. 启动交互式 WebUI（推荐）

在项目根目录运行：

```bash
python -m netpulse.cli serve --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8765
```

启动后在浏览器打开：**`http://127.0.0.1:8765`**

常用参数：
- `--host`：监听地址（默认 `127.0.0.1`）
- `--port`：监听端口（默认 `8765`）
- `--graphs-dir`：计算图目录，保存与加载均限定在该目录下（默认 `examples`）
- `--templates-dir`：工作流模板目录（默认 `templates`）
- `--plugins`：自定义插件目录（默认 `plugins`）

WebUI 常用操作：
- **添加节点**：从左侧元件库点击/拖入画布，或**双击画布空白处**打开快速搜索框回车创建。
- **拖拽连线**：从右侧 `output` 或金色 `tap` 端口圆点按住拖至目标节点左侧 `input` 端口释放，连线时自动触发 ERC 静态校验与 AutoBridge 推导。
- **画布内直接编辑元件**：节点标题右侧 **✎** 可重命名显示标签（不影响节点 id 与连线）；**⚙** 展开节点内联「快速参数编辑」面板，改值即触发 ERC 重算，无需切换到属性面板。
- **编辑参数与连接**：点击节点/连线在右侧 **属性编辑** 面板修改参数、权重或 `AutoBridge` 策略；每个参数项可 **↺ 恢复默认值**，数值越界或 JSON 格式错误会就地标红提示；同时可查看该元件的**中文简介**与**每个端口的中文含义与说明**（含布局与实时 `shape`）。支持 `Delete/Backspace` 删除选中项、`Ctrl+Z`/`Ctrl+Y` 撤销重做、`Ctrl+S` 保存。
- **模板与文件**：顶部 **新建 / 模板 / 导入 / 导出 / 另存为 / 删除** 管理工程文件；模板库内置 `01_spiral_mlp` 至 `05_lstm_time_series_forecast` 五个经典工作流。
- **直接训练未保存图**：编辑后无需先落盘，点击 **开始训练** 即可直接提交当前画布图运行，并在 **训练遥测 & ERC** 面板实时调速观察 Loss 曲线与张量脉冲。

### 3. CLI 检查与训练命令

```bash
# 列出已注册元件
python -m netpulse.cli list

# 执行 ERC 静态检查与 Meta 形状推导
python -m netpulse.cli check examples/mnist_atomic.json

# 编译并运行训练闭环（支持导出 .pt 权重）
python -m netpulse.cli train examples/mnist_atomic.json --save .cache/mnist_atomic.pt
```

### 4. 运行测试

```bash
python -m pytest tests -v
```

## 开源协议

本项目采用 [GNU Affero General Public License v3.0 (AGPL-3.0)](LICENSE) 协议开源。

================
templates/01_spiral_mlp.json

{
  "name": "template_spiral_mlp",
  "nodes": [
    {
      "id": "src",
      "type": "spiral_data",
      "params": {
        "num_classes": 3,
        "points_per_class": 200,
        "noise": 0.12
      },
      "position": { "x": 60.0, "y": 240.0 }
    },
    {
      "id": "mlp",
      "type": "mlp_block",
      "params": {
        "dims": [32, 32, 3],
        "activation": "relu"
      },
      "position": { "x": 360.0, "y": 220.0 }
    },
    {

================
tests/test_atomic_layers.py
================
"""原子级神经网络层、统一初始化与增强 Block 单元测试。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import torch

from netpulse.core.compiler import GraphCompiler
from netpulse.core.erc import run_erc
from netpulse.core.ir import GraphIR
from netpulse.core.registry import get_default_registry
from netpulse.core.trainer import Trainer


def test_atomic_init_and_erc_channel_check() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    # 验证常量初始化、orthogonal 初始化与 conv2d/linear 显式输入维度校验
    graph_bad = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "src", "type": "mnist_data", "params": {}},
                {"id": "c1", "type": "conv2d", "params": {"in_channels": 3, "out_channels": 8, "kernel_size": 5}},
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "e1", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "c1", "dst_port": "x", "bridge": "none"},
            ],
        }
    )
    res_bad = run_erc(graph_bad, reg)
    assert not res_bad.passed
    assert any(d.code == "DIM_MISMATCH" for d in res_bad.errors())

    graph_ok = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "src", "type": "spiral_data", "params": {}},
                {
                    "id": "lin1",
                    "type": "linear",
                    "params": {
                        "in_features": 2,
                        "out_features": 4,
                        "init": {"type": "constant", "val": 0.25},
                        "bias_init": "zeros",
                    },
                },
                {"id": "ln", "type": "layer_norm", "params": {}},
                {"id": "act", "type": "activation", "params": {"kind": "gelu"}},
                {"id": "lin2", "type": "linear", "params": {"in_features": 8, "out_features": 3, "init": "orthogonal"}},
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "e1", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "lin1", "dst_port": "x"},
                {"id": "e2", "src_node": "lin1", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "ln", "dst_port": "x"},
                {"id": "e3", "src_node": "ln", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "act", "dst_port": "x"},
                {"id": "e4", "src_node": "act", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "lin2", "dst_port": "x", "bridge": "auto"},
            ],
        }
    )
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph_ok, reg)
    assert "e4" in compiled.bridges_mod
    w1 = compiled.nodes_mod["lin1"].op.weight
    b1 = compiled.nodes_mod["lin1"].op.bias
    assert torch.allclose(w1, torch.full_like(w1, 0.25))
    assert torch.allclose(b1, torch.zeros_like(b1))


def test_enhanced_cnn_block_per_layer_cfg() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "src", "type": "mnist_data", "params": {}},
                {
                    "id": "cnn",
                    "type": "cnn_block",
                    "params": {
                        "layers": [
                            {"out_channels": 8, "kernel_size": 5, "padding": 2, "pool": "max", "pool_size": 2, "activation": "gelu"},
                            {"out_channels": 16, "kernel_size": 3, "padding": 1, "pool": "avg", "pool_size": 2, "activation": "relu"},
                        ],
                        "init": "kaiming_normal",
                    },
                },
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "e1", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "cnn", "dst_port": "x"},
            ],
        }
    )
    res = run_erc(graph, reg)
    assert res.passed
    assert res.node_out_specs["cnn"]["out"].shape == (64, 16, 7, 7)


def test_mnist_atomic_e2e() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.from_file("examples/mnist_atomic.json")
    graph.trainer.epochs = 1
    graph.trainer.max_steps = 20
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    trainer = Trainer(compiled)
    res = trainer.fit(verbose=False)
    assert res.steps == 20
    assert res.final_val_acc is not None and res.final_val_acc >= 0.70

================
tests/test_e2e_training.py
================
"""端到端训练闭环与权重导出重载测试。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import torch

from netpulse.core.compiler import GraphCompiler
from netpulse.core.ir import GraphIR
from netpulse.core.registry import get_default_registry
from netpulse.core.trainer import Trainer
from netpulse.core.types import Signal


def test_spiral_quickstart_e2e(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.from_file("examples/spiral_quickstart.json")
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    trainer = Trainer(compiled)
    res = trainer.fit(verbose=False)

    assert res.steps == 150
    assert res.final_val_acc is not None and res.final_val_acc >= 0.95

    ckpt = trainer.save_checkpoint(tmp_path / "spiral.pt")
    assert ckpt.exists()
    loaded = torch.load(ckpt, map_location="cpu")
    rebuilt = GraphCompiler.compile(GraphIR.model_validate(loaded["graph_ir"]), reg)
    rebuilt.load_state_dict(loaded["state_dict"])

    sample = {
        "src": {
            "x": Signal(data=torch.randn(4, 2), meta={"layout": "BD"}),
            "y": Signal(data=torch.zeros(4, dtype=torch.long), meta={"layout": "B"}),
        }
    }
    out = rebuilt(sample, mode="infer")
    assert out.outputs["mlp"]["out"].shape == (4, 3)


def test_mnist_tap_reg_e2e(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.from_file("examples/mnist_tap_reg.json")
    graph.trainer.epochs = 1
    graph.trainer.max_steps = 20
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    trainer = Trainer(compiled)
    res = trainer.fit(verbose=False)

    assert res.steps == 20
    assert res.final_val_acc is not None and res.final_val_acc >= 0.65
    assert "loss/ce" in res.history[-1]
    assert "loss/ortho_reg" in res.history[-1]

"""ERC 静态检查、AutoBridge、Tap 与自定义插件加载测试。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import torch

from netpulse.core.compiler import GraphCompiler
from netpulse.core.erc import run_erc
from netpulse.core.ir import GraphIR
from netpulse.core.registry import ComponentRegistry, get_default_registry
from netpulse.core.types import Signal


def test_erc_cycle_detection() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "a", "type": "mlp_block", "params": {"in_dim": 4, "dims": [4]}},
                {"id": "b", "type": "mlp_block", "params": {"in_dim": 4, "dims": [4]}},
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "e1", "src_node": "a", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "b", "dst_port": "x"},
                {"id": "e2", "src_node": "b", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "a", "dst_port": "x"},
            ],
        }
    )
    res = run_erc(graph, reg)
    assert not res.passed
    assert any(d.code == "CYCLE_DETECTED" for d in res.errors())


def test_erc_rank_mismatch_suggests_flatten() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "src", "type": "mnist_data", "params": {}},
                {"id": "head", "type": "mlp_block", "params": {"dims": [10]}},
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "e1", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "head", "dst_port": "x"},
            ],
        }
    )
    res = run_erc(graph, reg)
    assert not res.passed
    errs = [d for d in res.errors() if d.code == "RANK_MISMATCH"]
    assert errs and "Flatten" in errs[0].message


def test_erc_autobridge_dim_mismatch_and_repair() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    base_dict = {
        "nodes": [
            {"id": "src", "type": "spiral_data", "params": {}},
            {"id": "m1", "type": "mlp_block", "params": {"dims": [16]}},
            {"id": "m2", "type": "mlp_block", "params": {"in_dim": 8, "dims": [3]}},
            {"id": "ce", "type": "cross_entropy_loss", "params": {}},
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"id": "e1", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "m1", "dst_port": "x"},
            {"id": "e2", "src_node": "m1", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "m2", "dst_port": "x", "bridge": "none"},
            {"id": "e3", "src_node": "m2", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "ce", "dst_port": "logits"},
            {"id": "e4", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "y", "dst_node": "ce", "dst_port": "target"},
        ],
    }

    # bridge="none" 时应报错 DIM_MISMATCH
    res_fail = run_erc(GraphIR.model_validate(base_dict), reg)
    assert not res_fail.passed
    assert any(d.code == "DIM_MISMATCH" for d in res_fail.errors())

    # bridge="auto" 时自动插入 MatrixBridge 并通过编译与前向反传
    base_dict["edges"][1]["bridge"] = "auto"
    graph_ok = GraphIR.model_validate(base_dict)
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph_ok, reg)
    assert "e2" in compiled.bridges_mod

    batch = {
        "src": {
            "x": Signal(data=torch.randn(8, 2), meta={"layout": "BD"}),
            "y": Signal(data=torch.randint(0, 3, (8,)), meta={"layout": "B"}),
        }
    }
    out = compiled(batch, mode="train")
    assert out.total_loss is not None
    out.total_loss.backward()
    assert compiled.bridges_mod["e2"].proj.weight.grad is not None

    # infer 模式应跳过 LossNode
    infer_out = compiled(batch, mode="infer")
    assert infer_out.total_loss is None
    assert "ce" not in infer_out.outputs


def test_tensor_add_autobridge_residual() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry(None)
    graph = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "src", "type": "spiral_data", "params": {}},
                {"id": "m1", "type": "mlp_block", "params": {"dims": [12]}},
                {"id": "add", "type": "tensor_add", "params": {}},
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "ea", "src_node": "m1", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "add", "dst_port": "a"},
                {"id": "eb", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "add", "dst_port": "b", "bridge": "auto"},
                {"id": "em", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "m1", "dst_port": "x"},
            ],
        }
    )
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    assert "eb" in compiled.bridges_mod
    batch = {
        "src": {
            "x": Signal(data=torch.randn(5, 2), meta={"layout": "BD"}),
            "y": Signal(data=torch.randint(0, 3, (5,)), meta={"layout": "B"}),
        }
    }
    out = compiled(batch, mode="infer")
    assert out.outputs["add"]["out"].shape == (5, 12)


def test_plugin_autoload(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    plugin_file = tmp_path / "custom_scale.py"
    plugin_file.write_text(
        '''
import torch.nn as nn
from netpulse.core.component import BaseComponent
from netpulse.core.registry import register_component
from netpulse.core.types import ComponentSignature, PortSpec

class _ScaleMod(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, factor: float):
        super().__init__()
        self.factor = factor
    def forward(self, inputs):
        s = inputs["x"]
        return {"out": s.clone_with(data=s.data * self.factor)}

@register_component("custom_scale_test")
class CustomScale(BaseComponent):
    category = "op"
    @classmethod
    def declare_ports(cls, params):
        return ComponentSignature(
            inputs=[PortSpec(name="x", rank=[2], layout="BD")],
            outputs=[PortSpec(name="out", rank=[2], layout="BD")],
        )
    def build(self, params, in_specs):
        return _ScaleMod(float(params.get("factor", 2.0)))
''',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    reg = get_default_registry(tmp_path)
    assert reg.has("custom_scale_test")
    graph = GraphIR.model_validate(
        {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "src", "type": "spiral_data", "params": {}},
                {"id": "sc", "type": "custom_scale_test", "params": {"factor": 3.0}},
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"id": "e1", "src_node": "src", "src_port": "x", "dst_node": "sc", "dst_port": "x"},
            ],
        }
    )
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    x = torch.ones(4, 2)
    out = compiled({"src": {"x": Signal(data=x, meta={"layout": "BD"}), "y": Signal(data=torch.zeros(4, dtype=torch.long), meta={"layout": "B"})}}, mode="infer")
    assert torch.allclose(out.outputs["sc"]["out"].data, torch.full((4, 2), 3.0))


def test_plugin_error_isolation(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    bad_plugin = tmp_path / "broken_plugin.py"
    bad_plugin.write_text("raise RuntimeError('boom')\n", encoding="utf-8")

    pkg_dir = tmp_path / "good_pkg"
    pkg_dir.mkdir()
    (pkg_dir / "__init__.py").write_text("VALUE = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")

    reg = ComponentRegistry()
    loaded = reg.load_plugins(tmp_path)
    assert str(pkg_dir / "__init__.py") in loaded
    assert str(bad_plugin) in reg.plugin_errors
    assert "RuntimeError" in reg.plugin_errors[str(bad_plugin)]

tests/test_lstm_and_probes.py
