"""LSTM、序列数据源/算子及专属可视化分析元件测试。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from aiohttp.test_utils import TestClient, TestServer

from netpulse.core.compiler import GraphCompiler
from netpulse.core.erc import run_erc
from netpulse.core.ir import EdgeIR, GraphIR, LossConfig, NodeIR, TrainerConfig
from netpulse.core.registry import get_default_registry
from netpulse.core.trainer import Trainer
from netpulse.core.types import Signal
from netpulse.webui.server import create_app


def test_lstm_and_sequence_components_registered_and_erc() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry()
    for name in (
        "seq_wave_data",
        "image_to_seq",
        "seq_pool",
        "lstm",
        "gate_dynamics",
        "hidden_trajectory",
        "step_energy",
    ):
        assert reg.has(name), f"{name} 未注册"

    graph = GraphIR.from_file("examples/lstm_seq_observatory.json")
    erc = run_erc(graph, reg)
    assert erc.passed, erc.format_report()
    assert erc.node_out_specs["lstm"]["out"].layout == "BLD"
    assert erc.node_out_specs["lstm"]["out"].has_mask is True
    assert erc.node_out_specs["lstm"]["last_h"].shape == (32, 32)
    assert set(erc.node_tap_specs["lstm"].keys()) >= {"gate_i", "gate_f", "gate_o", "cell_state", "hidden_states"}


def test_lstm_mask_awareness_and_e2e_training() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry()
    graph = GraphIR.from_file("examples/lstm_seq_observatory.json")
    graph.trainer.epochs = 4
    graph.trainer.lr = 0.01

    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    res = Trainer(compiled).fit()
    assert res.steps > 0
    assert res.final_val_acc is not None and res.final_val_acc >= 0.70

    # 验证 3 个专属探针均产生完整快照且不引入可训练参数
    assert sum(p.numel() for n, p in compiled.named_parameters() if "probe_" in n) == 0

    snap_gates = compiled.nodes_mod["probe_gates"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_gates is not None
    assert snap_gates["view"] == "gate_dynamics"
    gd = snap_gates["gate_dynamics"]
    assert set(gd["gates"].keys()) == {"i", "f", "o", "c"}
    assert gd["valid_len"] <= gd["total_len"]
    assert len(gd["strip_rows"]) > 0

    snap_traj = compiled.nodes_mod["probe_traj"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_traj is not None
    assert snap_traj["view"] == "trajectory"
    traj = snap_traj["trajectory"]
    assert len(traj["points"]) == traj["valid_len"]
    assert 0.0 <= traj["explained_var"] <= 1.01

    snap_energy = compiled.nodes_mod["probe_energy"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_energy is not None
    assert snap_energy["view"] == "step_energy"
    se = snap_energy["step_energy"]
    assert len(se["energy"]) == len(se["delta"]) == se["valid_len"]

    # 校验 LSTM 变长 Mask 冻结特性：padding 步不改变 last_h
    lstm_mod = compiled.nodes_mod["lstm"]
    lstm_mod.eval()
    x = torch.randn(2, 10, 4)
    x_mod = x.clone()
    x_mod[0, 5:] += 99.0  # 仅修改第一个样本的 padding 部分
    mask = torch.ones(2, 10, dtype=torch.bool)
    mask[0, 5:] = False
    out1 = lstm_mod({"x": Signal(data=x, mask=mask, meta={"layout": "BLD"})})
    out2 = lstm_mod({"x": Signal(data=x_mod, mask=mask, meta={"layout": "BLD"})})
    assert torch.allclose(out1["last_h"].data[0], out2["last_h"].data[0], atol=1e-5)


def test_image_to_seq_with_lstm_and_autobridge() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry()
    graph = GraphIR(
        name="img_lstm",
        nodes=[
            NodeIR(id="img_src", type="mnist_data", params={"subset_train": 64, "subset_val": 32}),
            NodeIR(id="i2s", type="image_to_seq", params={"mode": "rows"}),
            NodeIR(id="lstm", type="lstm", params={"hidden_size": 16, "in_dim": 16}),
            NodeIR(id="head", type="linear", params={"out_features": 10}),
            NodeIR(id="ce", type="cross_entropy_loss", params={}),
        ],
        edges=[
            EdgeIR(id="e1", src_node="img_src", src_port="x", dst_node="i2s", dst_port="x"),
            EdgeIR(id="e2", src_node="i2s", src_port="out", dst_node="lstm", dst_port="x", bridge="auto"),
            EdgeIR(id="e3", src_node="lstm", src_port="last_h", dst_node="head", dst_port="x"),
            EdgeIR(id="e4", src_node="head", src_port="out", dst_node="ce", dst_port="logits"),
            EdgeIR(id="e5", src_node="img_src", src_port="y", dst_node="ce", dst_port="target"),
        ],
        losses=[LossConfig(node_id="ce", weight=1.0)],
        trainer=TrainerConfig(epochs=1, batch_size=16),
    )
    erc = run_erc(graph, reg)
    assert erc.passed, erc.format_report()
    assert "e2" in erc.auto_bridges  # 28 -> 16 自动矩阵桥

    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    batch = {
        "img_src": {
            "x": Signal(data=torch.randn(4, 1, 28, 28), meta={"layout": "BCHW"}),
            "y": Signal(data=torch.randint(0, 10, (4,)), meta={"layout": "B"}),
        }
    }
    out = compiled(batch, mode="train")
    assert out.outputs["lstm"]["out"].shape == (4, 28, 16)
    assert out.total_loss is not None
    out.total_loss.backward()


def test_webui_ws_streams_lstm_observatory_probes() -> None:
    async def _run() -> None:
        app = create_app(graphs_dir="examples", plugins_dir=None)
        server = TestServer(app)
        client = TestClient(server)
        await client.start_server()
        try:
            r_graph = await client.get("/api/graph?name=lstm_seq_observatory.json")
            assert r_graph.status == 200
            g_data = await r_graph.json()
            assert g_data["erc"]["passed"] is True

            async with client.ws_connect("/ws") as ws:
                await ws.receive_str(timeout=3)  # hello
                await client.post(
                    "/api/train/start",
                    json={"graph_name": "lstm_seq_observatory.json", "step_delay_ms": 15, "epochs": 1},
                )
                saw = False
                for _ in range(30):
                    msg = json.loads(await ws.receive_str(timeout=5))
                    if msg["type"] == "step" and msg.get("probes"):
                        probes = msg["probes"]
                        assert probes["probe_gates"]["view"] == "gate_dynamics"
                        assert probes["probe_traj"]["view"] == "trajectory"
                        assert probes["probe_energy"]["view"] == "step_energy"
                        saw = True
                        break
                assert saw, "WebSocket 未收到 LSTM 专属探针快照"
                await client.post("/api/train/stop")
        finally:
            await client.close()

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_lstm_forecast_classic_and_templates_erc() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry()
    assert reg.has("seq_forecast_data")
    assert reg.has("mse_loss")

    # 校验全部模板均可静态通过 ERC
    tpl_files = sorted(Path("templates").glob("*.json"))
    assert len(tpl_files) >= 5
    for p in tpl_files:
        g = GraphIR.from_file(p)
        erc = run_erc(g, reg)
        assert erc.passed, f"{p} ERC 失败: {erc.format_report()}"

    # 校验经典 LSTM 时序预测工作流端到端收敛与探针快照
    graph = GraphIR.from_file("examples/lstm_forecast_classic.json")
    graph.trainer.epochs = 6
    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(graph, reg)
    res = Trainer(compiled).fit(verbose=False)
    assert res.steps > 0
    assert res.final_val_loss is not None and res.final_val_loss < 0.08
    assert res.final_val_acc is None

    assert compiled.nodes_mod["probe_gates"].capture_snapshot() is not None
    assert compiled.nodes_mod["probe_traj"].capture_snapshot() is not None
    assert compiled.nodes_mod["probe_energy"].capture_snapshot() is not None
    assert compiled.nodes_mod["watch_mse"].capture_snapshot() is not None
    assert compiled.nodes_mod["peek_pred"].capture_snapshot() is not None

    # 校验 MSELoss 对预测与目标维度不匹配能报错拦截
    bad = graph.model_copy(deep=True)
    for n in bad.nodes:
        if n.id == "head":
            n.params["dims"] = [16, 4]
    bad_erc = run_erc(bad, reg)
    assert not bad_erc.passed
    assert any(d.code == "DIM_MISMATCH" for d in bad_erc.diagnostics)

================
tests/test_outputs.py
================
"""输出监视工具节点（monitor / scalar_watch / heatmap_preview / tensor_peek）测试。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from aiohttp.test_utils import TestClient, TestServer

from netpulse.core.compiler import GraphCompiler
from netpulse.core.erc import run_erc
from netpulse.core.ir import EdgeIR, GraphIR, NodeIR
from netpulse.core.registry import get_default_registry
from netpulse.core.types import Signal
from netpulse.webui.server import create_app


def test_output_components_registered_and_erc_compatible() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry()
    for name in ("monitor", "scalar_watch", "heatmap_preview", "tensor_peek"):
        assert reg.has(name), f"{name} 未注册"
        cls = reg.get(name)
        assert cls.category == "output"
        assert len(cls.param_schema()) >= 3

    base = GraphIR.from_file("examples/spiral_quickstart.json")
    base.nodes.extend([
        NodeIR(id="watch_loss", type="scalar_watch", params={"reduce": "mean"}),
        NodeIR(id="heat_out", type="heatmap_preview", params={"max_size": 32, "colormap": "coolwarm"}),
        NodeIR(id="peek_in", type="tensor_peek", params={"max_rows": 4, "max_cols": 4}),
        NodeIR(id="mon_tap", type="monitor", params={"mode": "auto"}),
    ])
    base.edges.extend([
        EdgeIR(id="eo1", src_node="ce", src_port="loss", dst_node="watch_loss", dst_port="x"),
        EdgeIR(id="eo2", src_node="mlp", src_port="out", dst_node="heat_out", dst_port="x"),
        EdgeIR(id="eo3", src_node="src", src_port="x", dst_node="peek_in", dst_port="x"),
        EdgeIR(id="eo4", src_node="mlp", src_port="layer_0", dst_node="mon_tap", dst_port="x", edge_kind="tap"),
    ])

    erc = run_erc(base, reg)
    assert erc.passed, erc.format_report()

    compiled = GraphCompiler.compile(base, reg)
    # 输出节点不引入任何可训练参数
    assert sum(p.numel() for n, p in compiled.named_parameters() if "watch_loss" in n or "heat_out" in n) == 0

    batch = {
        "src": {
            "x": Signal(data=torch.randn(16, 2), meta={"layout": "BD"}),
            "y": Signal(data=torch.randint(0, 3, (16,)), meta={"layout": "B"}),
        }
    }
    out = compiled(batch, mode="train")
    assert out.total_loss is not None
    out.total_loss.backward()

    # infer 模式下 ce 被跳过，依赖 ce.loss 的 watch_loss 应安全跳过而不报错
    infer_out = compiled(batch, mode="infer", compute_losses=False)
    assert "heat_out" in infer_out.outputs
    assert "watch_loss" not in infer_out.outputs

    # 校验各类快照结构
    snap_loss = compiled.nodes_mod["watch_loss"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_loss is not None
    assert snap_loss["view"] == "scalar"
    assert isinstance(snap_loss["scalar"], float)

    snap_heat = compiled.nodes_mod["heat_out"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_heat is not None
    assert snap_heat["view"] == "heatmap"
    assert snap_heat["heatmap"]["rows"] == 16
    assert snap_heat["heatmap"]["cols"] == 3
    assert len(snap_heat["heatmap"]["data"]) == 48

    snap_peek = compiled.nodes_mod["peek_in"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_peek is not None
    assert snap_peek["view"] == "raw"
    assert snap_peek["raw"]["rows"] == 4
    assert snap_peek["raw"]["cols"] == 2
    assert len(snap_peek["hist"]["counts"]) == 16

    snap_tap = compiled.nodes_mod["mon_tap"].capture_snapshot()
    assert snap_tap is not None
    assert snap_tap["view"] == "heatmap"


def test_probe_image_downsample_and_nan_safety() -> None:
    reg = get_default_registry()
    peek = reg.get("tensor_peek")().build({"view": "auto"}, {})
    img_batch = torch.rand(8, 1, 28, 28)
    peek({"x": Signal(data=img_batch, meta={"layout": "BCHW"})})
    snap = peek.capture_snapshot()
    assert snap is not None
    assert snap["view"] == "image"
    assert snap["images"] is not None
    assert snap["images"]["count"] == 4
    assert snap["images"]["height"] == 28 and snap["images"]["width"] == 28

    # 超大矩阵与 NaN/Inf 安全降采样
    heat = reg.get("heatmap_preview")().build({"max_size": 16, "symmetric": True}, {})
    big = torch.randn(100, 80)
    big[0, 0] = float("nan")
    big[1, 1] = float("inf")
    heat({"x": Signal(data=big, meta={"layout": "BD"})})
    hsnap = heat.capture_snapshot()
    assert hsnap is not None
    assert hsnap["heatmap"]["rows"] == 16
    assert hsnap["heatmap"]["cols"] == 16
    assert hsnap["heatmap"]["vmin"] == -hsnap["heatmap"]["vmax"]


def test_webui_ws_streams_probe_snapshots() -> None:
    import asyncio

    async def _run() -> None:
        app = create_app(graphs_dir="examples", plugins_dir=None)
        server = TestServer(app)
        client = TestClient(server)
        await client.start_server()
        try:
            spiral = json.loads(Path("examples/spiral_quickstart.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            spiral["nodes"].extend([
                {"id": "watch_ce", "type": "scalar_watch", "params": {"reduce": "mean"}},
                {"id": "heat_mlp", "type": "heatmap_preview", "params": {"max_size": 32}},
            ])
            spiral["edges"].extend([
                {"id": "ep1", "src_node": "ce", "src_port": "loss", "dst_node": "watch_ce", "dst_port": "x"},
                {"id": "ep2", "src_node": "mlp", "src_port": "out", "dst_node": "heat_mlp", "dst_port": "x"},
            ])

            r_erc = await client.post("/api/erc/check", json={"graph": spiral})
            assert r_erc.status == 200
            erc_data = await r_erc.json()
            assert erc_data["erc"]["passed"] is True
            watch_node = next(n for n in erc_data["nodes"] if n["id"] == "watch_ce")
            assert watch_node["category"] == "output"
            assert "x" in watch_node["in_specs"]

            async with client.ws_connect("/ws") as ws:
                await ws.receive_str(timeout=3)  # hello
                await client.post(
                    "/api/train/start",
                    json={"graph_name": "probe_test.json", "graph": spiral, "step_delay_ms": 15, "epochs": 1},
                )
                saw_probe = False
                for _ in range(30):
                    msg = json.loads(await ws.receive_str(timeout=5))
                    if msg["type"] == "step" and msg.get("probes"):
                        assert "watch_ce" in msg["probes"]
                        assert "heat_mlp" in msg["probes"]
                        assert msg["probes"]["watch_ce"]["view"] == "scalar"
                        assert msg["probes"]["heat_mlp"]["view"] == "heatmap"
                        saw_probe = True
                        break
                assert saw_probe, "WebSocket step 消息中未包含探针快照"
                await client.post("/api/train/stop")
        finally:
            await client.close()

    asyncio.run(_run())

================
tests/test_webui.py
================
"""外挂 WebUI REST API 与 WebSocket 实时数据流测试。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from pathlib import Path

from aiohttp.test_utils import TestClient, TestServer

from netpulse.core.ir import GraphIR
from netpulse.webui.server import create_app


def test_webui_http_and_ws_flow() -> None:
    async def _run() -> None:
        app = create_app(graphs_dir="examples", plugins_dir=None)
        server = TestServer(app)
        client = TestClient(server)
        await client.start_server()
        try:
            # 1. HTML 页面、全量元件 Schema 与图列表
            r_home = await client.get("/")
            assert r_home.status == 200
            assert "NetPulse WebUI" in await r_home.text()

            r_comps = await client.get("/api/components")
            assert r_comps.status == 200
            comps = (await r_comps.json())["components"]
            assert len(comps) >= 17
            assert "conv2d" in comps
            assert any(p["name"] == "out_channels" for p in comps["conv2d"]["params"])

            r_list = await client.get("/api/graphs")
            assert r_list.status == 200
            graphs = (await r_list.json())["graphs"]
            assert "mnist_atomic.json" in graphs
            assert "spiral_quickstart.json" in graphs

            # 2. 获取图详情 + ERC 与自动排布坐标
            r_graph = await client.get("/api/graph", params={"name": "mnist_atomic.json"})
            assert r_graph.status == 200
            gdata = await r_graph.json()
            assert gdata["erc"]["passed"] is True
            assert len(gdata["nodes"]) >= 15
            assert all("position" in n for n in gdata["nodes"])

            # 3. 实时 ERC 校验接口（含成功与维度冲突诊断）
            spiral_raw = json.loads(Path("examples/spiral_quickstart.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            r_erc_ok = await client.post("/api/erc/check", json={"graph": spiral_raw})
            assert r_erc_ok.status == 200
            assert (await r_erc_ok.json())["erc"]["passed"] is True

            bad_raw = json.loads(json.dumps(spiral_raw))
            bad_raw["nodes"][1]["params"]["in_dim"] = 99
            bad_raw["edges"][0]["bridge"] = "none"
            r_erc_bad = await client.post("/api/erc/check", json={"graph": bad_raw})
            assert r_erc_bad.status == 200
            bad_res = await r_erc_bad.json()
            assert bad_res["erc"]["passed"] is False
            assert any(d["code"] == "DIM_MISMATCH" for d in bad_res["erc"]["diagnostics"])

            # 4. 连接 WebSocket 并使用内存修改后的 inline graph 启动极速训练 + 调速 + 停止
            async with client.ws_connect("/ws") as ws:
                hello = json.loads(await ws.receive_str(timeout=3))
                assert hello["type"] == "hello"

                r_start = await client.post(
                    "/api/train/start",
                    json={
                        "graph_name": "inline_spiral.json",
                        "graph": spiral_raw,
                        "step_delay_ms": 20,
                        "epochs": 2,
                    },
                )
                assert r_start.status == 200

                r_speed = await client.post(
                    "/api/train/speed",
                    json={"step_delay_ms": 10, "paused": False},
                )
                assert r_speed.status == 200
                assert (await r_speed.json())["step_delay_ms"] == 10

                saw_step = False
                for _ in range(30):
                    raw = await ws.receive_str(timeout=5)
                    msg = json.loads(raw)
                    if msg["type"] == "step":
                        saw_step = True
                        assert "total_loss" in msg
                        assert "mlp" in msg["node_stats"]
                        assert "ce" in msg["loss_dict"]
                        break
                assert saw_step, "未收到训练 step 实时推送"

                r_stop = await client.post("/api/train/stop")
                assert r_stop.status == 200
        finally:
            await client.close()

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_webui_save_graph_and_path_guard(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    async def _run() -> None:
        app = create_app(graphs_dir=tmp_path, plugins_dir=None)
        server = TestServer(app)
        client = TestClient(server)
        await client.start_server()
        try:
            spiral = GraphIR.from_file("examples/spiral_quickstart.json").model_dump()

            # 禁止路径穿越
            r_bad = await client.post("/api/graph/save", json={"name": "../evil.json", "graph": spiral})
            assert r_bad.status == 400

            # 正常保存到 graphs_dir
            r_ok = await client.post("/api/graph/save", json={"name": "saved_spiral.json", "graph": spiral})
            assert r_ok.status == 200
            body = await r_ok.json()
            assert body["ok"] is True
            assert (tmp_path / "saved_spiral.json").exists()

            # 导出为 YAML 与 JSON
            r_exp_yaml = await client.post(
                "/api/graph/export",
                json={"name": "saved_spiral.json", "format": "yaml", "graph": spiral},
            )
            assert r_exp_yaml.status == 200
            exp_yaml = await r_exp_yaml.json()
            assert exp_yaml["filename"] == "saved_spiral.yaml"
            assert "spiral_data" in exp_yaml["content"]

            # 导入 YAML 并可选择保存到 graphs_dir
            r_imp = await client.post(
                "/api/graph/import",
                json={"name": "imported_spiral.yaml", "content": exp_yaml["content"], "save": True},
            )
            assert r_imp.status == 200
            imp_body = await r_imp.json()
            assert imp_body["ok"] is True
            assert imp_body["saved"] is True
            assert imp_body["graph"]["erc"]["passed"] is True
            assert (tmp_path / "imported_spiral.yaml").exists()

            # 加载列表应包含两个工作流
            r_list2 = await client.get("/api/graphs")
            graphs2 = (await r_list2.json())["graphs"]
            assert "saved_spiral.json" in graphs2
            assert "imported_spiral.yaml" in graphs2

            # 删除工作流（含路径穿越防护）
            r_del_bad = await client.post("/api/graph/delete", json={"name": "../evil.json"})
            assert r_del_bad.status == 400
            r_del = await client.post("/api/graph/delete", json={"name": "imported_spiral.yaml"})
            assert r_del.status == 200
            assert not (tmp_path / "imported_spiral.yaml").exists()
        finally:
            await client.close()

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_webui_templates_api() -> None:
    async def _run() -> None:
        app = create_app(graphs_dir="examples", plugins_dir=None, templates_dir="templates")
        server = TestServer(app)
        client = TestClient(server)
        await client.start_server()
        try:
            r_tpls = await client.get("/api/templates")
            assert r_tpls.status == 200
            tpls = (await r_tpls.json())["templates"]
            files = [t["file"] for t in tpls]
            assert "01_spiral_mlp.json" in files
            assert "05_lstm_time_series_forecast.json" in files

            r_tpl = await client.get("/api/template", params={"name": "05_lstm_time_series_forecast.json"})
            assert r_tpl.status == 200
            data = await r_tpl.json()
            assert data["is_template"] is True
            assert data["erc"]["passed"] is True

            r_bad = await client.get("/api/template", params={"name": "../secret.json"})
            assert r_bad.status == 404
        finally:
            await client.close()

    asyncio.run(_run())
